  1175	                        const auto nx = (float) tickToX ((double) at);
  1176	                        const auto nw = juce::jmax (rounded ? 2.0f : 1.0f, (float) (n.length * pixelsPerTick) - 1.0f);
  1177	                        if (nx > visible.getRight() || nx + nw < visible.getX())
  1178	                            continue;
  1179	                        const auto ny = body.getBottom() - ((float) n.key - bottomKey + 0.5f) / rows * body.getHeight() - noteHeight * 0.5f;
  1180	                        if (rounded)
  1181	                            g.fillRoundedRectangle (nx, ny, nw, noteHeight, juce::jmin (1.0f, noteHeight * 0.5f));
  1182	                        else
  1183	                            g.fillRect (nx, ny, nw, noteHeight);
  1184	                    }
  1185	                }
  1186	            }
  1187	
  1188	            veilIfMuted (c, r);
  1189	            paintClipOutline (g, r, base, selected, current);
  1190	        }
  1191	
  1192	        {
  1193	            const uitrace::Part tally (uitrace::part::batch);
  1194	            smallClips.fill (g);
  1195	        }
  1196	
  1197	        if (song.performanceZoneEnd() > 0)
  1198	            paintPerformance (g, grid);
  1199	
  1200	        // the clip under the mouse: outline, the grip of the edge it is on, its menu button
  1201	        if (hover.clip >= 0 && hover.clip < (int) song.playlist.size() && drag == Drag::none)
  1202	        {
  1203	            const auto r = clipBounds (hover.clip).toFloat();
  1204	            g.setColour (colour::textPrimary.withAlpha (isSelected (hover.clip) ? 0.0f : 0.55f));
  1205	            g.drawRoundedRectangle (r.reduced (0.5f), radius::sm, 1.2f);
  1206	
  1207	            if (hover.target == gesture::Target::leftEdge || hover.target == gesture::Target::rightEdge)
  1208	            {
  1209	                const auto x = hover.target == gesture::Target::rightEdge ? r.getRight() - 4.0f : r.getX() + 1.0f;
  1210	                g.setColour (colour::textPrimary);
  1211	                g.fillRoundedRectangle (x, r.getY() + clipTitleHeight + 3.0f, 3.0f, juce::jmax (4.0f, r.getHeight() - clipTitleHeight - 6.0f), 1.5f);
  1212	            }
  1213	
  1214	            if (const auto button = clipMenuButton (hover.clip); ! button.isEmpty())
  1215	            {
  1216	                const auto b = button.toFloat();
  1217	                g.setColour (hover.target == gesture::Target::clipMenu ? colour::bg0.withAlpha (0.75f) : colour::bg0.withAlpha (0.45f));
  1218	                g.fillRoundedRectangle (b, 2.0f);
  1219	                icons::draw (g, Icon::chevronDown, b.reduced (1.0f), colour::textPrimary);
  1220	            }
  1221	        }
  1222	
  1223	        // FL 2026's gain handle (round 166e): the hovered audio clip's, and the one being dragged
  1224	        if (const auto gainClip = drag == Drag::clipGain ? dragClip : drag == Drag::none ? hover.clip : -1; gainClip >= 0 && gainClip < (int) song.playlist.size())
  1225	            if (const auto handle = gainHandle (gainClip); ! handle.isEmpty())
  1226	            {
  1227	                const auto active = drag == Drag::clipGain || hover.target == gesture::Target::gainHandle;
  1228	                juce::Path half;
  1229	                half.addPieSegment (handle.getX(), handle.getY(), handle.getWidth(), handle.getWidth(), -juce::MathConstants<float>::halfPi,
  1230	                                    juce::MathConstants<float>::halfPi, 0.0f);
  1231	                g.setColour (active ? colour::textPrimary : colour::textPrimary.withAlpha (0.7f));
  1232	                g.fillPath (half);
  1233	                g.setColour (colour::bg0.withAlpha (0.8f));
  1234	                g.strokePath (half, juce::PathStrokeType (1.0f));
  1235	            }
  1236	
  1237	        if (! rubberBand.isEmpty())
  1238	        {
  1239	            g.setColour (colour::selectionFill);
  1240	            g.fillRect (rubberBand);
  1241	            g.setColour (colour::selection);
  1242	            g.drawRect (rubberBand);
  1243	        }
  1244	
  1245	        if (drag == Drag::knife)
  1246	        {
  1247	            juce::Path cut;
  1248	            cut.startNewSubPath (knifeFrom.toFloat());
  1249	            cut.lineTo (knifeTo.toFloat());
  1250	            juce::Path dashed;
  1251	            const float dash[] = { 5.0f, 3.0f };
  1252	            juce::PathStrokeType (1.5f).createDashedStroke (dashed, cut, dash, 2);
  1253	            g.setColour (colour::textPrimary);
  1254	            g.fillPath (dashed);
  1255	        }
  1256	
  1257	        if (playheadTick >= 0.0 && ! cachePainting)
  1258	        {
  1259	            g.setColour (colour::playhead);
  1260	            g.fillRect (juce::Rectangle<float> ((float) tickToX (playheadTick) - 0.5f, (float) grid.getY(), 1.5f, (float) grid.getHeight()));
  1261	        }
  1262	    }
  1263	
  1264	    // --- ruler (drawn in its own coordinates, below the toolbar) -----------------
  1265	    {
  1266	        const uitrace::Scope traceRuler ("Playlist ruler", false);
  1267	        juce::Graphics::ScopedSaveState state (g);
  1268	        g.setOrigin (0, rulerTop);
  1269	        const auto ruler = juce::Rectangle<int> (headerWidth, 0, grid.getWidth(), timelineHeight);
  1270	        g.setColour (colour::bg2);
  1271	        g.fillRect (ruler);
  1272	        g.setColour (colour::border);
  1273	        g.fillRect (ruler.withTop (ruler.getBottom() - 1));
  1274	        g.reduceClipRegion (ruler);
  1275	        g.setFont (font (type::small));
  1276	
  1277	        if (song.hasLoop()) // the time selection
  1278	        {
  1279	            const auto lx0 = (float) tickToX ((double) song.loopStart), lx1 = (float) tickToX ((double) song.loopEnd);
  1280	            g.setColour (colour::accent.withAlpha (0.16f));
  1281	            g.fillRect (juce::Rectangle<float> (lx0, 0.0f, lx1 - lx0, (float) timelineHeight));
  1282	            g.setColour (colour::accent.withAlpha (0.75f));
  1283	            g.fillRect (juce::Rectangle<float> (lx0, 0.0f, lx1 - lx0, 4.0f));
  1284	        }
  1285	
  1286	        const auto barPixels = bar * pixelsPerTick;
  1287	        const auto every = barPixels < 30.0 ? (barPixels < 10.0 ? 16 : 4) : 1; // numbers spaced further apart when zoomed out
  1288	
  1289	        const auto rulerClip = g.getClipBounds(); // (the numbers reach 40 px right of their bar line)
  1290	        for (const auto& b : song.barsBetween (juce::jmax ((std::int64_t) std::max (0.0, scrollTicks), (std::int64_t) xToTick (rulerClip.getX() - 44) - bar),
  1291	                                               juce::jmin ((std::int64_t) xToTick (grid.getRight()), (std::int64_t) xToTick (rulerClip.getRight())) + 1))
  1292	        {
  1293	            if ((b.number - 1) % every != 0)
  1294	                continue;
  1295	
  1296	            const auto x = (int) tickToX ((double) b.tick);
  1297	            g.setColour (colour::textDisabled);
  1298	            g.drawVerticalLine (x, 12.0f, (float) timelineHeight);
  1299	            g.setColour (colour::textSecondary);
  1300	            textcache::drawText (g, juce::String (b.number), x + 3, 2, 40, timelineHeight - 4, juce::Justification::centredLeft);
  1301	        }
  1302	
  1303	        // markers: small flag with the name; time signature changes show the fraction
  1304	        g.setFont (font (type::caption, true));
  1305	
  1306	        for (const auto& m : song.markers())
  1307	        {
  1308	            const auto x = (float) tickToX ((double) m.tick);
  1309	
  1310	            if (x < (float) ruler.getX() - 200.0f || x > (float) ruler.getRight())
  1311	                continue;
  1312	
  1313	            const auto label = markerLabel (m);
  1314	            const auto width = (float) juce::GlyphArrangement::getStringWidthInt (g.getCurrentFont(), label) + 8.0f;
  1315	            const auto tag = juce::Rectangle<float> (x, (float) timelineHeight - 11.0f, width, 11.0f);
  1316	            // recording markers (Start / Stop recording) in the accent colour, as the record button
  1317	            const auto punch = m.action == Marker::punchIn || m.action == Marker::punchOut;
  1318	            const auto acts = ! m.isSignature() && m.action != 0 && ! punch;
  1319	            g.setColour (m.isSignature() ? colour::info : punch ? colour::accent : acts ? colour::automation : colour::warning);
  1320	            g.fillRoundedRectangle (tag, 2.0f);
  1321	            g.drawVerticalLine ((int) x, 0.0f, (float) timelineHeight);
  1322	            g.setColour (colour::bg0);
  1323	            textcache::drawText (g, label, tag.reduced (3.0f, 0.0f), juce::Justification::centredLeft, false);
  1324	        }
  1325	
  1326	        if (playheadTick >= 0.0 && ! cachePainting)
  1327	        {
  1328	            const auto x = (float) tickToX (playheadTick);
  1329	            juce::Path tri;
  1330	            tri.addTriangle (x - 6, 0, x + 6, 0, x, 9);
  1331	            g.setColour (colour::playhead);
  1332	            g.fillPath (tri);
  1333	        }
  1334	    }
  1335	
  1336	    // --- track headers -----------------------------------------------------
  1337	    // FL's layout: the name on the left, a group header's fold arrow and the mute LED on the right; grouped
  1338	    // tracks sit indented on a block in their header's colour; members of a folded group are thin rows
  1339	    {
  1340	        const uitrace::Scope traceHeaders ("Playlist headers", false);
  1341	        const auto headers = juce::Rectangle<int> (0, headerHeight, headerWidth, grid.getHeight());
  1342	        juce::Graphics::ScopedSaveState state (g);
  1343	        if (g.reduceClipRegion (headers)) // (not repainted, e.g. the playhead's strip: nothing to draw; round 174e)
  1344	        {
  1345	            g.setColour (colour::bg2);
  1346	            g.fillRect (headers);
  1347	            g.setFont (font (type::small));
  1348	
  1349	            auto colourOf = [&song] (int t) { return t >= 0 && t < (int) song.playlistTracks.size() ? song.playlistTracks[(std::size_t) t].colour : 0u; };
  1350	
  1351	            // group blocks first (behind the headers): from the header's top to the last member's bottom
  1352	            for (int t = 0; t < tracks; ++t)
  1353	            {
  1354	                if (! noteedit::isGroupHeader (song, t))
  1355	                    continue;
  1356	                const auto members = noteedit::groupMembers (song, t);
  1357	                const auto last = members.empty() ? t : members.back();
  1358	                const auto top = trackToY (t), bottom = trackToY (last) + heightOf (last);
  1359	                if (top > headers.getBottom() || bottom < headers.getY())
  1360	                    continue;
  1361	                auto block = colour::bg3;
  1362	                if (const auto c = colourOf (t); c != 0)
  1363	                    block = block.interpolatedWith (tame (juce::Colour (c)), 0.28f);
  1364	                g.setColour (block);
  1365	                g.fillRoundedRectangle (juce::Rectangle<float> (1.0f, (float) top + 1.0f, (float) headerWidth - 2.0f, (float) (bottom - top) - 2.0f), radius::md);
  1366	            }
  1367	
  1368	            for (int t = 0; t < tracks; ++t)
  1369	            {
  1370	                const auto y = trackToY (t);
  1371	                const auto trackHeight = heightOf (t);
  1372	
  1373	                if (y > headers.getBottom() || y + trackHeight < headers.getY())
  1374	                    continue;
  1375	
  1376	                if (noteedit::isFoldedAway (song, t))
  1377	                    continue; // the group block shows through: a thin row, as FL draws a folded group
  1378	
  1379	                const auto muted = song.isTrackMuted (t);
  1380	                const auto custom = t < (int) song.playlistTracks.size() && ! song.playlistTracks[(std::size_t) t].name.empty();
  1381	                const auto hovered = hover.header && hover.track == t;
  1382	                const auto member = noteedit::groupHeaderOf (song, t) >= 0 && ! noteedit::isGroupHeader (song, t);
  1383	                const auto groupHead = noteedit::isGroupHeader (song, t);
  1384	                const auto left = member ? (float) groupIndent : 2.0f;
  1385	                auto plate = muted ? colour::bg2 : colour::bg3;
  1386	                const auto trackColour = colourOf (t);
  1387	                if (trackColour != 0) // the track's colour (FL projects colour their track headers by group)
  1388	                    plate = plate.interpolatedWith (tame (juce::Colour (trackColour)), muted ? 0.22f : (groupHead ? 0.62f : 0.5f));
  1389	                if (hovered)
  1390	                    plate = plate.interpolatedWith (colour::bg5, 0.6f);
  1391	                const auto plateArea = juce::Rectangle<float> (left, (float) y + 1.0f, (float) headerWidth - 2.0f - left, (float) trackHeight - 3.0f);
  1392	                g.setColour (plate);
  1393	                g.fillRoundedRectangle (plateArea, radius::sm);
  1394	
  1395	                if (isTrackSelected (t)) // the selected tracks (FL marks them next to the LED)
  1396	                {
  1397	                    g.setColour (colour::accent);
  1398	                    g.fillRoundedRectangle (plateArea.withWidth (3.0f).reduced (0.0f, 3.0f), 1.5f);
  1399	                }
  1400	
  1401	                const auto led = trackLed (t).toFloat();
  1402	                g.setColour (muteLed (muted));
  1403	                g.fillEllipse (led.withSizeKeepingCentre (8.0f, 8.0f));
  1404	                if (hovered && hover.led) // the LED is a button: ring it
  1405	                {
  1406	                    g.setColour (colour::textPrimary.withAlpha (0.7f));
  1407	                    g.drawEllipse (led.withSizeKeepingCentre (12.0f, 12.0f), 1.0f);
  1408	                }
  1409	
  1410	                auto textRight = led.getX() - 2.0f;
  1411	
  1412	                // an audio track's record arm (and, on tall rows, input) button: red while its mixer track is armed (FL 2026)
  1413	                if (const auto armBox = trackArm (t); ! armBox.isEmpty())
  1414	                {
  1415	                    const auto insert = trackmodes::armableInsert (song, t);
  1416	                    const auto armed = engine.isInsertArmed (insert);
  1417	                    const auto b = armBox.toFloat();
  1418	                    const auto lit = hovered && hover.arm;
  1419	                    if (armed)
  1420	                    {
  1421	                        g.setColour (colour::record);
  1422	                        g.fillEllipse (b.withSizeKeepingCentre (10.0f, 10.0f));
  1423	                    }
  1424	                    else
  1425	                    {
  1426	                        g.setColour ((lit ? colour::textPrimary : colour::textSecondary).withAlpha (muted ? 0.5f : 1.0f));
  1427	                        g.fillEllipse (b.withSizeKeepingCentre (4.0f, 4.0f));
  1428	                    }
  1429	                    g.setColour (armed ? colour::record.brighter (lit ? 0.4f : 0.0f) : (lit ? colour::textPrimary : colour::textSecondary.withAlpha (0.7f)));
  1430	                    g.drawEllipse (b.withSizeKeepingCentre (10.0f, 10.0f), 1.2f);
  1431	                    textRight = b.getX() - 2.0f;
  1432	
  1433	                    if (const auto inBox = trackInput (t); ! inBox.isEmpty())
  1434	                    {
  1435	                        // "Audio input source": an arrow into a bar, lit when the mixer track has an input
  1436	                        const auto r = inBox.toFloat().reduced (1.5f);
  1437	                        const auto hasInput = song.mixer[(std::size_t) insert].inputChannel >= 0;
  1438	                        g.setColour (hovered && hover.input ? colour::textPrimary : (hasInput ? colour::accent : colour::textSecondary));
  1439	                        juce::Path in;
  1440	                        const auto cy = r.getCentreY();
  1441	                        in.startNewSubPath (r.getX(), cy);
  1442	                        in.lineTo (r.getRight() - 3.5f, cy);
  1443	                        in.startNewSubPath (r.getRight() - 6.5f, cy - 3.0f);
  1444	                        in.lineTo (r.getRight() - 3.5f, cy);
  1445	                        in.lineTo (r.getRight() - 6.5f, cy + 3.0f);
  1446	                        g.strokePath (in, juce::PathStrokeType (1.2f));
  1447	                        g.fillRect (juce::Rectangle<float> (r.getRight() - 1.5f, r.getY(), 1.3f, r.getHeight()));
  1448	                        textRight = r.getX() - 2.0f;
  1449	                    }
  1450	                }
  1451	
  1452	                if (const auto arrow = trackArrow (t); ! arrow.isEmpty())
  1453	                {
  1454	                    const auto collapsed = t < (int) song.playlistTracks.size() && song.playlistTracks[(std::size_t) t].collapsed;
  1455	                    const auto a = arrow.toFloat().withSizeKeepingCentre (8.0f, 5.0f);
  1456	                    juce::Path tri;
  1457	                    if (collapsed) // folded: points down (FL), open: up
  1458	                        tri.addTriangle (a.getX(), a.getY(), a.getRight(), a.getY(), a.getCentreX(), a.getBottom());
  1459	                    else
  1460	                        tri.addTriangle (a.getX(), a.getBottom(), a.getRight(), a.getBottom(), a.getCentreX(), a.getY());
  1461	                    g.setColour (hovered && hover.arrow ? colour::textPrimary : colour::textSecondary);
  1462	                    g.fillPath (tri);
  1463	                    textRight = juce::jmin (textRight, (float) arrow.getX());
  1464	                }
  1465	
  1466	                // the track's icon before its name, as FL's track header shows it (round 168h)
  1467	                auto textLeft = (int) left + 6;
  1468	                if (const auto icon = trackicons::iconFor (t < (int) song.playlistTracks.size() ? song.playlistTracks[(std::size_t) t].icon : 0);
  1469	                    icon != trackicons::Icon::none && trackHeight >= 14)
  1470	                {
  1471	                    const auto size = (float) juce::jmin (14, trackHeight - 4);
  1472	                    ui::drawTrackIcon (g, icon, juce::Rectangle<float> ((float) textLeft, (float) y + ((float) trackHeight - size) * 0.5f, size, size),
  1473	                                       muted ? colour::textDisabled : colour::textSecondary.brighter (0.3f));
  1474	                    textLeft += (int) size + 4;
  1475	                }
  1476	                g.setColour (muted ? colour::textDisabled : (custom ? colour::textPrimary : colour::textSecondary));
  1477	                g.setFont (font (type::small, groupHead));
  1478	                textcache::drawFittedText (g, custom ? juce::String::fromUTF8 (song.playlistTracks[(std::size_t) t].name.c_str())
  1479	                                         : tr ("Track {0}", { juce::String (t + 1) }),
  1480	                                  textLeft, y, juce::jmax (8, (int) textRight - textLeft - 2), trackHeight, juce::Justification::centredLeft,
  1481	                                  trackHeight >= 30 ? 2 : 1, 0.8f);
  1482	
  1483	                // the divider under the header: lit while hovered or dragged (drag resizes the track)
  1484	                if (hover.divider == t || (drag == Drag::trackResize && (resizeTrack == t || resizeAll)))
  1485	                {
  1486	                    g.setColour (colour::accent);
  1487	                    g.fillRoundedRectangle (juce::Rectangle<float> (6.0f, (float) (y + trackHeight) - 2.0f, (float) headerWidth - 12.0f, 3.0f), 1.5f);
  1488	                }
  1489	            }
  1490	        }
  1491	    }
  1492	
  1493	    // toolbar band, and the corner left of the ruler
  1494	    g.setColour (colour::bg3);
  1495	    g.fillRect (0, 0, getWidth(), toolbarHeight);
  1496	    g.setColour (colour::bg2);
  1497	    g.fillRect (0, toolbarHeight, headerWidth, headerHeight - toolbarHeight);
  1498	    g.setColour (colour::border);
  1499	    g.fillRect (0, toolbarHeight - 1, getWidth(), 1);
  1500	    g.fillRect (0, headerHeight - 1, headerWidth, 1);
  1501	    g.fillRect (headerWidth - 1, toolbarHeight, 1, getHeight() - toolbarHeight);
  1502	
  1503	    // the vertical zoom handle above the scroll bar (FL's track height control): a grip of three lines
  1504	    {
  1505	        const auto r = trackZoomHandle().toFloat().reduced (1.0f, 2.0f);
  1506	        const auto active = trackZoomHover || drag == Drag::trackZoom;
  1507	        theme::fillRoundRect (g, r, theme::radius::sm, active ? colour::bg4 : colour::bg2);
  1508	        g.setColour (active ? colour::accent : (std::abs (trackZoom - 1.0) > 1.0e-3 ? colour::textSecondary : colour::textDisabled));
  1509	        const auto cx = r.getCentreX(), cy = r.getCentreY();
  1510	        for (int i = -1; i <= 1; ++i)
  1511	            g.fillRect (juce::Rectangle<float> (cx - 3.0f, cy + (float) i * 4.0f - 0.5f, 6.0f, 1.2f));
  1512	        g.drawLine (cx, r.getY() + 2.0f, cx, cy - 6.0f, 1.0f);
  1513	        g.drawLine (cx, cy + 6.0f, cx, r.getBottom() - 2.0f, 1.0f);
  1514	    }
  1515	    uitrace::flushParts ("Playlist", trace.elapsedMs());
  1516	}
  1517	
  1518	void PlaylistComponent::paintOverview (juce::Graphics& g, juce::Rectangle<float> area, double total)
  1519	{
  1520	    // every clip of the arrangement in miniature (its colour, its track's row), bar lines, the song's end
  1521	    using namespace theme;
  1522	    const auto& song = engine.getSong();
  1523	    const auto scale = area.getWidth() / juce::jmax (1.0, total);
  1524	    const auto bar = (double) ppq * juce::jmax (1, song.beatsPerBar);
  1525	
  1526	    g.setColour (colour::bg2);
  1527	    g.fillRect (area.withWidth (juce::jmin (area.getWidth(), (float) (song.songLength() * scale))));
  1528	
  1529	    const auto every = bar * scale < 3.0 ? 16 : bar * scale < 6.0 ? 4 : 1;
  1530	    g.setColour (colour::gridBar.withAlpha (0.5f));
  1531	    for (int b = every; b * bar < total; b += every)
  1532	        g.fillRect ((float) (area.getX() + b * bar * scale), area.getY(), 1.0f, area.getHeight());
  1533	
  1534	    int rows = 1;
  1535	    for (const auto& c : song.playlist)
  1536	        rows = juce::jmax (rows, c.track + 1);
  1537	
  1538	    const auto inner = area.reduced (0.0f, 1.0f);
  1539	    const auto rowHeight = inner.getHeight() / (float) rows;
  1540	
  1541	    for (const auto& c : song.playlist)
  1542	    {
  1543	        auto base = colour::automation;
  1544	        if (c.kind == ClipKind::pattern && c.pattern >= 0 && c.pattern < (int) song.patterns.size())
  1545	            base = tame (patternColour (song, c.pattern));
  1546	        else if (c.kind == ClipKind::audio && c.channel >= 0 && c.channel < (int) song.channels.size())
  1547	            base = tame (audioClipColour (song.channels[(std::size_t) c.channel]));
  1548	
  1549	        const auto y = inner.getY() + rowHeight * (float) c.track;
  1550	        g.setColour (c.muted ? base.withAlpha (0.35f) : base);
  1551	        g.fillRect ((float) (area.getX() + c.start * scale), y, juce::jmax (1.0f, (float) (c.length * scale) - 0.5f),
  1552	                    juce::jmax (1.0f, rowHeight - (rowHeight >= 3.0f ? 0.5f : 0.0f)));
  1553	    }
  1554	}
  1555	
  1556	// The playhead over the cached image (round 174e): the line in the grid and the triangle on the ruler, as paint()
  1557	// draws them without the cache
  1558	void PlaylistComponent::paintPlayhead (juce::Graphics& g)
  1559	{
  1560	    if (playheadTick < 0.0)
  1561	        return;
  1562	    using namespace theme;
  1563	    const auto grid = gridArea();
  1564	    const auto x = (float) tickToX (playheadTick);
  1565	    {
  1566	        juce::Graphics::ScopedSaveState state (g);
  1567	        if (g.reduceClipRegion (grid))
  1568	        {
  1569	            g.setColour (colour::playhead);
  1570	            g.fillRect (juce::Rectangle<float> (x - 0.5f, (float) grid.getY(), 1.5f, (float) grid.getHeight()));
  1571	        }
  1572	    }
  1573	    juce::Graphics::ScopedSaveState state (g);
  1574	    if (g.reduceClipRegion (juce::Rectangle<int> (headerWidth, rulerTop, grid.getWidth(), timelineHeight)))
  1575	    {
  1576	        juce::Path tri;
  1577	        tri.addTriangle (x - 6, (float) rulerTop, x + 6, (float) rulerTop, x, (float) rulerTop + 9.0f);
  1578	        g.setColour (colour::playhead);
  1579	        g.fillPath (tri);
  1580	    }
  1581	}
  1582	
  1583	void PlaylistComponent::paintOverChildren (juce::Graphics& g)
  1584	{
  1585	    const auto& song = engine.getSong();
  1586	    if (song.performanceMode)
  1587	    {
  1588	        // Performance mode: a tag in the grid's top-right corner (left of the Ripple tag), and each playing track's progress on its header
  1589	        using namespace theme;
  1590	        const auto grid = gridArea();
  1591	        const auto tag = juce::Rectangle<float> ((float) grid.getRight() - 176.0f, (float) grid.getY() + 4.0f, 100.0f, 18.0f);
  1592	        g.setColour (colour::bg0.withAlpha (0.8f));
  1593	        g.fillRoundedRectangle (tag, radius::sm);
  1594	        g.setColour (colour::info);
  1595	        g.drawRoundedRectangle (tag.reduced (0.5f), radius::sm, 1.0f);
  1596	        g.setFont (font (type::caption, true));
  1597	        textcache::drawText (g, tr ("Performance"), tag.toNearestInt(), juce::Justification::centred);
  1598	
  1599	        const auto& live = engine.performanceState();
  1600	        if (showTrackProgress && live.anyPlaying.load (std::memory_order_relaxed))
  1601	        {
  1602	            juce::Graphics::ScopedSaveState state (g);
  1603	            g.reduceClipRegion (0, grid.getY(), headerWidth, grid.getHeight());
  1604	            for (int t = 0; t < juce::jmin (numTracks(), perf::maxTracks); ++t)
  1605	            {
  1606	                const auto& view = live.tracks[(std::size_t) t];
  1607	                if (view.playing.load (std::memory_order_relaxed) < 0)
  1608	                    continue;
  1609	                const auto y = trackToY (t), h = heightOf (t);
  1610	                if (y > grid.getBottom() || y + h < grid.getY())
  1611	                    continue;
  1612	                const auto p = juce::jlimit (0.0f, 1.0f, view.progress.load (std::memory_order_relaxed));
  1613	                const auto bar = juce::Rectangle<float> (4.0f, (float) (y + h) - 5.0f, (float) headerWidth - 8.0f, 3.0f);
  1614	                g.setColour (colour::bg0);
  1615	                g.fillRect (bar);
  1616	                g.setColour (colour::ledOn);
  1617	                g.fillRect (bar.withWidth (bar.getWidth() * p));
  1618	            }
  1619	        }
  1620	    }
  1621	
  1622	    if (! ripple)
  1623	        return;
  1624	
  1625	    // ripple editing is on: a small tag in the grid's top-right corner
  1626	    const auto grid = gridArea();
  1627	    const auto tag = juce::Rectangle<float> ((float) grid.getRight() - 70.0f, (float) grid.getY() + 4.0f, 64.0f, 18.0f);
  1628	    g.setColour (theme::colour::accentMuted);
  1629	    g.fillRoundedRectangle (tag, theme::radius::sm);
  1630	    g.setColour (theme::colour::accent);
  1631	    g.drawRoundedRectangle (tag.reduced (0.5f), theme::radius::sm, 1.0f);
  1632	    g.setFont (theme::font (theme::type::caption, true));
  1633	    textcache::drawText (g, tr ("Ripple"), tag.toNearestInt(), juce::Justification::centred);
  1634	}
  1635	
  1636	void PlaylistComponent::addSmallClip (SmallClips& batch, float physicalScale, const Clip& c, juce::Rectangle<float> r, bool current)
  1637	{
  1638	    const auto& song = engine.getSong();
  1639	    juce::Colour base;
  1640	    if (c.kind == ClipKind::audio)
  1641	    {
  1642	        if (c.channel < 0 || c.channel >= (int) song.channels.size())
  1643	            return;
  1644	        base = audioClipColour (song.channels[(std::size_t) c.channel]);
  1645	    }
  1646	    else
  1647	        base = theme::tame (patternColour (song, c.pattern));
  1648	
  1649	    const auto title = r.withHeight (clipHeaderHeight (r));
  1650	    const auto body = r.withTrimmedTop (title.getHeight());
  1651	    batch.bodies[clipBodyColour (base).getARGB()].addWithoutMerging (body);
  1652	    if (title.getHeight() >= 4.0f)
  1653	        batch.bodies[clipHeaderColour (base).getARGB()].addWithoutMerging (title);
  1654	    batch.edges[(current ? theme::colour::textPrimary.withAlpha (0.55f) : theme::colour::bg0.withAlpha (0.85f)).getARGB()].addWithoutMerging (
  1655	        { r.getRight() - 1.0f, r.getY(), 1.0f, r.getHeight() });
  1656	
  1657	    if (c.kind == ClipKind::audio && body.getWidth() >= 3.0f && body.getHeight() >= 3.0f)
  1658	    {
  1659	        const auto& channel = song.channels[(std::size_t) c.channel];
  1660	        const auto path = juce::String::fromUTF8 (channel.samplePath.c_str());
  1661	        const auto summary = ui::WaveformCache::getInstance()->find (path, [this, path] { return engine.getSampleLibrary().load (path); });
  1662	        if (summary == nullptr)
  1663	            return;
  1664	        // the same mapping as paintAudioClip
  1665	        const auto wave = body.reduced (0.0f, body.getHeight() >= 14.0f ? 2.0f : 1.0f);
  1666	        const auto total = (double) summary->frames;
  1667	        const auto perTick = clipops::framesPerTick (channel, c, total, summary->sampleRate, song.bpm);
  1668	        const auto startTick = (double) c.offset + (double) (wave.getX() - r.getX()) / pixelsPerTick;
  1669	        const auto forward = ! clipops::playsBackwards (channel, c);
  1670	        const auto startFrame = forward ? startTick * perTick : total - startTick * perTick;
  1671	        const auto colour = clipContentColour (base);
  1672	        auto style = ui::currentWaveStyle (colour, juce::jmin (ui::normalisingGain (*summary), 4.0f) * c.gain);
  1673	        if (! style.spectral && c.gain > 1.0f)
  1674	            style.gain = juce::jmin (style.gain, 8.0f);
  1675	        if (! style.spectral)
  1676	            ui::appendWaveformPath (batch.waves[colour.getARGB()], physicalScale, wave, wave, *summary, startFrame,
  1677	                                    (forward ? perTick : -perTick) / pixelsPerTick, style);
  1678	        return;
  1679	    }
  1680	
  1681	    if (c.kind == ClipKind::pattern && c.pattern >= 0 && c.pattern < (int) song.patterns.size() && body.getHeight() >= 4.0f)
  1682	    {
  1683	        // the notes as one-pixel-wide marks at their pitch (the same placement as the full clip)
  1684	        const auto& notes = song.patterns[(std::size_t) c.pattern].notes;
  1685	        if (notes.empty())
  1686	            return;
  1687	        int low = 127, high = 0;
  1688	        for (const auto& n : notes)
  1689	        {
  1690	            low = juce::jmin (low, n.key);
  1691	            high = juce::jmax (high, n.key);
  1692	        }
  1693	        const auto inner = body.reduced (0.0f, body.getHeight() >= 12.0f ? 3.0f : 1.0f);
  1694	        const auto span = juce::jmax (6, high - low + 2);
  1695	        const auto bottomKey = (low + high) / 2.0f - span / 2.0f;
  1696	        const auto rows = (float) span + 1.0f;
  1697	        const auto noteHeight = juce::jlimit (1.5f, 2.5f, inner.getHeight() / rows);
  1698	        auto& marks = batch.contents[clipContentColour (base).getARGB()];
  1699	        // what plays: looped along a stretched clip with the LYNX option (issue #25), else once (FL, round 168k)
  1700	        const auto passes = clipPasses (c, song.patterns[(std::size_t) c.pattern].effectiveLength (song.beatsPerBar));
  1701	        for (auto pass = passes.first; pass <= passes.last; ++pass)
  1702	            for (const auto& n : notes)
  1703	            {
  1704	                const auto at = c.start - c.offset + pass * passes.length + n.start;
  1705	                if ((passes.loops && n.start >= passes.length) || at + n.length <= c.start || at >= c.end())
  1706	                    continue;
  1707	                const auto nx = juce::jmax (inner.getX(), (float) tickToX ((double) at));
  1708	                const auto nr = juce::jmin (inner.getRight() - 1.0f, nx + juce::jmax (1.0f, (float) (n.length * pixelsPerTick) - 1.0f));
  1709	                if (nr <= nx)
  1710	                    continue;
  1711	                const auto ny = inner.getBottom() - ((float) n.key - bottomKey + 0.5f) / rows * inner.getHeight() - noteHeight * 0.5f;
  1712	                marks.addWithoutMerging ({ nx, ny, nr - nx, noteHeight });
  1713	            }
  1714	    }
  1715	}
  1716	
  1717	void PlaylistComponent::paintAudioClip (juce::Graphics& g, const Clip& c, juce::Rectangle<float> r)
  1718	{
  1719	    const uitrace::Scope trace ("Playlist audio clip", false);
  1720	    const auto& song = engine.getSong();
  1721	
  1722	    if (c.channel < 0 || c.channel >= (int) song.channels.size())
  1723	        return;
  1724	
  1725	    const auto& channel = song.channels[(std::size_t) c.channel];
  1726	    const auto colour = audioClipColour (channel);
  1727	    const auto name = juce::String::fromUTF8 (channel.name.c_str());
  1728	    const auto body = paintClipFrame (g, r, colour, name, ClipGlyph::audio);
  1729	
  1730	    // the waveform (ui/WaveformView, analysed in the background): the clean style fills the body with the
  1731	    // peak envelope mirrored around the centre, in a light tint of the clip colour
  1732	    const auto path = juce::String::fromUTF8 (channel.samplePath.c_str());
  1733	    const auto summary = ui::WaveformCache::getInstance()->find (path, [this, path] { return engine.getSampleLibrary().load (path); });
  1734	
  1735	    if (summary != nullptr && body.getHeight() >= 3.0f && body.getWidth() >= 3.0f) // (narrower: the body colour says enough)
  1736	    {
  1737	        const auto wave = body.reduced (0.0f, body.getHeight() >= 14.0f ? 2.0f : 1.0f);
  1738	        const auto total = (double) summary->frames;
  1739	        const auto perTick = clipops::framesPerTick (channel, c, total, summary->sampleRate, song.bpm);
  1740	        // r's left edge is the clip start, i.e. tick `offset` of the audio (counted from the file's end when
  1741	        // the channel is reversed)
  1742	        const auto startTick = (double) c.offset + (double) (wave.getX() - r.getX()) / pixelsPerTick;
  1743	        const auto forward = ! clipops::playsBackwards (channel, c);
  1744	        const auto startFrame = forward ? startTick * perTick : total - startTick * perTick;
  1745	        auto style = ui::currentWaveStyle (clipContentColour (colour), juce::jmin (ui::normalisingGain (*summary), 4.0f) * c.gain);
  1746	        if (! style.spectral && c.gain > 1.0f) // louder clip gain: the shape may hit the top, which is honest
  1747	            style.gain = juce::jmin (style.gain, 8.0f);
  1748	        const uitrace::Part tally (uitrace::part::waveform);
  1749	        ui::paintWaveform (g, wave, *summary, startFrame, (forward ? perTick : -perTick) / pixelsPerTick, style);
  1750	    }
  1751	
  1752	    paintClipOutline (g, r, colour, false, false);
  1753	
  1754	    // fades: a shaded ramp over the body and a handle at the header's corner; an automatic crossfade (round 166e)
  1755	    // replaces that side's fade with FL's two crossing S-curves over the overlap
  1756	    const auto fadeIn = c.effectiveFadeIn(), fadeOut = c.effectiveFadeOut();
  1757	    if (fadeIn > 0 || fadeOut > 0)
  1758	    {
  1759	        const auto inX = r.getX() + (float) (fadeIn * pixelsPerTick);
  1760	        const auto outX = r.getRight() - (float) (fadeOut * pixelsPerTick);
  1761	        const auto shade = theme::colour::bg0.withAlpha (0.45f);
  1762	        const auto line = clipContentColour (colour).withAlpha (0.9f);
  1763	        // the gain curve from x0 to x1 (rising: a fade in), as a line along the body
  1764	        auto curve = [&body] (float x0, float x1, bool rising, bool sCurve) {
  1765	            juce::Path p;
  1766	            const auto steps = sCurve ? juce::jlimit (2, 48, (int) std::abs (x1 - x0) / 3) : 1;
  1767	            for (int k = 0; k <= steps; ++k)
  1768	            {
  1769	                const auto t = (float) k / (float) steps;
  1770	                const auto level = sCurve ? clipops::crossfadeCurve (rising ? t : 1.0f - t) : (rising ? t : 1.0f - t);
  1771	                const juce::Point<float> q { x0 + (x1 - x0) * t, body.getBottom() - level * body.getHeight() };
  1772	                if (k == 0)
  1773	                    p.startNewSubPath (q);
  1774	                else
  1775	                    p.lineTo (q);
  1776	            }
  1777	            return p;
  1778	        };
  1779	        auto paintFade = [&] (float x0, float x1, bool rising, bool cross) {
  1780	            auto edge = curve (x0, x1, rising, cross);
  1781	            auto area = edge;
  1782	            area.lineTo (x1, body.getY());
  1783	            area.lineTo (x0, body.getY());
  1784	            area.closeSubPath();
  1785	            g.setColour (shade);
  1786	            g.fillPath (area);
  1787	            g.setColour (line);
  1788	            g.strokePath (edge, juce::PathStrokeType (1.2f));
  1789	            if (cross) // the neighbour's side of the crossfade, which this clip covers
  1790	            {
  1791	                g.setColour (line.withMultipliedAlpha (0.6f));
  1792	                g.strokePath (curve (x0, x1, ! rising, true), juce::PathStrokeType (1.0f));
  1793	            }
  1794	        };
  1795	
  1796	        if (fadeIn > 0)
  1797	            paintFade (body.getX(), inX, true, c.crossfadeIn > 0);
  1798	        if (fadeOut > 0)
  1799	            paintFade (outX, body.getRight(), false, c.crossfadeOut > 0);
  1800	    }
  1801	}
  1802	
  1803	juce::Colour PlaylistComponent::audioClipColour (const ChannelDef& channel)
  1804	{
  1805	    return channel.colour != 0 ? theme::tame (juce::Colour (channel.colour)) : theme::colour::audioClip;
  1806	}
  1807	
  1808	void PlaylistComponent::paintAutomationClip (juce::Graphics& g, const Clip& c, juce::Rectangle<float> r)
  1809	{
  1810	    const auto& song = engine.getSong();
  1811	
  1812	    if (c.automation < 0 || c.automation >= (int) song.automations.size())
  1813	        return;
  1814	
  1815	    const auto& automation = song.automations[(std::size_t) c.automation];
  1816	    const auto colour = automation.target.kind == AutomationTargetKind::none ? theme::colour::textDisabled
  1817	                                                                              : theme::colour::automation.withMultipliedSaturation (0.75f).withMultipliedBrightness (0.8f);
  1818	    paintClipFrame (g, r, colour, juce::String::fromUTF8 (automation.name.c_str()), ClipGlyph::automation);
  1819	    paintClipOutline (g, r, colour, false, false);
  1820	
  1821	    // automation curve over the clip's range
  1822	    const auto body = r.withTrimmedTop (juce::jmin (14.0f, r.getHeight() * 0.4f)).reduced (1.0f, 2.0f); // = automationBody
  1823	    const auto clip = g.getClipBounds().toFloat();
  1824	    juce::Path curve;
  1825	    bool started = false;
  1826	
  1827	    for (auto x = std::max (body.getX(), clip.getX()); x <= std::min (body.getRight(), clip.getRight()); x += 2.0f)
  1828	    {
  1829	        const auto value = automation.valueAt ((double) c.offset + xToTick ((int) x) - (double) c.start);
  1830	        const auto y = body.getBottom() - value * body.getHeight();
  1831	
  1832	        if (! started)
  1833	            curve.startNewSubPath (x, y);
  1834	        else
  1835	            curve.lineTo (x, y);
  1836	
  1837	        started = true;
  1838	    }
  1839	
  1840	    g.setColour (automation.target.kind == AutomationTargetKind::none ? theme::colour::textSecondary : theme::colour::automation.brighter (0.3f));
  1841	    g.strokePath (curve, juce::PathStrokeType (1.5f));
  1842	
  1843	    // round 165: FL's tension handles between the points (hollow circles: drag up / down bends the segment)
  1844	    if (r.getWidth() > 24.0f && body.getHeight() >= 10.0f)
  1845	        for (int i = 0; i + 1 < (int) automation.points.size(); ++i)
  1846	        {
  1847	            const auto& a = automation.points[(std::size_t) i];
  1848	            const auto& b = automation.points[(std::size_t) i + 1];
  1849	            const auto x0 = tickToX ((double) (c.start + a.tick - c.offset)), x1 = tickToX ((double) (c.start + b.tick - c.offset));
  1850	            const auto mid = 0.5 * (double) (a.tick + b.tick);
  1851	            const auto midX = (float) (0.5 * (x0 + x1));
  1852	            if (x1 - x0 < 16.0 || midX < body.getX() || midX > body.getRight())
  1853	                continue;
  1854	            const auto y = body.getBottom() - automation.valueAt (mid) * body.getHeight();
  1855	            g.setColour (theme::colour::automation.brighter (0.2f).withAlpha (0.9f));
  1856	            g.drawEllipse (midX - 3.0f, y - 3.0f, 6.0f, 6.0f, 1.2f);
  1857	        }
  1858	
  1859	    // the points inside the clip, as handles (edited right here)
  1860	    if (r.getWidth() > 24.0f)
  1861	        for (const auto& p : automation.points)
  1862	        {
  1863	            const auto songTick = (double) (c.start + p.tick - c.offset);
  1864	            if (songTick < (double) c.start || songTick > (double) c.end())
  1865	                continue;
  1866	            const auto x = (float) tickToX (songTick);
  1867	            const auto y = body.getBottom() - p.value * body.getHeight();
  1868	            g.setColour (theme::colour::bg0);
  1869	            g.fillEllipse (x - 3.5f, y - 3.5f, 7.0f, 7.0f);
  1870	            g.setColour (theme::colour::automation.brighter (0.5f));
  1871	            g.fillEllipse (x - 2.5f, y - 2.5f, 5.0f, 5.0f);
  1872	        }
  1873	}
  1874	
  1875	juce::Rectangle<float> PlaylistComponent::automationBody (const Clip& c) const
  1876	{
  1877	    const auto x0 = (float) tickToX ((double) c.start), x1 = (float) tickToX ((double) c.end());
  1878	    const auto y = (float) trackToY (c.track);
  1879	    const auto r = juce::Rectangle<float> (x0, y + 1.0f, juce::jmax (3.0f, x1 - x0 - 1.0f), (float) heightOf (c.track) - 3.0f);
  1880	    return r.withTrimmedTop (juce::jmin (14.0f, r.getHeight() * 0.4f)).reduced (1.0f, 2.0f);
  1881	}
  1882	
  1883	int PlaylistComponent::automationPointAt (int clip, juce::Point<int> pos) const
  1884	{
  1885	    const auto& song = engine.getSong();
  1886	
  1887	    if (clip < 0 || clip >= (int) song.playlist.size() || song.playlist[(std::size_t) clip].kind != ClipKind::automation)
  1888	        return -1;
  1889	
  1890	    const auto& c = song.playlist[(std::size_t) clip];
  1891	    if (c.automation < 0 || c.automation >= (int) song.automations.size())
  1892	        return -1;
  1893	
  1894	    const auto body = automationBody (c);
  1895	    const auto& points = song.automations[(std::size_t) c.automation].points;
  1896	
  1897	    for (int i = (int) points.size() - 1; i >= 0; --i)
  1898	    {
  1899	        const auto songTick = (double) (c.start + points[(std::size_t) i].tick - c.offset);
  1900	        if (songTick < (double) c.start || songTick > (double) c.end())
  1901	            continue;
  1902	        const juce::Point<float> at ((float) tickToX (songTick), body.getBottom() - points[(std::size_t) i].value * body.getHeight());
  1903	        if (at.getDistanceFrom (pos.toFloat()) <= 6.0f)
  1904	            return i;
  1905	    }
  1906	
  1907	    return -1;
  1908	}
  1909	
  1910	bool PlaylistComponent::isOnAutomationCurve (int clip, juce::Point<int> pos) const
  1911	{
  1912	    const auto& song = engine.getSong();
  1913	
  1914	    if (clip < 0 || clip >= (int) song.playlist.size() || song.playlist[(std::size_t) clip].kind != ClipKind::automation)
  1915	        return false;
  1916	
  1917	    const auto& c = song.playlist[(std::size_t) clip];
  1918	    if (c.automation < 0 || c.automation >= (int) song.automations.size())
  1919	        return false;
  1920	
  1921	    const auto body = automationBody (c);
  1922	    if (! body.expanded (0.0f, 4.0f).contains (pos.toFloat()))
  1923	        return false;
  1924	
  1925	    const auto value = song.automations[(std::size_t) c.automation].valueAt ((double) c.offset + xToTick (pos.x) - (double) c.start);
  1926	    return std::abs (body.getBottom() - value * body.getHeight() - (float) pos.y) <= 5.0f;
  1927	}
  1928	
  1929	bool PlaylistComponent::automationEditable (int clip) const
  1930	{
  1931	    const auto& song = engine.getSong();
  1932	    if (clip < 0 || clip >= (int) song.playlist.size())
  1933	        return false;
  1934	    const auto& c = song.playlist[(std::size_t) clip];
  1935	    if (c.kind != ClipKind::automation || c.automation < 0 || c.automation >= (int) song.automations.size())
  1936	        return false;
  1937	    const auto body = automationBody (c);
  1938	    return body.getWidth() > 24.0f && body.getHeight() >= 10.0f;
  1939	}
  1940	
  1941	juce::Point<float> PlaylistComponent::automationHandlePos (int clip, int point) const
  1942	{
  1943	    if (! automationEditable (clip))
  1944	        return { -1.0f, -1.0f };
  1945	    const auto& song = engine.getSong();
  1946	    const auto& c = song.playlist[(std::size_t) clip];
  1947	    const auto& automation = song.automations[(std::size_t) c.automation];
  1948	    if (point < 0 || point + 1 >= (int) automation.points.size())
  1949	        return { -1.0f, -1.0f };
  1950	    const auto& a = automation.points[(std::size_t) point];
  1951	    const auto& b = automation.points[(std::size_t) point + 1];
  1952	    const auto x0 = tickToX ((double) (c.start + a.tick - c.offset)), x1 = tickToX ((double) (c.start + b.tick - c.offset));
  1953	    const auto body = automationBody (c);
  1954	    const auto midX = (float) (0.5 * (x0 + x1));
  1955	    if (x1 - x0 < 16.0 || midX < body.getX() || midX > body.getRight())
  1956	        return { -1.0f, -1.0f };
  1957	    return { midX, body.getBottom() - automation.valueAt (0.5 * (double) (a.tick + b.tick)) * body.getHeight() };
  1958	}
  1959	
  1960	int PlaylistComponent::automationHandleAt (int clip, juce::Point<int> p) const
  1961	{
  1962	    if (! automationEditable (clip))
  1963	        return -1;
  1964	    const auto& song = engine.getSong();
  1965	    const auto n = (int) song.automations[(std::size_t) song.playlist[(std::size_t) clip].automation].points.size();
  1966	    for (int i = 0; i + 1 < n; ++i)
  1967	        if (const auto h = automationHandlePos (clip, i); h.x >= 0.0f && h.getDistanceFrom (p.toFloat()) <= 5.0f)
  1968	            return i;
  1969	    return -1;
  1970	}
  1971	
  1972	void PlaylistComponent::showAutomationPointMenu (int clip, int point)
  1973	{
  1974	    // FL's point menu (the parts LYNX's automation has): delete, copy / paste / type in the value, straight segment
  1975	    const auto& song = engine.getSong();
  1976	    if (clip < 0 || clip >= (int) song.playlist.size())
  1977	        return;
  1978	    const auto a = song.playlist[(std::size_t) clip].automation;
  1979	    if (a < 0 || a >= (int) song.automations.size() || point < 0 || point >= (int) song.automations[(std::size_t) a].points.size())
  1980	        return;
  1981	    const auto& automation = song.automations[(std::size_t) a];
  1982	    const auto value = automation.points[(std::size_t) point].value;
  1983	    juce::PopupMenu menu;
  1984	    menu.addSectionHeader (automationPointValueText (automation.target, value));
  1985	    menu.addItem (tr ("Delete"), automation.points.size() > 1, false, [this, a, point] {
  1986	        engine.editSong ([a, point] (Song& s) {
  1987	            auto& points = s.automations[(std::size_t) a].points;
  1988	            if (points.size() > 1 && point < (int) points.size())
  1989	                points.erase (points.begin() + point);
  1990	        });
  1991	    });
  1992	    menu.addSeparator();
  1993	    menu.addItem (tr ("Copy value"), [value] { copiedAutomationValue() = value; });
  1994	    menu.addItem (tr ("Paste value"), copiedAutomationValue() >= 0.0f, false, [this, a, point] {
  1995	        engine.editSong ([a, point, v = copiedAutomationValue()] (Song& s) {
  1996	            auto& points = s.automations[(std::size_t) a].points;
  1997	            if (point < (int) points.size())
  1998	                points[(std::size_t) point].value = v;
  1999	        });
  2000	    });
  2001	    menu.addItem (tr ("Type in value..."), [this, a, point] { typeAutomationPointValue (engine, a, point); });
  2002	    menu.addItem (tr ("Straight segment (reset the curve)"), point + 1 < (int) automation.points.size(), false, [this, a, point] {
  2003	        engine.editSong ([a, point] (Song& s) {
  2004	            auto& points = s.automations[(std::size_t) a].points;
  2005	            if (point < (int) points.size())
  2006	                points[(std::size_t) point].tension = 0.0f;
  2007	        });
  2008	    });
  2009	    menu.addSeparator();
  2010	    menu.addItem (tr ("Edit points..."), [this, a] { openAutomationEditor (engine, a); });
  2011	    menu.showMenuAsync (juce::PopupMenu::Options().withTargetComponent (this).withMousePosition());
  2012	}
  2013	
  2014	int PlaylistComponent::fadeHandleAt (int index, juce::Point<int> p) const
  2015	{
  2016	    const auto& c = engine.getSong().playlist[(std::size_t) index];
  2017	
  2018	    if (c.kind != ClipKind::audio)
  2019	        return 0;
  2020	
  2021	    const auto y = trackToY (c.track) + 1 + 15;
  2022	    const auto inX = tickToX ((double) c.start) + c.fadeIn * pixelsPerTick;
  2023	    const auto outX = tickToX ((double) c.end()) - c.fadeOut * pixelsPerTick;
  2024	
  2025	    if (std::abs (p.y - y) > 6)
  2026	        return 0;
  2027	    if (std::abs (p.x - (inX + 2.0)) <= 6.0)
  2028	        return 1;
  2029	    if (std::abs (p.x - (outX - 2.0)) <= 6.0)
  2030	        return 2;
  2031	    return 0;
  2032	}
  2033	
  2034	juce::Rectangle<float> PlaylistComponent::gainHandle (int index) const
  2035	{
  2036	    const auto& song = engine.getSong();
  2037	    if (index < 0 || index >= (int) song.playlist.size() || song.playlist[(std::size_t) index].kind != ClipKind::audio)
  2038	        return {};
  2039	    const auto r = clipBounds (index).toFloat();
  2040	    if (r.getWidth() < 30.0f || r.getHeight() - clipTitleHeight < 16.0f)
  2041	        return {};
  2042	    // FL 2026: a semicircle sitting on the clip's bottom edge, in the middle
  2043	    constexpr float radius = 5.0f;
  2044	    return { r.getCentreX() - radius, r.getBottom() - radius - 1.0f, radius * 2.0f, radius + 1.0f };
  2045	}
  2046	
  2047	void PlaylistComponent::showGainMenu (int index)
  2048	{
  2049	    const auto& song = engine.getSong();
  2050	    if (index < 0 || index >= (int) song.playlist.size())
  2051	        return;
  2052	    std::vector<int> targets;
  2053	    for (auto i : isSelected (index) ? selection : std::vector<int> { index })
  2054	        if (i >= 0 && i < (int) song.playlist.size() && song.playlist[(std::size_t) i].kind == ClipKind::audio)
  2055	            targets.push_back (i);
  2056	    const auto db = clipops::clipGainToDb (song.playlist[(std::size_t) index].gain);
  2057	    auto setDb = [this, targets] (double value) {
  2058	        const auto g = clipops::clipGainFromDb (value);
  2059	        engine.editSong ([targets, g] (Song& s) {
  2060	            for (auto i : targets)
  2061	                if (i < (int) s.playlist.size())
  2062	                    s.playlist[(std::size_t) i].gain = g;
  2063	        });
  2064	    };
  2065	
  2066	    // FL 2026's gain handle menu [SHOT round 166e]
  2067	    juce::PopupMenu menu;
  2068	    menu.addSectionHeader (tr ("Gain: {0}", { gainText (song.playlist[(std::size_t) index].gain) }));
  2069	    menu.addItem (tr ("Reset gain"), [setDb] { setDb (0.0); });
  2070	    menu.addItem (tr ("Normalize"), [this, targets] { normalizeClips (targets); });
  2071	    menu.addSeparator();
  2072	    menu.addItem (tr ("Copy value"), [db] { copiedGainDb = db; hasCopiedGain = true; });
  2073	    menu.addItem (tr ("Paste value"), hasCopiedGain, false, [setDb] { setDb (copiedGainDb); });
  2074	    menu.addItem (tr ("Type in value..."), [db, setDb] {
  2075	        auto* window = new juce::AlertWindow (tr ("Clip gain"), tr ("Decibels ({0} to {1})", { juce::String (clipops::clipGainMinDb, 1), "+" + juce::String (clipops::clipGainMaxDb, 0) }),
  2076	                                              juce::MessageBoxIconType::NoIcon);
  2077	        window->addTextEditor ("value", juce::String (db, 2));
  2078	        window->addButton (tr ("OK"), 1, juce::KeyPress (juce::KeyPress::returnKey));
  2079	        window->addButton (tr ("Cancel"), 0, juce::KeyPress (juce::KeyPress::escapeKey));
  2080	        window->enterModalState (true, juce::ModalCallbackFunction::create ([window, setDb] (int result) {
  2081	                                     if (result != 1)
  2082	                                         return;
  2083	                                     const auto typed = window->getTextEditorContents ("value").trim();
  2084	                                     setDb (typed.containsIgnoreCase ("inf") ? clipops::clipGainMinDb
  2085	                                                                             : typed.retainCharacters ("0123456789.,-+").replaceCharacter (',', '.').getDoubleValue());
  2086	                                 }),
  2087	                                 true);
  2088	    });
  2089	    menu.showMenuAsync (juce::PopupMenu::Options().withTargetComponent (this).withMousePosition());
  2090	}
  2091	
  2092	int PlaylistComponent::clipAt (int x, int y, bool& onRightEdge) const
  2093	{
  2094	    bool left = false;
  2095	    return clipAt (x, y, onRightEdge, left);
  2096	}
  2097	
  2098	int PlaylistComponent::clipAt (int x, int y, bool& onRightEdge, bool& onLeftEdge) const
  2099	{
  2100	    const auto& playlist = engine.getSong().playlist;
  2101	    const auto track = yToTrack (y);
  2102	    onRightEdge = onLeftEdge = false;
  2103	
  2104	    for (int i = (int) playlist.size() - 1; i >= 0; --i)
  2105	    {
  2106	        const auto& c = playlist[(std::size_t) i];
  2107	
  2108	        if (c.track != track)
  2109	            continue;
  2110	
  2111	        // the left edge only on clips wide enough to still be grabbed in the middle
  2112	        gesture::EdgeRules rules;
  2113	        rules.minimumForLeft = 24.0;
  2114	
  2115	        if (const auto zone = gesture::zoneAt (x, tickToX ((double) c.start), tickToX ((double) c.end()), rules); zone != gesture::Zone::outside)
  2116	        {
  2117	            onRightEdge = zone == gesture::Zone::rightEdge;
  2118	            onLeftEdge = zone == gesture::Zone::leftEdge;
  2119	            return i;
  2120	        }
  2121	    }
  2122	
  2123	    return -1;
  2124	}
  2125	
  2126	std::int64_t PlaylistComponent::snapLine (double tick) const
  2127	{
  2128	    // the grid line nearest to `tick` (FL's ruler click, round 169d); "Nearest" (clip edges) as snap() does
  2129	    const auto& option = grid::resolve (snapBox);
  2130	    if (option.kind == grid::Kind::nearest)
  2131	        return snap (tick, true);
  2132	    const auto step = gridTicks();
  2133	    if (step == (std::int64_t) ppq * juce::jmax (1, engine.getSong().beatsPerBar)) // (whole bars: time signatures)
  2134	    {
  2135	        const auto b = engine.getSong().barAt ((std::int64_t) std::max (0.0, std::floor (tick)));
  2136	        return tick - (double) b.tick >= (double) b.length * 0.5 ? b.tick + b.length : b.tick;
  2137	    }
  2138	    return noteedit::snapRound (tick, step);
  2139	}
  2140	
  2141	void PlaylistComponent::seekTo (int x, bool snapOn)
  2142	{
  2143	    const auto tick = juce::jmax (0.0, snapOn ? (double) snapLine (xToTick (x)) : xToTick (x));
  2144	
  2145	    if (engine.getSong().mode != PlayMode::song)
  2146	        engine.editSong ([] (Song& s) { s.mode = PlayMode::song; }, false);
  2147	
  2148	    // while playing, FL's "Quantize song jumps" makes the jump wait for the next beat / bar
  2149	    const auto waits = engine.getTransport().playing.load() && engine.quantizeSongJumps.load() > 0;
  2150	    engine.jumpTo (tick);
  2151	    if (! waits)
  2152	        playheadTick = tick;
  2153	    repaint();
  2154	}
  2155	
  2156	bool PlaylistComponent::isSelected (int index) const
  2157	{
  2158	    return std::binary_search (selection.begin(), selection.end(), index);
  2159	}
  2160	
  2161	void PlaylistComponent::setSelection (std::vector<int> indices)
  2162	{
  2163	    std::sort (indices.begin(), indices.end());
  2164	    indices.erase (std::unique (indices.begin(), indices.end()), indices.end());
  2165	    selection = std::move (indices);
  2166	    knownClipCount = engine.getSong().playlist.size();
  2167	    repaint();
  2168	}
  2169	
  2170	void PlaylistComponent::sortClips (std::vector<int> placed)
  2171	{
  2172	    auto tracked = selection;
  2173	    const auto selected = tracked.size();
  2174	    tracked.insert (tracked.end(), placed.begin(), placed.end());
  2175	    std::vector<int> conflicts;
  2176	    engine.editSong ([&tracked, &conflicts, selected] (Song& s) {
  2177	        noteedit::sortByStartTracking (s.playlist, tracked);
  2178	        // the placed audio clips on an audio track play through its mixer track (round 146a; issue #15: only those,
  2179	        // FL's way — a shared channel waits for the "Make clip unique?" answer)
  2180	        const std::vector<int> moved (tracked.begin() + (std::ptrdiff_t) selected, tracked.end());
  2181	        conflicts = trackmodes::audioTrackConflicts (s, moved);
  2182	        trackmodes::followAudioTracks (s, moved);
  2183	    }, false);
  2184	    placed.assign (tracked.begin() + (std::ptrdiff_t) selected, tracked.end());
  2185	    tracked.resize (selected);
  2186	    setSelection (tracked);
  2187	    if (! conflicts.empty())
  2188	        askToMakeClipsUnique (std::move (placed), std::move (conflicts));
  2189	}
  2190	
  2191	void PlaylistComponent::askToMakeClipsUnique (std::vector<int> placed, std::vector<int> channels)
  2192	{
  2193	    // FL 2026 asks once per channel; Yes = a copy of the channel for the placed clips, routed to the audio track
  2194	    if (channels.empty())
  2195	        return;
  2196	    const auto channel = channels.front();
  2197	    channels.erase (channels.begin());
  2198	    const auto& song = engine.getSong();
  2199	    if (channel < 0 || channel >= (int) song.channels.size())
  2200	        return askToMakeClipsUnique (std::move (placed), std::move (channels));
  2201	    // what the clips were when asked: an edit in between (the box is not modal) must not hit other clips
  2202	    std::vector<std::tuple<int, int, std::int64_t>> was;
  2203	    for (const auto i : placed)
  2204	        if (i >= 0 && i < (int) song.playlist.size())
  2205	            was.emplace_back (i, song.playlist[(std::size_t) i].track, song.playlist[(std::size_t) i].start);
  2206	    const auto name = juce::String::fromUTF8 (song.channels[(std::size_t) channel].name.c_str());
  2207	    auto options = juce::MessageBoxOptions()
  2208	                       .withIconType (juce::MessageBoxIconType::QuestionIcon)
  2209	                       .withTitle (tr ("Make clip unique?"))
  2210	                       .withMessage (tr ("The clip \"{0}\" is also used on another audio track.\n\nDo you want to make this instance unique and then route it to this audio track?",
  2211	                                         { name }))
  2212	                       .withButton (tr ("Yes"))
  2213	                       .withButton (tr ("No"))
  2214	                       .withAssociatedComponent (this);
  2215	    juce::AlertWindow::showAsync (options, [safe = juce::Component::SafePointer<PlaylistComponent> (this), placed, channels, channel, was] (int result) mutable {
  2216	        if (safe == nullptr)
  2217	            return;
  2218	        if (result == 1)
  2219	        {
  2220	            std::vector<int> still;
  2221	            const auto& now = safe->engine.getSong().playlist;
  2222	            for (const auto& [i, track, start] : was)
  2223	                if (i < (int) now.size() && now[(std::size_t) i].track == track && now[(std::size_t) i].start == start
  2224	                    && now[(std::size_t) i].channel == channel)
  2225	                    still.push_back (i);
  2226	            if (! still.empty())
  2227	                safe->engine.editSong ([&still] (Song& s) { trackmodes::followAudioTracks (s, still, true); });
  2228	        }
  2229	        safe->askToMakeClipsUnique (std::move (placed), std::move (channels));
  2230	    });
  2231	}
  2232	
  2233	Clip PlaylistComponent::makeBrushClip (int track, std::int64_t start) const
  2234	{
  2235	    // what is chosen in the Picker: the current pattern, an audio clip or an automation clip
  2236	    const auto& song = engine.getSong();
  2237	    Clip c;
  2238	    c.pattern = song.currentPattern;
  2239	    c.track = track;
  2240	    c.start = juce::jmax<std::int64_t> (0, start);
  2241	    c.length = song.patterns[(std::size_t) song.currentPattern].effectiveLength (song.beatsPerBar);
  2242	
  2243	    if (brush.kind == ClipKind::audio && brush.index >= 0 && brush.index < (int) song.channels.size()
  2244	        && song.channels[(std::size_t) brush.index].instrument == InstrumentKind::audioClip)
  2245	    {
  2246	        c.kind = ClipKind::audio;
  2247	        c.channel = brush.index;
  2248	        c.pattern = 0;
  2249	
  2250	        // audio length in ticks at the current BPM
  2251	        if (auto data = engine.getSampleLibrary().load (juce::String::fromUTF8 (song.channels[(std::size_t) brush.index].samplePath.c_str())))
  2252	            c.length = juce::jmax<std::int64_t> (ppq / 4, (std::int64_t) ((double) data->frames() / data->sampleRate * song.bpm / 60.0 * ppq));
  2253	    }
  2254	    else if (brush.kind == ClipKind::automation && brush.index >= 0 && brush.index < (int) song.automations.size())
  2255	    {
  2256	        c.kind = ClipKind::automation;
  2257	        c.automation = brush.index;
  2258	        c.pattern = 0;
  2259	        c.length = (std::int64_t) ppq * juce::jmax (1, song.beatsPerBar) * 4; // 4 bars
  2260	    }
  2261	
  2262	    return c;
  2263	}

  2265	void PlaylistComponent::setTimeSelection (std::int64_t from, std::int64_t to)
  2266	{
  2267	    engine.editSong ([from, to] (Song& s) {
  2268	        s.loopStart = from;
  2269	        s.loopEnd = to;
  2270	        s.mode = PlayMode::song;
  2271	    }, false);
  2272	
  2273	    setSelection (to > from ? noteedit::clipsInTime (engine.getSong().playlist, from, to) : std::vector<int> {});
  2274	}

  2276	void PlaylistComponent::setTimeSelectionForSnapshot (int fromBar, int toBar)
  2277	{
  2278	    const auto bar = (std::int64_t) ppq * juce::jmax (1, engine.getSong().beatsPerBar);
  2279	    setTimeSelection (fromBar * bar, toBar * bar);
  2280	}
