  2282	void PlaylistComponent::setViewForSnapshot (double pixelsPerBar, int firstBar, int firstTrack)
  2283	{
  2284	    const auto bar = (double) ppq * juce::jmax (1, engine.getSong().beatsPerBar);
  2285	    pixelsPerTick = pixelsPerBar / bar;
  2286	    scrollTicks = firstBar * bar;
  2287	    scrollY = juce::jmax (0, firstTrack) * defaultRowHeight();
  2288	    updateScrollBars();
  2289	    repaint();
  2290	}
  2291	
  2292	ui::Hint PlaylistComponent::hoverForSnapshot()
  2293	{
  2294	    const auto& playlist = engine.getSong().playlist;
  2295	    const auto grid = gridArea();
  2296	
  2297	    // the last clip fully in view (the floating Channel Rack tends to cover the first ones)
  2298	    for (int i = (int) playlist.size() - 1; i >= 0; --i)
  2299	        if (const auto r = clipBounds (i); grid.contains (r) && r.getWidth() >= 40 && playlist[(std::size_t) i].kind == ClipKind::pattern)
  2300	        {
  2301	            const auto at = juce::Point<int> (r.getRight() - 2, r.getCentreY() + 4);
  2302	            updateHover (at, {});
  2303	            return hintAt (at);
  2304	        }
  2305	
  2306	    return {};
  2307	}
  2308	
  2309	void PlaylistComponent::mouseDown (const juce::MouseEvent& e)
  2310	{
  2311	#if JUCE_ANDROID
  2312	    cancelTouchHold();
  2313	#endif
  2314	    downMods = e.mods;
  2315	    startedOnEmpty = erasedSomething = holdExpired = false;
  2316	    hover = {};
  2317	    gliding = false;
  2318	
  2319	    if (e.mods.isMiddleButtonDown() && e.x >= headerWidth) // middle-drag pans, in every tool
  2320	    {
  2321	        const auto onClips = gridArea().contains (e.getPosition());
  2322	        const auto onRuler = e.y >= rulerTop && e.y < headerHeight;
  2323	        if (onClips && flmods::ctrl (e.mods) && ! e.mods.isAltDown()) // FL: Ctrl+middle-drag = vertical zoom
  2324	        {
  2325	            drag = Drag::heightZoom;
  2326	            const auto track = yToTrack (e.y);
  2327	            heightZoomTrackY = trackToY (track); // the top of the track under the mouse stays where it is
  2328	            heightZoomStartHeight = juce::jmax (1, heightOf (track));
  2329	            trackZoomStart = trackZoom;
  2330	            heightZoomDrag.begin (e.y);
  2331	            e.source.enableUnboundedMouseMovement (true);
  2332	            return;
  2333	        }
  2334	        if (onRuler || (onClips && e.mods.isAltDown())) // FL: middle-drag on the ruler / Alt+middle-drag = time zoom
  2335	        {
  2336	            drag = Drag::timeZoom;
  2337	            const auto width = (double) juce::jmax (1, gridArea().getWidth());
  2338	            timeZoomDrag.begin (width / pixelsPerTick, scrollTicks + width * 0.5 / pixelsPerTick, e.x, e.y);
  2339	            e.source.enableUnboundedMouseMovement (true);
  2340	            return;
  2341	        }
  2342	
  2343	        drag = Drag::pan;
  2344	        panStartTicks = scrollTicks;
  2345	        panStartY = scrollY;
  2346	        setMouseCursor (editorCursor (gesture::Cursor::pan));
  2347	        return;
  2348	    }
  2349	
  2350	    // the vertical zoom handle: drag up = taller tracks
  2351	    if (trackZoomHandle().contains (e.getPosition()) && e.mods.isLeftButtonDown())
  2352	    {
  2353	        drag = Drag::trackZoom;
  2354	        trackZoomStart = trackZoom;
  2355	        setMouseCursor (juce::MouseCursor::UpDownResizeCursor);
  2356	        return;
  2357	    }
  2358	
  2359	    // track header divider: drag resizes the track above it (Shift: every track)
  2360	    if (const auto divider = dividerAt (e.getPosition()); divider >= 0 && e.mods.isLeftButtonDown())
  2361	    {
  2362	        drag = Drag::trackResize;
  2363	        resizeTrack = divider;
  2364	        resizeAll = e.mods.isShiftDown();
  2365	        resizeStartHeight = heightOf (divider);
  2366	        setMouseCursor (juce::MouseCursor::UpDownResizeCursor);
  2367	        return;
  2368	    }
  2369	
  2370	    // track header: the LED mutes (Ctrl+click or right-click on it: solo), a group header's arrow folds the
  2371	    // group, a click elsewhere selects the track; right-click elsewhere: track menu
  2372	    if (e.x < headerWidth && e.y >= headerHeight && e.y < gridArea().getBottom())
  2373	    {
  2374	        const auto track = yToTrack (e.y);
  2375	
  2376	        if (noteedit::isFoldedAway (engine.getSong(), track)) // a thin row of a folded group: only its menu
  2377	        {
  2378	            if (flmods::popup (e.mods))
  2379	                showTrackMenu (track);
  2380	            return;
  2381	        }
  2382	
  2383	        const auto onLed = trackLed (track).expanded (3).contains (e.getPosition());
  2384	        const auto onArrow = trackArrow (track).contains (e.getPosition());
  2385	
  2386	        // an audio track's buttons (FL 2026): [LMB] on the record button arms / disarms its mixer track, on the input
  2387	        // button opens the input source menu; a right-click on them does nothing in FL (no track menu either)
  2388	        const auto onArm = trackArm (track).expanded (2).contains (e.getPosition());
  2389	        const auto onInput = trackInput (track).expanded (2).contains (e.getPosition());
  2390	        if (onArm || onInput)
  2391	        {
  2392	            const auto insert = trackmodes::armableInsert (engine.getSong(), track);
  2393	            if (! flmods::popup (e.mods) && insert > 0)
  2394	            {
  2395	                if (onArm)
  2396	                    mixerinput::toggleArm (engine, insert, this);
  2397	                else
  2398	                    mixerinput::showMenu (engine, insert, this);
  2399	            }
  2400	            repaint (0, headerHeight, headerWidth, getHeight() - headerHeight);
  2401	            return;
  2402	        }
  2403	
  2404	        if (onLed && (flmods::popup (e.mods) || flmods::ctrl (e.mods)))
  2405	            engine.editSong ([track, count = numTracks()] (Song& s) { noteedit::soloTrack (s, track, count); });
  2406	        else if (flmods::popup (e.mods))
  2407	            showTrackMenu (track);
  2408	        else if (onLed)
  2409	            editTrack (track, [] (PlaylistTrack& t) { t.muted = ! t.muted; });
  2410	        else if (onArrow)
  2411	            engine.editSong ([track] (Song& s) {
  2412	                const auto folded = track < (int) s.playlistTracks.size() && s.playlistTracks[(std::size_t) track].collapsed;
  2413	                noteedit::setGroupCollapsed (s, track, ! folded);
  2414	            });
  2415	        else if (! noteedit::isFoldedAway (engine.getSong(), track))
  2416	        {
  2417	            // Ctrl+click adds / removes a track, Shift+click selects the tracks from the last clicked one (round 165m:
  2418	            // FL's track menu settings, e.g. Performance settings, apply to every selected track)
  2419	            if (flmods::ctrl (e.mods))
  2420	            {
  2421	                if (selectedTracks.empty() && currentTrack >= 0)
  2422	                    selectedTracks.push_back (currentTrack);
  2423	                if (const auto it = std::find (selectedTracks.begin(), selectedTracks.end(), track); it != selectedTracks.end())
  2424	                    selectedTracks.erase (it);
  2425	                else
  2426	                    selectedTracks.push_back (track);
  2427	            }
  2428	            else if (e.mods.isShiftDown() && currentTrack >= 0)
  2429	            {
  2430	                selectedTracks.clear();
  2431	                for (int t = juce::jmin (currentTrack, track); t <= juce::jmax (currentTrack, track); ++t)
  2432	                    selectedTracks.push_back (t);
  2433	            }
  2434	            else
  2435	                selectedTracks = { track };
  2436	            currentTrack = track;
  2437	            repaint (0, headerHeight, headerWidth, getHeight() - headerHeight);
  2438	        }
  2439	        return;
  2440	    }
  2441	
  2442	    // ruler, FL 2026's rules (core/RulerGestures.h, docs/analise/REA_rodada168t.md): click = position; Ctrl / Shift /
  2443	    // double-click drag, the Select tool or a right-drag = time selection; Ctrl+right-drag or the Zoom tool = zoom to
  2444	    // a range; Shift+drag moves the selection. On a Mac, FL's Ctrl is Cmd (or Control, which is not a right click here)
  2445	    if (e.y >= rulerTop && e.y < headerHeight && e.x >= headerWidth)
  2446	    {
  2447	        const auto fl = flmods::of (e.mods);
  2448	        const auto& song = engine.getSong();
  2449	        const auto snapOn = ! e.mods.isAltDown();
  2450	        const auto tick = juce::jmax<std::int64_t> (0, snap (xToTick (e.x), snapOn));
  2451	        const auto markerHit = markerTagAt (e.x, e.y);
  2452	
  2453	        ruler::Press press;
  2454	        press.mods = fl;
  2455	        press.clicks = e.getNumberOfClicks();
  2456	        press.tool = tools.getTool();
  2457	        press.onMarkerTag = markerHit >= 0;
  2458	        press.hasSelection = song.hasLoop();
  2459	        press.insideSelection = song.hasLoop() && xToTick (e.x) >= (double) song.loopStart && xToTick (e.x) < (double) song.loopEnd;
  2460	
  2461	        loopAnchor = tick;
  2462	        rulerDoubleDrag = false;
  2463	        rulerMenuOnClick = false;
  2464	
  2465	        switch (ruler::press (press))
  2466	        {
  2467	            case ruler::Action::markerMenu:
  2468	                showRulerMenu (e.x);
  2469	                return;
  2470	
  2471	            case ruler::Action::zoomRange:
  2472	                drag = Drag::zoomRange;
  2473	                rubberBand = {};
  2474	                return;
  2475	
  2476	            case ruler::Action::adjust:
  2477	                drag = Drag::loop;
  2478	                loopAnchor = ruler::adjustAnchor (song.loopStart, song.loopEnd, tick);
  2479	                setTimeSelection (juce::jmin (loopAnchor, tick), juce::jmax (loopAnchor, tick));
  2480	                return;
  2481	
  2482	            case ruler::Action::select:
  2483	                drag = Drag::loop;
  2484	                // FL 2026 [SHOT round 166e]: a double click held and dragged draws a time selection, with the song
  2485	                // position at its start; released in place it clears it (as a Ctrl+click does)
  2486	                rulerDoubleDrag = press.clicks >= 2 && ! fl.right;
  2487	                // LYNX: a right click released in place with no selection opens the ruler menu (FL: does nothing)
  2488	                rulerMenuOnClick = fl.right;
  2489	                return;
  2490	
  2491	            case ruler::Action::markerSpan:
  2492	            {
  2493	                const auto from = song.markers()[(std::size_t) markerHit].tick;
  2494	                auto to = juce::jmax (from, song.songLength());
  2495	                for (const auto& m : song.markers())
  2496	                    if (m.tick > from)
  2497	                        to = juce::jmin (to, m.tick);
  2498	                if (to > from)
  2499	                    setTimeSelection (from, to);
  2500	                return;
  2501	            }
  2502	
  2503	            case ruler::Action::moveSelection:
  2504	                drag = Drag::timeShift;
  2505	                timeMoveFrom = song.loopStart;
  2506	                timeMoveTo = song.loopEnd;
  2507	                timeMoveAnchor = tick;
  2508	                return;
  2509	
  2510	            case ruler::Action::moveWithClips:
  2511	                drag = Drag::timeMove;
  2512	                timeMoveOriginals = song.playlist;
  2513	                timeMoveFrom = song.loopStart;
  2514	                timeMoveTo = song.loopEnd;
  2515	                timeMoveAnchor = tick;
  2516	                timeMoveCopy = false;
  2517	                return;
  2518	
  2519	            case ruler::Action::marker:
  2520	            {
  2521	                // a click on a marker's tag goes to the marker (LYNX), quantized while playing like any song jump
  2522	                const auto markerTick = song.markers()[(std::size_t) markerHit].tick;
  2523	                const auto waits = engine.getTransport().playing.load() && engine.quantizeSongJumps.load() > 0;
  2524	                engine.jumpToMarker (markerHit);
  2525	                if (! waits)
  2526	                    playheadTick = (double) markerTick;
  2527	                repaint();
  2528	                return;
  2529	            }
  2530	
  2531	            case ruler::Action::seek:
  2532	                break;
  2533	        }
  2534	
  2535	        drag = Drag::seek;
  2536	        seekTo (e.x, snapOn);
  2537	        EditorGlobals::get().seekMark = tick;
  2538	        return;
  2539	    }
  2540	
  2541	    if (! gridArea().contains (e.getPosition()))
  2542	        return;
  2543	
  2544	    bool rightEdge = false, leftEdge = false;
  2545	    const auto hit = clipAt (e.x, e.y, rightEdge, leftEdge);
  2546	    const auto tool = tools.getTool();
  2547	    if (e.source.isTouch() && ! e.mods.isRightButtonDown() && ! flmods::ctrl (e.mods)) {
  2548	        const auto nowMs = juce::Time::getMillisecondCounter();
  2549	        if (hit >= 0 && hit == lastTouchTapClip && (nowMs - lastTouchTapMs) < 420 && e.getPosition().getDistanceFrom (lastTouchTapPos) < 28) {
  2550	            lastTouchTapMs = 0; lastTouchTapClip = -1; drag = Drag::none; openClipSource (hit, e.mods.isAltDown()); return;
  2551	        }
  2552	        if (hit >= 0) { lastTouchTapMs = nowMs; lastTouchTapPos = e.getPosition(); lastTouchTapClip = hit; }
  2553	        else { lastTouchTapMs = 0; lastTouchTapClip = -1; }
  2554	    }
  2555	
  2556	    // Performance mode (round 165m): while the song plays, a click on a clip of the performance zone launches it (the
  2557	    // button's release is the release of the Hold modes) and a right-click stops its track; Ctrl / Alt edit as usual
  2558	    if (const auto& song = engine.getSong(); hit >= 0 && song.mode == PlayMode::song && song.inPerformanceZone (song.playlist[(std::size_t) hit])
  2559	                                            && engine.getTransport().playing.load() && ! flmods::ctrl (e.mods) && ! e.mods.isAltDown())
  2560	    {
  2561	        const auto& clip = song.playlist[(std::size_t) hit];
  2562	        perf::Command c;
  2563	        c.track = clip.track;
  2564	        c.clipStart = clip.start;
  2565	        c.type = flmods::popup (e.mods) ? perf::Command::Type::stopTrack : perf::Command::Type::press;
  2566	        engine.performanceCommand (c);
  2567	        if (c.type == perf::Command::Type::press)
  2568	        {
  2569	            drag = Drag::perfLaunch;
  2570	            perfTrack = clip.track;
  2571	            perfClip = clip.start;
  2572	        }
  2573	        return;
  2574	    }
  2575	
  2576	    // Ctrl+right-click on empty space: zoom to show everything; again: back to the previous view
  2577	    if (e.mods.isRightButtonDown() && flmods::ctrl (e.mods) && hit < 0)
  2578	    {
  2579	        if (zoomBeforeAll > 0.0)
  2580	        {
  2581	            pixelsPerTick = zoomBeforeAll;
  2582	            scrollTicks = scrollBeforeAll;
  2583	            zoomBeforeAll = 0.0;
  2584	            updateScrollBars();
  2585	            repaint();
  2586	        }
  2587	        else
  2588	        {
  2589	            zoomBeforeAll = pixelsPerTick;
  2590	            scrollBeforeAll = scrollTicks;
  2591	            zoomAll();
  2592	        }
  2593	        return;
  2594	    }
  2595	
  2596	    // the menu button in a clip's title strip
  2597	    if (hit >= 0 && e.mods.isLeftButtonDown() && ! flmods::ctrl (e.mods) && clipMenuButton (hit).contains (e.getPosition()))
  2598	    {
  2599	        showClipMenu (hit);
  2600	        return;
  2601	    }
  2602	
  2603	    // Zoom tool: right-click zooms out around the point (a click zooms in, a drag zooms to the range)
  2604	    if (tool == EditTool::zoom && e.mods.isRightButtonDown() && ! e.mods.isAltDown())
  2605	    {
  2606	        zoomOut (e.x);
  2607	        return;
  2608	    }
  2609	
  2610	    // Alt+right-click on a clip: hear it while the button is held
  2611	    if (e.mods.isRightButtonDown() && e.mods.isAltDown() && hit >= 0)
  2612	    {
  2613	        startPreview (hit, e.x);
  2614	        return;
  2615	    }
  2616	
  2617	    if (e.mods.isRightButtonDown() && hit >= 0 && gainHandle (hit).expanded (2.0f).contains (e.position))
  2618	    {
  2619	        showGainMenu (hit);
  2620	        return;
  2621	    }
  2622	
  2623	    if (e.mods.isRightButtonDown())
  2624	    {
  2625	        // round 165, FL's in-Playlist automation editing: right-click on a point = its menu, on a tension handle =
  2626	        // straight segment, anywhere else in the curve's area = a new point there, dragged while the button is held
  2627	        // (Shift: on the curve, keeping the level); the title strip (or a clip too small to edit) still deletes
  2628	        if (const auto point = automationPointAt (hit, e.getPosition()); point >= 0)
  2629	        {
  2630	            showAutomationPointMenu (hit, point);
  2631	            return;
  2632	        }
  2633	        if (const auto handle = automationHandleAt (hit, e.getPosition()); handle >= 0)
  2634	        {
  2635	            const auto automation = engine.getSong().playlist[(std::size_t) hit].automation;
  2636	            engine.editSong ([automation, handle] (Song& s) {
  2637	                auto& points = s.automations[(std::size_t) automation].points;
  2638	                if (handle < (int) points.size())
  2639	                    points[(std::size_t) handle].tension = 0.0f;
  2640	            });
  2641	            return;
  2642	        }
  2643	        if (automationEditable (hit) && automationBody (engine.getSong().playlist[(std::size_t) hit]).contains (e.position))
  2644	        {
  2645	            const auto& c = engine.getSong().playlist[(std::size_t) hit];
  2646	            const auto& automation = engine.getSong().automations[(std::size_t) c.automation];
  2647	            AutomationPoint p;
  2648	            p.tick = juce::jlimit<std::int64_t> (c.offset, c.offset + c.length, snap (xToTick (e.x), ! e.mods.isAltDown()) - c.start + c.offset);
  2649	            const auto body = automationBody (c);
  2650	            p.value = e.mods.isShiftDown() ? automation.valueAt ((double) p.tick)
  2651	                                           : juce::jlimit (0.0f, 1.0f, (body.getBottom() - (float) e.y) / juce::jmax (1.0f, body.getHeight()));
  2652	            int added = -1;
  2653	            engine.editSong ([a = c.automation, p, &added] (Song& s) {
  2654	                auto& points = s.automations[(std::size_t) a].points;
  2655	                const auto at = std::upper_bound (points.begin(), points.end(), p.tick, [] (std::int64_t t, const AutomationPoint& q) { return t < q.tick; });
  2656	                added = (int) (at - points.begin());
  2657	                points.insert (at, p);
  2658	            });
  2659	            setSelection ({});
  2660	            drag = Drag::autoPoint;
  2661	            autoClip = hit;
  2662	            autoPoint = added;
  2663	            return;
  2664	        }
  2665	
  2666	        // on the clip menu icon (top left): the clip menu; anywhere else on a clip: delete, as in FL
  2667	        // (press and hold on empty space: Knife)
  2668	        if (hit >= 0 && clipMenuButton (hit).contains (e.getPosition()))
  2669	        {
  2670	            showClipMenu (hit);
  2671	            return;
  2672	        }
  2673	
  2674	        // FL 2026 [SHOT, round 165z-b]: a double right-click is the Mute tool while the button stays down. The first
  2675	        // click already deleted the clip under it and FL leaves it deleted; the sweep then mutes / unmutes every clip
  2676	        // it crosses, the first one deciding which (issue #11)
  2677	        const auto now = juce::Time::getMillisecondCounter();
  2678	        const auto doubleRight = gesture::isDoubleClick (lastRightClickTime, now, lastRightClickPos.getDistanceFrom (e.getPosition()));
  2679	        lastRightClickTime = doubleRight ? 0 : now;
  2680	        lastRightClickPos = e.getPosition();
  2681	        if (doubleRight)
  2682	        {
  2683	            drag = Drag::muting;
  2684	            touchedClips.clear();
  2685	            muteSweep = {};
  2686	            bool r = false, l = false;
  2687	            if (const auto clip = clipAt (e.x, e.y, r, l); clip >= 0)
  2688	            {
  2689	                touchedClips.push_back (clip);
  2690	                const auto m = muteSweep.touch (engine.getSong().playlist[(std::size_t) clip].muted);
  2691	                engine.editSong ([clip, m] (Song& s) { s.playlist[(std::size_t) clip].muted = m; });
  2692	            }
  2693	            return;
  2694	        }
  2695	
  2696	        drag = Drag::erase;
  2697	        startedOnEmpty = hit < 0;
  2698	        setSelection ({}); // indices change while deleting
  2699	
  2700	        if (hit >= 0)
  2701	        {
  2702	            erasedSomething = true;
  2703	            engine.editSong ([hit] (Song& s) { s.playlist.erase (s.playlist.begin() + hit); });
  2704	        }
  2705	        return;
  2706	    }
  2707	
  2708	    // every tool: Ctrl+click on a clip adds/removes it from the selection; Ctrl+drag selects (Shift adds)
  2709	    if (hit >= 0 && flmods::ctrl (e.mods))
  2710	    {
  2711	        auto s = selection;
  2712	
  2713	        if (isSelected (hit))
  2714	            s.erase (std::find (s.begin(), s.end(), hit));
  2715	        else
  2716	            s.push_back (hit);
  2717	
  2718	        setSelection (s);
  2719	        return;
  2720	    }
  2721	
  2722	    if ((hit < 0 && flmods::ctrl (e.mods)) || tool == EditTool::lasso
  2723	        || (hit < 0 && e.mods.isShiftDown() && tool != EditTool::brush))
  2724	    {
  2725	        drag = Drag::select;
  2726	        dragClip = hit; // a Lasso click without dragging selects this clip
  2727	        selectionBefore = e.mods.isShiftDown() ? selection : std::vector<int> {};
  2728	        rubberBand = {};
  2729	        setSelection (selectionBefore);
  2730	        return;
  2731	    }
  2732	
  2733	    const auto snapOn = ! e.mods.isAltDown();
  2734	
  2735	    auto grabClips = [&] (Drag kind) {
  2736	        if (! isSelected (hit))
  2737	            setSelection ({});
  2738	
  2739	        const auto& playlist = engine.getSong().playlist;
  2740	        dragClip = hit;
  2741	        dragIndices = selection.empty() ? std::vector<int> { hit } : selection;
  2742	        dragOriginals.clear();
  2743	
  2744	        for (auto i : dragIndices)
  2745	            dragOriginals.push_back (playlist[(std::size_t) i]);
  2746	
  2747	        drag = kind;
  2748	        grabOffset = playlist[(std::size_t) hit].start - snap (xToTick (e.x), snapOn);
  2749	#if JUCE_ANDROID
  2750	        // A stationary touch maps to desktop right-click on a clip. Movement beyond the touch slop cancels
  2751	        // the hold and continues as an ordinary clip drag, including when the press began in the title strip.
  2752	        if (e.source.isTouch() && kind == Drag::move)
  2753	        {
  2754	            touchHoldPending = true;
  2755	            touchHoldClip = hit;
  2756	            touchHoldSource = e.source.getIndex();
  2757	            touchHoldScreenStart = e.source.getScreenPosition();
  2758	            touchHoldOpensMenu = e.y < clipBounds (hit).getY() + (int) clipTitleHeight;
  2759	            startTimer (500);
  2760	        }
  2761	#endif
  2762	    };
  2763	
  2764	    switch (tool)
  2765	    {
  2766	        case EditTool::eraser:
  2767	            drag = Drag::erase;
  2768	            setSelection ({});
  2769	            if (hit >= 0)
  2770	                engine.editSong ([hit] (Song& s) { s.playlist.erase (s.playlist.begin() + hit); });
  2771	            return;
  2772	
  2773	        case EditTool::mute:
  2774	            drag = Drag::muting;
  2775	            touchedClips.clear();
  2776	            muteSweep = {};
  2777	            if (hit >= 0)
  2778	            {
  2779	                touchedClips.push_back (hit);
  2780	                const auto m = muteSweep.touch (engine.getSong().playlist[(std::size_t) hit].muted);
  2781	                engine.editSong ([hit, m] (Song& s) { s.playlist[(std::size_t) hit].muted = m; });
  2782	            }
  2783	            else
  2784	            {
  2785	                muteSweep.touch (false); // the Mute tool started on empty space mutes
  2786	            }
  2787	            return;
  2788	
  2789	        case EditTool::knife:
  2790	            drag = Drag::knife;
  2791	            knifeFrom = knifeTo = e.getPosition();
  2792	            repaint();
  2793	            return;
  2794	
  2795	        case EditTool::zoom:
  2796	            drag = Drag::zoomRange;
  2797	            rubberBand = {};
  2798	            return;
  2799	
  2800	        case EditTool::listen:
  2801	            drag = Drag::listen;
  2802	            seekTo (e.x, ! e.mods.isAltDown());
  2803	            engine.play();
  2804	            return;
  2805	
  2806	        case EditTool::slip:
  2807	            if (hit >= 0)
  2808	                grabClips (Drag::slip);
  2809	            return;
  2810	
  2811	        case EditTool::pencil:
  2812	        case EditTool::brush:
  2813	        case EditTool::drumPaint:
  2814	        case EditTool::lasso:
  2815	            break;
  2816	    }
  2817	
  2818	    // automation clip: drag a point, or click on the curve to add one (Pencil and Brush); round 165 (FL): Alt+click
  2819	    // deletes a point, the hollow handle between two points bends the segment
  2820	    if (hit >= 0 && ! e.mods.isShiftDown() && (tool == EditTool::pencil || tool == EditTool::brush))
  2821	    {
  2822	        auto point = automationPointAt (hit, e.getPosition());
  2823	
  2824	        if (const auto handle = point < 0 ? automationHandleAt (hit, e.getPosition()) : -1; handle >= 0)
  2825	        {
  2826	            const auto& song = engine.getSong();
  2827	            const auto& points = song.automations[(std::size_t) song.playlist[(std::size_t) hit].automation].points;
  2828	            setSelection ({});
  2829	            drag = Drag::autoTension;
  2830	            autoClip = hit;
  2831	            autoPoint = handle;
  2832	            tensionStart = points[(std::size_t) handle].tension;
  2833	            // a positive tension lowers a rising segment's middle and raises a falling one's: the handle follows the mouse
  2834	            tensionSign = points[(std::size_t) handle + 1].value >= points[(std::size_t) handle].value ? -1.0f : 1.0f;
  2835	            return;
  2836	        }
  2837	
  2838	        if (point < 0 && isOnAutomationCurve (hit, e.getPosition()))
  2839	        {
  2840	            const auto& c = engine.getSong().playlist[(std::size_t) hit];
  2841	            AutomationPoint p;
  2842	            p.tick = juce::jmax<std::int64_t> (0, snap (xToTick (e.x), snapOn) - c.start + c.offset);
  2843	            const auto body = automationBody (c);
  2844	            p.value = juce::jlimit (0.0f, 1.0f, (body.getBottom() - (float) e.y) / juce::jmax (1.0f, body.getHeight()));
  2845	            engine.editSong ([a = c.automation, p] (Song& s) {
  2846	                auto& points = s.automations[(std::size_t) a].points;
  2847	                points.push_back (p);
  2848	                std::stable_sort (points.begin(), points.end(), [] (auto& l, auto& r) { return l.tick < r.tick; });
  2849	            });
  2850	            point = automationPointAt (hit, e.getPosition());
  2851	        }
  2852	
  2853	        if (point >= 0)
  2854	        {
  2855	            setSelection ({});
  2856	            drag = Drag::autoPoint;
  2857	            autoClip = hit;
  2858	            autoPoint = point;
  2859	            return;
  2860	        }
  2861	    }
  2862	
  2863	    // audio clip fade handles (drawn at the top corners of the body)
  2864	    if (hit >= 0)
  2865	        if (const auto fade = fadeHandleAt (hit, e.getPosition()); fade != 0)
  2866	        {
  2867	            setSelection ({});
  2868	            dragClip = hit;
  2869	            dragIndices = { hit };
  2870	            drag = fade == 1 ? Drag::fadeIn : Drag::fadeOut;
  2871	            return;
  2872	        }
  2873	
  2874	    // FL 2026's gain handle (round 166e): drag up / down; on a selected clip, every selected audio clip follows
  2875	    if (hit >= 0 && gainHandle (hit).expanded (2.0f).contains (e.position))
  2876	    {
  2877	        const auto& song = engine.getSong();
  2878	        dragClip = hit;
  2879	        dragIndices.clear();
  2880	        gainStartDb.clear();
  2881	        for (auto i : isSelected (hit) ? selection : std::vector<int> { hit })
  2882	            if (i >= 0 && i < (int) song.playlist.size() && song.playlist[(std::size_t) i].kind == ClipKind::audio)
  2883	            {
  2884	                dragIndices.push_back (i);
  2885	                gainStartDb.push_back (clipops::clipGainToDb (song.playlist[(std::size_t) i].gain));
  2886	            }
  2887	        gainStartY = e.y;
  2888	        drag = Drag::clipGain;
  2889	        postDragHint();
  2890	        return;
  2891	    }
  2892	
  2893	    // Alt+Shift+drag inside a clip: slip its content
  2894	    if (hit >= 0 && e.mods.isAltDown() && e.mods.isShiftDown() && ! rightEdge && ! leftEdge)
  2895	    {
  2896	        grabClips (Drag::slip);
  2897	        return;
  2898	    }
  2899	
  2900	    // Shift+drag on a clip body drags a copy (on an edge, Shift trims like a plain edge drag)
  2901	    if (hit >= 0 && e.mods.isShiftDown() && ! rightEdge && ! leftEdge)
  2902	    {
  2903	        const auto sources = isSelected (hit) ? selection : std::vector<int> { hit };
  2904	        const auto grabbedAt = (std::size_t) (std::find (sources.begin(), sources.end(), hit) - sources.begin());
  2905	        std::vector<Clip> copies;
  2906	        std::vector<int> added;
  2907	
  2908	        for (auto i : sources)
  2909	            copies.push_back (engine.getSong().playlist[(std::size_t) i]);
  2910	
  2911	        // Shift+V held: the copies get their own pattern / automation (FL's "make unique" while cloning)
  2912	        const auto unique = juce::KeyPress::isKeyCurrentlyDown ('V') || juce::KeyPress::isKeyCurrentlyDown ('v');
  2913	
  2914	        engine.editSong ([&] (Song& s) {
  2915	            for (const auto& c : copies)
  2916	            {
  2917	                added.push_back ((int) s.playlist.size());
  2918	                s.playlist.push_back (c);
  2919	            }
  2920	
  2921	            if (unique)
  2922	                polish::makeClipsUnique (s, added);
  2923	        });
  2924	
  2925	        if (unique)
  2926	            for (std::size_t j = 0; j < added.size(); ++j)
  2927	                copies[j] = engine.getSong().playlist[(std::size_t) added[j]];
  2928	        setSelection (added);
  2929	        dragIndices = added;
  2930	        dragOriginals = copies;
  2931	        dragClip = added[grabbedAt];
  2932	        drag = Drag::move;
  2933	        grabOffset = copies[grabbedAt].start - snap (xToTick (e.x), snapOn);
  2934	        return;
  2935	    }
  2936	
  2937	    if (hit >= 0)
  2938	    {
  2939	        grabClips (rightEdge ? Drag::resize : leftEdge ? Drag::trimLeft : Drag::move);
  2940	        stretchOriginals.clear();
  2941	        if (EditorGlobals::get().stretchOnResize && (rightEdge || leftEdge))
  2942	            stretchOriginals = engine.getSong().playlist;
  2943	
  2944	        if (drag == Drag::resize && ripple)
  2945	        {
  2946	            setSelection ({}); // a ripple resize works on the grabbed clip only
  2947	            dragIndices = { hit };
  2948	            dragOriginals = { engine.getSong().playlist[(std::size_t) hit] };
  2949	            rippleOriginals = engine.getSong().playlist;
  2950	        }
  2951	
  2952	        const auto& c = engine.getSong().playlist[(std::size_t) hit];
  2953	
  2954	        if (c.kind == ClipKind::pattern && c.pattern != engine.getSong().currentPattern)
  2955	            engine.editSong ([p = c.pattern] (Song& s) { s.currentPattern = p; }, false);
  2956	        return;
  2957	    }
  2958	
  2959	    // paints what is chosen in the Picker (the current pattern, an audio clip or an automation clip)
  2960	    setSelection ({});
  2961	    // an instrument track places its own pattern (FL: named after the channel, made on the first click; round 151)
  2962	    if (const auto track = yToTrack (e.y); brush.kind == ClipKind::pattern && track >= 0 && track < (int) engine.getSong().playlistTracks.size()
  2963	                                           && engine.getSong().playlistTracks[(std::size_t) track].mode == PlaylistTrackMode::instrument)
  2964	    {
  2965	        engine.editSong ([track] (Song& s) {
  2966	            if (const auto p = trackmodes::instrumentTrackPattern (s, track); p >= 0)
  2967	                s.currentPattern = p;
  2968	        });
  2969	        engine.setSelectedChannel (engine.getSong().playlistTracks[(std::size_t) track].channel); // its piano roll is the channel's
  2970	    }
  2971	
  2972	    auto c = makeBrushClip (yToTrack (e.y), snap (xToTick (e.x), snapOn));
  2973	
  2974	    if (c.kind == ClipKind::pattern)
  2975	        brush = {};
  2976	    // a track locked to content places its own content (FL: the paint loop of FUN_00e5cd20 → FUN_01220fe0, round 168f)
  2977	    pltracks::applyLockToContent (engine.getSong(), c);
  2978	
  2979	    engine.editSong ([c] (Song& s) {
  2980	        s.playlist.push_back (c);
  2981	        s.mode = PlayMode::song; // someone arranging wants to hear the song
  2982	    });
  2983	    dragClip = (int) engine.getSong().playlist.size() - 1;
  2984	    dragIndices = { dragClip };
  2985	    dragOriginals = { c };
  2986	    grabOffset = 0;
  2987	
  2988	    if (tool == EditTool::brush)
  2989	    {
  2990	        drag = Drag::paint;
  2991	        paintClip = c;
  2992	        nextPaintTick = c.end();
  2993	        return;
  2994	    }
  2995	
  2996	    drag = Drag::move;
  2997	}
  2998	
  2999	void PlaylistComponent::mouseDrag (const juce::MouseEvent& e)
  3000	{
  3001	#if JUCE_ANDROID
  3002	    if (touchHoldPending && e.getDistanceFromDragStart() > 8.0f)
  3003	        cancelTouchHold();
  3004	#endif
  3005	    if (drag == Drag::perfLaunch)
  3006	        return; // (a launch: nothing moves)
  3007	
  3008	    if (drag == Drag::trackZoom)
  3009	    {
  3010	        setTrackZoom (trackZoomStart * std::pow (2.0, -e.getDistanceFromDragStartY() / 80.0), headerHeight);
  3011	        return;
  3012	    }
  3013	
  3014	    if (drag == Drag::heightZoom)
  3015	    {
  3016	        heightZoomDrag.move (e.y, flmods::ctrl (e.mods));
  3017	        const auto height = heightZoomDrag.height (heightZoomStartHeight, 8, 640);
  3018	        setTrackZoom (trackZoomStart * (double) height / (double) heightZoomStartHeight, heightZoomTrackY);
  3019	        return;
  3020	    }
  3021	
  3022	    if (drag == Drag::timeZoom)
  3023	    {
  3024	        const auto width = (double) juce::jmax (1, gridArea().getWidth());
  3025	        timeZoomDrag.move (e.x, e.y, flmods::ctrl (e.mods));
  3026	        pixelsPerTick = juce::jlimit (4.0 / (4 * ppq), 800.0 / (4 * ppq), width / timeZoomDrag.span);
  3027	        scrollTicks = juce::jmax (0.0, timeZoomDrag.centre - width * 0.5 / pixelsPerTick);
  3028	        zoomBeforeAll = 0.0;
  3029	        updateScrollBars();
  3030	        repaint();
  3031	        ui::postHint ({ tr ("Zooming: drag up = closer, sideways = scroll  [Ctrl] zoom only"), {}, false, false });
  3032	        return;
  3033	    }
  3034	
  3035	    if (drag == Drag::trackResize)
  3036	    {
  3037	        // the size shown, kept at 100% zoom (the vertical zoom scales every track)
  3038	        const auto height = polish::clampTrackHeight ((int) std::lround ((resizeStartHeight + e.getDistanceFromDragStartY()) / trackZoom));
  3039	        std::vector<int> tracks;
  3040	        if (resizeAll)
  3041	            for (int t = 0; t < numTracks(); ++t)
  3042	                tracks.push_back (t);
  3043	        else
  3044	            tracks.push_back (resizeTrack);
  3045	
  3046	        if (heightOf (resizeTrack) != scaledHeight (height))
  3047	            setTrackHeights (tracks, height);
  3048	
  3049	        ui::postHint ({ resizeAll ? tr ("Resizing every track") : tr ("Resizing the track"), juce::String (scaledHeight (height)) + " px", false, false });
  3050	        return;
  3051	    }
  3052	
  3053	    if (drag == Drag::preview)
  3054	        return;
  3055	
  3056	    const auto snapOn = ! e.mods.isAltDown();
  3057	    // after the step below, the hint bar says what the gesture is doing
  3058	    const struct PostAfter
  3059	    {
  3060	        PlaylistComponent& list;
  3061	        ~PostAfter() { list.postDragHint(); }
  3062	    } postAfter { *this };
  3063	
  3064	    switch (drag)
  3065	    {
  3066	        case Drag::none:
  3067	        case Drag::trackResize:
  3068	        case Drag::trackZoom:
  3069	        case Drag::heightZoom:
  3070	        case Drag::timeZoom:
  3071	        case Drag::preview:
  3072	            return;
  3073	
  3074	        case Drag::pan:
  3075	            scrollTicks = juce::jmax (0.0, panStartTicks - e.getDistanceFromDragStartX() / pixelsPerTick);
  3076	            scrollY = juce::jmax (0, panStartY - e.getDistanceFromDragStartY());
  3077	            updateScrollBars();
  3078	            repaint();
  3079	            return;
  3080	
  3081	        case Drag::autoPoint:
  3082	        {
  3083	            const auto& song = engine.getSong();
  3084	            if (autoClip < 0 || autoClip >= (int) song.playlist.size())
  3085	                return;
  3086	            const auto& c = song.playlist[(std::size_t) autoClip];
  3087	            if (c.automation < 0 || c.automation >= (int) song.automations.size())
  3088	                return;
  3089	            const auto& points = song.automations[(std::size_t) c.automation].points;
  3090	            if (autoPoint < 0 || autoPoint >= (int) points.size())
  3091	                return;
  3092	
  3093	            // the point stays between its neighbours; Shift pressed after the click keeps the time, Ctrl the value
  3094	            auto tick = snap (xToTick (e.x), snapOn) - c.start + c.offset;
  3095	            const auto lo = autoPoint > 0 ? points[(std::size_t) autoPoint - 1].tick : 0;
  3096	            const auto hi = autoPoint + 1 < (int) points.size() ? points[(std::size_t) autoPoint + 1].tick : std::numeric_limits<std::int64_t>::max();
  3097	            tick = juce::jlimit (lo, hi, tick);
  3098	            const auto body = automationBody (c);
  3099	            auto value = juce::jlimit (0.0f, 1.0f, (body.getBottom() - (float) e.y) / juce::jmax (1.0f, body.getHeight()));
  3100	
  3101	            if (e.mods.isShiftDown() && ! downMods.isShiftDown())
  3102	                tick = points[(std::size_t) autoPoint].tick;
  3103	            if (flmods::ctrl (e.mods) && ! flmods::ctrl (downMods))
  3104	                value = points[(std::size_t) autoPoint].value;
  3105	
  3106	            engine.editSong ([a = c.automation, i = autoPoint, tick, value] (Song& s) {
  3107	                auto& p = s.automations[(std::size_t) a].points[(std::size_t) i];
  3108	                p.tick = tick;
  3109	                p.value = value;
  3110	            });
  3111	            return;
  3112	        }
  3113	
  3114	        case Drag::autoTension:
  3115	        {
  3116	            const auto& song = engine.getSong();
  3117	            if (autoClip < 0 || autoClip >= (int) song.playlist.size())
  3118	                return;
  3119	            const auto a = song.playlist[(std::size_t) autoClip].automation;
  3120	            if (a < 0 || a >= (int) song.automations.size() || autoPoint < 0 || autoPoint + 1 >= (int) song.automations[(std::size_t) a].points.size())
  3121	                return;
  3122	            // the handle follows the mouse: a drag over the curve's height bends the segment fully (Ctrl: 4× finer)
  3123	            const auto range = juce::jmax (30.0f, automationBody (song.playlist[(std::size_t) autoClip]).getHeight()) * (flmods::ctrl (e.mods) ? 4.0f : 1.0f);
  3124	            const auto up = -(float) e.getDistanceFromDragStartY() / range;
  3125	            const auto tension = juce::jlimit (-1.0f, 1.0f, tensionStart + tensionSign * up);
  3126	            engine.editSong ([a, i = autoPoint, tension] (Song& s) { s.automations[(std::size_t) a].points[(std::size_t) i].tension = tension; });
  3127	            return;
  3128	        }
  3129	
  3130	        case Drag::timeMove:
  3131	        {
  3132	            const auto here = juce::jmax<std::int64_t> (0, snap (xToTick (e.x), snapOn));
  3133	            const auto delta = juce::jmax (-timeMoveFrom, here - timeMoveAnchor);
  3134	            std::vector<int> moved;
  3135	            engine.editSong ([&] (Song& s) {
  3136	                s.playlist = timeMoveOriginals;
  3137	                moved = noteedit::moveTimeRange (s.playlist, timeMoveFrom, timeMoveTo, delta, timeMoveCopy);
  3138	                s.loopStart = timeMoveFrom + delta;
  3139	                s.loopEnd = timeMoveTo + delta;
  3140	            });
  3141	            setSelection (moved);
  3142	            return;
  3143	        }
  3144	
  3145	        case Drag::timeShift: // FL: Shift+drag on the ruler moves the time selection (its length kept)
  3146	        {
  3147	            const auto here = juce::jmax<std::int64_t> (0, snap (xToTick (e.x), snapOn));
  3148	            const auto r = ruler::moved (timeMoveFrom, timeMoveTo, here - timeMoveAnchor);
  3149	            setTimeSelection (r.from, r.to);
  3150	            return;
  3151	        }
  3152	
  3153	        case Drag::seek:
  3154	        case Drag::listen:
  3155	            seekTo (e.x, snapOn);
  3156	            return;
  3157	
  3158	        case Drag::loop:
  3159	        {
  3160	            const auto here = juce::jmax<std::int64_t> (0, snap (xToTick (e.x) + (snapOn ? gridTicks() / 2.0 : 0.0), snapOn));
  3161	            setTimeSelection (juce::jmin (loopAnchor, here), juce::jmax (loopAnchor, here));
  3162	            return;
  3163	        }
  3164	
  3165	        case Drag::clipGain:
  3166	        {
  3167	            const auto delta = (gainStartY - e.y) * clipops::clipGainDbPerPixel;
  3168	            std::vector<std::pair<int, float>> gains;
  3169	            for (std::size_t k = 0; k < dragIndices.size(); ++k)
  3170	                gains.push_back ({ dragIndices[k], clipops::clipGainFromDb (gainStartDb[k] + delta) });
  3171	            engine.editSong ([gains] (Song& s) {
  3172	                for (auto [i, g] : gains)
  3173	                    if (i < (int) s.playlist.size())

  3175	            });
  3176	            postDragHint();
  3177	            repaint();
  3178	            return;
  3179	        }
  3180	
  3181	        case Drag::fadeIn:
  3182	        case Drag::fadeOut:
  3183	        {
  3184	            const auto& clip = engine.getSong().playlist[(std::size_t) dragClip];
  3185	            const auto tick = snapOn ? snap (xToTick (e.x) + gridTicks() / 2.0, true) : (std::int64_t) xToTick (e.x);
  3186	            auto length = drag == Drag::fadeIn ? tick - clip.start : clip.end() - tick;
  3187	            length = juce::jlimit<std::int64_t> (0, clip.length, length);
  3188	            const auto in = drag == Drag::fadeIn;
  3189	
  3190	            if ((in ? clip.fadeIn : clip.fadeOut) == std::min (length, clip.length - (in ? clip.fadeOut : clip.fadeIn)))
  3191	                return;
  3192	
  3193	            engine.editSong ([i = dragClip, length, in] (Song& s) {
  3194	                auto& c = s.playlist[(std::size_t) i];
  3195	                (in ? c.fadeIn : c.fadeOut) = std::min (length, c.length - (in ? c.fadeOut : c.fadeIn));
  3196	            });
  3197	            return;
  3198	        }
  3199	
  3200	        case Drag::select:
  3201	        case Drag::zoomRange:
  3202	        {
  3203	            if (e.getDistanceFromDragStart() < 3 && rubberBand.isEmpty())
  3204	                return;
  3205	
  3206	            const auto area = gridArea();
  3207	
  3208	            if (drag == Drag::zoomRange) // a time range over the whole height (also when dragged on the ruler)
  3209	            {
  3210	                const auto x0 = juce::jlimit (area.getX(), area.getRight(), juce::jmin (e.getMouseDownX(), e.x));
  3211	                const auto x1 = juce::jlimit (area.getX(), area.getRight(), juce::jmax (e.getMouseDownX(), e.x));

  3213	                repaint();
  3214	                return;
  3215	            }
  3216	
  3217	            rubberBand = juce::Rectangle<int> (e.getMouseDownPosition(), e.getPosition()).getIntersection (area);
  3218

  3220	            const auto tr0 = yToTrack (rubberBand.getY()), tr1 = yToTrack (rubberBand.getBottom());
  3221	            const auto& playlist = engine.getSong().playlist;
  3222	            auto s = selectionBefore;
  3223	
  3224	            for (int i = 0; i < (int) playlist.size(); ++i)
  3225	            {
  3226	                const auto& c = playlist[(std::size_t) i];
  3227	
  3228	                if ((double) c.end() > t0 && (double) c.start < t1 && c.track >= tr0 && c.track <= tr1)
  3229	                    s.push_back (i);
  3230	            }
  3231	
  3232	            setSelection (s);
  3233	            return;
  3234	        }
  3235	
  3236	        case Drag::erase:
  3237	        {
  3238	            // right press-and-hold on empty space, then drag: a temporary Knife
  3239	            if (e.mods.isRightButtonDown() && startedOnEmpty && ! erasedSomething && ! holdExpired)
  3240	            {
  3241	                if (e.getLengthOfMousePress() >= 350)
  3242	                {
  3243	                    drag = Drag::knife;
  3244	                    knifeFrom = e.getMouseDownPosition();
  3245	                    knifeTo = e.getPosition();
  3246	                    repaint();
  3247	                    return;
  3248	                }
  3249	
  3250	                if (e.getDistanceFromDragStart() > 3)

  3252	            }

  3256

  3263	        }
  3264	
  3265	        case Drag::muting:

  3269	
  3270	            if (hit >= 0 && std::find (touchedClips.begin(), touchedClips.end(), hit) == touchedClips.end())
  3271	            {
  3272	                touchedClips.push_back (hit); // a double right-click started on empty space: the first clip decides
  3273	                const auto m = muteSweep.touch (engine.getSong().playlist[(std::size_t) hit].muted);
  3274	                engine.editSong ([hit, m] (Song& s) { s.playlist[(std::size_t) hit].muted = m; });

  3278	
  3279	        case Drag::knife:
  3280	            knifeTo = e.getPosition();
  3281	            repaint();
  3282	            return;
  3283	
  3284	        case Drag::paint:
  3285	        {
  3286	            // copies of the painted clip, end to end, on the track under the mouse
  3287	            const auto tick = snap (xToTick (e.x), snapOn);
  3288	            const auto length = juce::jmax<std::int64_t> (1, paintClip.length);
  3289

  3291	            {
  3292	                auto c = paintClip;
  3293	                c.start = nextPaintTick;
  3294	                c.track = yToTrack (e.y);
  3295	                nextPaintTick += length;
  3296	                if (noteedit::paintWouldStack (engine.getSong().playlist, c))

  3298	                engine.editSong ([c] (Song& s) { s.playlist.push_back (c); });
  3299	            }
  3300	            return;
  3301	        }
  3302	
  3303	        case Drag::slip:
  3304	        {
  3305	            // the content moves by the (snapped) mouse movement; the clips stay put
  3306	            const auto delta = snap (xToTick (e.x), snapOn) - snap (xToTick (e.getMouseDownX()), snapOn);
  3307	            const auto grabbedAt = (std::size_t) (std::find (dragIndices.begin(), dragIndices.end(), dragClip) - dragIndices.begin());
  3308	            auto probe = dragOriginals[grabbedAt];
  3309	            noteedit::slip (probe, delta);
  3310	
  3311	            if (engine.getSong().playlist[(std::size_t) dragClip].offset == probe.offset)
  3312	                return;
  3313	
  3314	            engine.editSong ([this, delta] (Song& s) {
  3315	                for (std::size_t j = 0; j < dragIndices.size(); ++j)
  3316	                {
  3317	                    auto c = dragOriginals[j];
  3318	                    noteedit::slip (c, delta);
  3319	                    s.playlist[(std::size_t) dragIndices[j]].offset = c.offset;
  3320	                }
  3321	            });
  3322	            return;
  3323	        }
  3324	
  3325	        case Drag::move:
  3326	        {
  3327	            const auto grabbedAt = (std::size_t) (std::find (dragIndices.begin(), dragIndices.end(), dragClip) - dragIndices.begin());
  3328	            const auto& grabbed = dragOriginals[grabbedAt];
  3329	            auto deltaTicks = juce::jmax<std::int64_t> (0, snap (xToTick (e.x), snapOn) + grabOffset) - grabbed.start;
  3330	            auto deltaTracks = yToTrack (e.y) - grabbed.track;
  3331	
  3332	            // the group keeps its shape: clamp by the leftmost / topmost clip
  3333	            for (const auto& c : dragOriginals)
  3334	            {
  3335	                deltaTicks = juce::jmax (deltaTicks, -c.start);
  3336	                deltaTracks = juce::jlimit (-c.track, 499 - c.track, deltaTracks);
  3337	            }
  3338	
  3339	            const auto& current = engine.getSong().playlist[(std::size_t) dragClip];
  3340	
  3341	            if (current.start == grabbed.start + deltaTicks && current.track == grabbed.track + deltaTracks)
  3342	                return;
  3343	
  3344	            engine.editSong ([this, deltaTicks, deltaTracks] (Song& s) {
  3345	                for (std::size_t j = 0; j < dragIndices.size(); ++j)
  3346	                {
  3347	                    auto& c = s.playlist[(std::size_t) dragIndices[j]];
  3348	                    c.start = dragOriginals[j].start + deltaTicks;
  3349	                    c.track = dragOriginals[j].track + deltaTracks;
  3350	                }
  3351	            });
  3352	            return;
  3353	        }
  3354	
  3355	        case Drag::resize:
  3356	        {
  3357	            const auto grabbedAt = (std::size_t) (std::find (dragIndices.begin(), dragIndices.end(), dragClip) - dragIndices.begin());
  3358	            const auto& grabbed = dragOriginals[grabbedAt];
  3359	            const auto minimum = snapOn ? juce::jmax<std::int64_t> (10, gridTicks()) : (std::int64_t) 10;
  3360	            const auto end = snapOn ? snap (xToTick (e.x) + gridTicks() / 2.0, true) : (std::int64_t) xToTick (e.x);
  3361	            const auto delta = juce::jmax (minimum, end - grabbed.start) - grabbed.length;
  3362	
  3363	            if (engine.getSong().playlist[(std::size_t) dragClip].length == grabbed.length + delta)
  3364	                return;
  3365	
  3366	            // the STRETCH switch (FL's ClipStretchBtn, round 168f): an audio clip's audio stretches with its edge
  3367	            if (EditorGlobals::get().stretchOnResize && grabbed.kind == ClipKind::audio && ! stretchOriginals.empty())
  3368	            {
  3369	                engine.editSong ([this, delta, minimum] (Song& s) {
  3370	                    s.playlist = stretchOriginals;
  3371	                    for (std::size_t j = 0; j < dragIndices.size(); ++j)
  3372	                        if (const auto& o = dragOriginals[j]; o.kind == ClipKind::audio)
  3373	                            pltracks::stretchAudioClip (s, stretchOriginals, dragIndices[j], o.start, juce::jmax (minimum, o.length + delta),
  3374	                                                        EditorGlobals::get().createVariants);
  3375	                });
  3376	                return;
  3377	            }
  3378	
  3379	            if (ripple && ! rippleOriginals.empty()) // the later clips on the track follow the end
  3380	            {
  3381	                engine.editSong ([this, length = juce::jmax (minimum, grabbed.length + delta)] (Song& s) {
  3382	                    s.playlist = rippleOriginals;
  3383	                    noteedit::rippleResize (s.playlist, dragClip, length);
  3384	                });
  3385	                return;
  3386	            }
  3387	
  3388	            engine.editSong ([this, delta, minimum] (Song& s) {
  3389	                for (std::size_t j = 0; j < dragIndices.size(); ++j)
  3390	                {
  3391	                    auto& c = s.playlist[(std::size_t) dragIndices[j]];
  3392	                    c.length = juce::jmax (minimum, dragOriginals[j].length + delta);
  3393	                    c.fadeIn = juce::jmin (c.fadeIn, c.length);
  3394	                    c.fadeOut = juce::jmin (c.fadeOut, c.length - c.fadeIn);
  3395	                }
  3396	            });
  3397	            return;
  3398	        }
  3399	
  3400	        case Drag::trimLeft:
  3401	        {
  3402	            // the start moves, the content stays where it is (the offset follows)
  3403	            const auto grabbedAt = (std::size_t) (std::find (dragIndices.begin(), dragIndices.end(), dragClip) - dragIndices.begin());
  3404	            const auto& grabbed = dragOriginals[grabbedAt];
  3405	            const auto minimum = snapOn ? juce::jmax<std::int64_t> (10, gridTicks()) : (std::int64_t) 10;
  3406	            const auto start = snapOn ? snap (xToTick (e.x) + gridTicks() / 2.0, true) : (std::int64_t) xToTick (e.x);
  3407	            const auto delta = start - grabbed.start;
  3408	            auto probe = grabbed;
  3409	            noteedit::trimStart (probe, grabbed.start + delta, minimum);
  3410	
  3411	            if (engine.getSong().playlist[(std::size_t) dragClip].start == probe.start)
  3412	                return;
  3413	
  3414	            if (EditorGlobals::get().stretchOnResize && grabbed.kind == ClipKind::audio && ! stretchOriginals.empty())
  3415	            {
  3416	                // stretching from the start: the end stays (round 168f)
  3417	                engine.editSong ([this, delta, minimum] (Song& s) {
  3418	                    s.playlist = stretchOriginals;
  3419	                    for (std::size_t j = 0; j < dragIndices.size(); ++j)
  3420	                        if (const auto& o = dragOriginals[j]; o.kind == ClipKind::audio)
  3421	                        {
  3422	                            const auto newStart = juce::jlimit<std::int64_t> (0, o.end() - minimum, o.start + delta);
  3423	                            pltracks::stretchAudioClip (s, stretchOriginals, dragIndices[j], newStart, o.end() - newStart,
  3424	                                                        EditorGlobals::get().createVariants);
  3425	                        }
  3426	                });
  3427	                return;
  3428	            }
  3429	
  3430	            engine.editSong ([this, delta, minimum] (Song& s) {
  3431	                for (std::size_t j = 0; j < dragIndices.size(); ++j)
  3432	                {
  3433	                    auto c = dragOriginals[j];
  3434	                    noteedit::trimStart (c, c.start + delta, minimum);
  3435	                    s.playlist[(std::size_t) dragIndices[j]] = c;
  3436	                }
  3437	            });
  3438	            return;
  3439	        }
  3440	    }
  3441	}
  3442	
  3443	void PlaylistComponent::postDragHint()
  3444	{
  3445	    const auto& song = engine.getSong();
  3446	    const auto count = juce::String ((int) juce::jmax<std::size_t> (1, dragIndices.size()));
  3447	    auto position = [&song] (std::int64_t tick) {
  3448	        const auto bar = song.barAt (juce::jmax<std::int64_t> (0, tick));
  3449	        const auto beat = (tick - bar.tick) / ppq + 1;
  3450	        return juce::String (bar.number) + ":" + juce::String (beat);
  3451	    };
  3452	    const auto clip = dragClip >= 0 && dragClip < (int) song.playlist.size() ? &song.playlist[(std::size_t) dragClip] : nullptr;
  3453	    ui::Hint hint;
  3454	
  3455	    switch (drag)
  3456	    {
  3457	        case Drag::move:
  3458	            if (clip == nullptr)
  3459	                return;
  3460	            hint.text = tr ("Moving {0} clip(s)  [Alt] no snap", { count });
  3461	            hint.value = position (clip->start) + "  " + tr ("Track {0}", { juce::String (clip->track + 1) });
  3462	            break;
  3463	        case Drag::resize:
  3464	        case Drag::trimLeft:
  3465	            if (clip == nullptr)
  3466	                return;
  3467	            hint.text = tr ("Resizing {0} clip(s)  [Alt] no snap", { count });
  3468	            hint.value = position (drag == Drag::resize ? clip->end() : clip->start);
  3469	            break;
  3470	        case Drag::slip: hint.text = tr ("Sliding the content inside the clip  [Alt] no snap"); break;
  3471	        case Drag::clipGain:
  3472	            if (clip == nullptr)
  3473	                return;
  3474	            hint.text = tr ("Clip gain: drag up / down ({0} clip(s))", { count });
  3475	            hint.value = tr ("Gain: {0}", { gainText (clip->gain) });
  3476	            break;
  3477	        case Drag::fadeIn:
  3478	        case Drag::fadeOut: hint.text = tr ("Setting the fade  [Alt] no snap"); break;
  3479	        case Drag::autoPoint: hint.text = tr ("Moving the point: then [Shift] keeps time  [Ctrl] keeps value"); break;
  3480	        case Drag::autoTension: hint.text = tr ("Bending the segment: drag up / down  [Ctrl] fine"); break;
  3481	        case Drag::select:
  3482	            hint.text = tr ("Selecting  [Shift] adds to the selection");
  3483	            hint.value = tr ("{0} selected", { juce::String ((int) selection.size()) });
  3484	            break;
  3485	        case Drag::loop:
  3486	            hint.text = tr ("Time selection  [Alt] no snap");
  3487	            hint.value = position (song.loopStart) + " - " + position (song.loopEnd);
  3488	            break;
  3489	        case Drag::erase: hint.text = tr ("Deleting what the mouse crosses"); break;
  3490	        case Drag::muting: hint.text = tr ("Muting / unmuting what the mouse crosses"); break;
  3491	        case Drag::knife: hint.text = tr ("Cutting along the line  [Alt] no snap"); break;
  3492	        case Drag::paint: hint.text = tr ("Painting clips  [Alt] no snap"); break;
  3493	        case Drag::timeMove: hint.text = tr ("Moving the time selection with its clips  [Alt] no snap"); break;
  3494	        case Drag::timeShift:
  3495	            hint.text = tr ("Moving the time selection  [Alt] no snap");
  3496	            hint.value = position (song.loopStart) + " - " + position (song.loopEnd);
  3497	            break;
  3498	        case Drag::seek:
  3499	            hint.text = tr ("Song position  [Alt] no snap");
  3500	            hint.value = position ((std::int64_t) playheadTick);
  3501	            break;
  3502	        case Drag::listen:
  3503	        case Drag::pan:
  3504	        case Drag::zoomRange:
  3505	        case Drag::trackResize:
  3506	        case Drag::trackZoom:
  3507	        case Drag::heightZoom:
  3508	        case Drag::timeZoom:
  3509	        case Drag::preview:
  3510	        case Drag::none: return;
  3511	    }
  3512	
  3513	    ui::postHint (hint);
  3514	}
  3515	
  3516	#if JUCE_ANDROID
  3517	void PlaylistComponent::cancelTouchHold()
  3518	{
  3519	    stopTimer();
  3520	    touchHoldPending = touchHoldTriggered = touchHoldOpensMenu = false;
  3521	    touchHoldClip = touchHoldSource = -1;
  3522	}
  3523	
  3524	void PlaylistComponent::timerCallback()
  3525	{
  3526	    if (! touchHoldPending || touchHoldTriggered)
  3527	    {
  3528	        stopTimer();
  3529	        return;
  3530	    }
  3531	
  3532	    const auto* source = juce::Desktop::getInstance().getMouseSource (touchHoldSource);
  3533	    if (source == nullptr || ! source->isDragging())
  3534	    {
  3535	        cancelTouchHold();
  3536	        return;
  3537	    }
  3538	
  3539	    const auto screenPosition = source->getScreenPosition();
  3540	    if (screenPosition.getDistanceFrom (touchHoldScreenStart) > 8.0f)
  3541	    {
  3542	        cancelTouchHold();
  3543	        return;
  3544	    }
  3545	
  3546	    const auto position = getLocalPoint (nullptr, screenPosition.roundToInt());
  3547	    bool rightEdge = false, leftEdge = false;
  3548	    if (clipAt (position.x, position.y, rightEdge, leftEdge) != touchHoldClip)
  3549	    {
  3550	        cancelTouchHold();
  3551	        return;
  3552	    }
  3553	
  3554	    // Commit on release so a hold that turns into a drag can still move the clip.
  3555	    touchHoldTriggered = true;
  3556	    stopTimer();
  3557	}
  3558	#endif
  3559	
  3560	void PlaylistComponent::mouseUp (const juce::MouseEvent& e)
  3561	{
  3562	#if JUCE_ANDROID
  3563	    if (touchHoldPending && touchHoldTriggered)
  3564	    {
  3565	        const auto clip = touchHoldClip;
  3566	        const auto opensMenu = touchHoldOpensMenu;
  3567	        bool rightEdge = false, leftEdge = false;
  3568	        const auto releasedOnClip = e.source.isTouch() && e.getDistanceFromDragStart() <= 8.0f
  3569	                                    && clipAt (e.x, e.y, rightEdge, leftEdge) == clip;
  3570	        cancelTouchHold();
  3571	
  3572	        if (releasedOnClip && clip >= 0 && clip < (int) engine.getSong().playlist.size())
  3573	        {
  3574	            drag = Drag::none;
  3575	            dragClip = -1;
  3576	            dragIndices.clear();
  3577	            dragOriginals.clear();
  3578	
  3579	            if (opensMenu)
  3580	                showClipMenu (clip);
  3581	            else
  3582	            {
  3583	                setSelection ({ clip });
  3584	                deleteSelection();
  3585	                ui::postHint ({ tr ("Clip deleted"), tr ("Undo from the Edit menu") }, 3000);
  3586	            }
  3587	
  3588	            repaint();
  3589	            return;
  3590	        }
  3591	    }
  3592	    cancelTouchHold();
  3593	#endif
  3594	    ui::clearPostedHint();
  3595	    if (drag == Drag::perfLaunch)
  3596	    {
  3597	        perf::Command c;
  3598	        c.type = perf::Command::Type::release;
  3599	        c.track = perfTrack;
  3600	        c.clipStart = perfClip;
  3601	        engine.performanceCommand (c);
  3602	        drag = Drag::none;
  3603	        perfTrack = -1;
  3604	        return;
  3605	    }
  3606	    if (drag == Drag::knife)
  3607	        cutWithKnife (knifeFrom, knifeTo, ! e.mods.isAltDown());
  3608	
  3609	    if (drag == Drag::zoomRange)
  3610	    {
  3611	        if (rubberBand.getWidth() > 4)
  3612	            zoomToTicks (xToTick (rubberBand.getX()), xToTick (rubberBand.getRight()));
  3613	        else
  3614	            zoomIn (e.x); // a click zooms in around the point (right-click zooms out)
  3615	    }
  3616	
  3617	    if (drag == Drag::select && rubberBand.isEmpty() && dragClip >= 0)
  3618	    {
  3619	        auto s = selectionBefore;
  3620	        if (auto it = std::find (s.begin(), s.end(), dragClip); it != s.end())
  3621	            s.erase (it);
  3622	        else
  3623	            s.push_back (dragClip);
  3624	        setSelection (s);
  3625	    }
  3626	
  3627	    // Ctrl+click on the ruler without dragging clears the time selection (and so does a double click); a right click
  3628	    // in place keeps what it did (FL: moved the nearer edge) and, with no selection, opens LYNX's ruler menu
  3629	    if (drag == Drag::loop && e.getDistanceFromDragStart() < 3 && ! flmods::of (downMods).right)
  3630	        setTimeSelection (0, 0);
  3631	    else if (drag == Drag::loop && rulerMenuOnClick && ruler::isClick (e.getDistanceFromDragStartX(), e.getDistanceFromDragStartY()))
  3632	    {
  3633	        rulerMenuOnClick = false;
  3634	        showRulerMenu (e.getMouseDownX());
  3635	    }
  3636	    else if (drag == Drag::loop && rulerDoubleDrag && engine.getSong().hasLoop())
  3637	    {
  3638	        const auto start = (double) engine.getSong().loopStart;
  3639	        const auto waits = engine.getTransport().playing.load() && engine.quantizeSongJumps.load() > 0;
  3640	        engine.jumpTo (start);
  3641	        if (! waits)
  3642	            playheadTick = start;
  3643	    }
  3644	    rulerDoubleDrag = false;
  3645	    rulerMenuOnClick = false;
  3646	
  3647	    if (drag == Drag::listen)
  3648	        engine.stop();
  3649	
  3650	    if (drag == Drag::preview)
  3651	        stopPreview();
  3652	
  3653	    resizeTrack = -1;
  3654	
  3655	    if (drag == Drag::move)
  3656	    {
  3657	        // FL reroutes only the clips that landed on another track: sliding a clip
  3658	        // along its own audio track leaves its channel's insert alone
  3659	        std::vector<int> moved;
  3660	        const auto& playlist = engine.getSong().playlist;
  3661	        for (std::size_t j = 0; j < dragIndices.size() && j < dragOriginals.size(); ++j)
  3662	        {
  3663	            const auto i = dragIndices[j];
  3664	            if (i >= 0 && i < (int) playlist.size() && playlist[(std::size_t) i].track != dragOriginals[j].track)
  3665	                moved.push_back (i);
  3666	        }
  3667	        sortClips (moved);
  3668	    }
  3669	    else if (drag == Drag::paint)
  3670	    {
  3671	        std::vector<int> painted; // the first painted clip and the copies appended after it
  3672	        for (auto i = dragClip; i >= 0 && i < (int) engine.getSong().playlist.size(); ++i)
  3673	            painted.push_back (i);
  3674	        sortClips (painted);
  3675	    }
  3676	    else if (drag == Drag::resize || drag == Drag::trimLeft || drag == Drag::timeMove)
  3677	        sortClips();
  3678	
  3679	    rippleOriginals.clear();
  3680	    timeMoveOriginals.clear();
  3681	    autoClip = autoPoint = -1;
  3682	
  3683	    drag = Drag::none;
  3684	    dragClip = -1;
  3685	    dragIndices.clear();
  3686	    dragOriginals.clear();
  3687	    touchedClips.clear();
  3688	    rubberBand = {};
  3689	    knownClipCount = engine.getSong().playlist.size();
  3690	    hover = {};
  3691	    updateHover (e.getPosition(), e.mods); // the cursor of what is under the mouse now
  3692	    repaint();
  3693	}
  3694	
  3695	void PlaylistComponent::cutWithKnife (juce::Point<int> from, juce::Point<int> to, bool snapOn)
  3696	{
  3697	    const auto& playlist = engine.getSong().playlist;
  3698	    std::vector<std::pair<int, std::int64_t>> cuts;
  3699	    const auto round = snapOn ? gridTicks() / 2.0 : 0.0;
  3700	
  3701	    if (from.getDistanceFrom (to) < 3)
  3702	    {
  3703	        bool edge = false;
  3704	        if (const auto hit = clipAt (from.x, from.y, edge); hit >= 0)
  3705	            cuts.push_back ({ hit, snap (xToTick (from.x) + round, snapOn) });
  3706	    }
  3707	    else
  3708	    {
  3709	        // every track the line crosses is cut where the line passes its middle
  3710	        const auto top = juce::jmin (from.y, to.y), bottom = juce::jmax (from.y, to.y);
  3711	
  3712	        for (int i = 0; i < (int) playlist.size(); ++i)
  3713	        {
  3714	            const auto& c = playlist[(std::size_t) i];
  3715	            const auto trackHeight = heightOf (c.track);
  3716	            const auto rowY = trackToY (c.track) + trackHeight / 2;
  3717	
  3718	            if (rowY < top - trackHeight / 2 || rowY > bottom + trackHeight / 2)
  3719	                continue;
  3720	
  3721	            const auto y = juce::jlimit (top, bottom, rowY);
  3722	            const auto x = from.y == to.y ? (double) from.x : from.x + (double) (y - from.y) * (to.x - from.x) / (double) (to.y - from.y);
  3723	            const auto tick = snap (xToTick ((int) std::round (x)) + round, snapOn);
  3724	
  3725	            if (tick > c.start && tick < c.end())
  3726	                cuts.push_back ({ i, tick });
  3727	        }
  3728	    }
  3729	
  3730	    if (cuts.empty())
  3731	        return;
  3732	
  3733	    setSelection ({});
  3734	    engine.editSong ([&cuts] (Song& s) {
  3735	        for (auto [index, tick] : cuts)
  3736	        {
  3737	            auto rest = splitClip (s.playlist[(std::size_t) index], tick);
  3738	            s.playlist.push_back (rest);
  3739	        }
  3740	    });
  3741	    sortClips();
  3742	}
  3743	
  3744	void PlaylistComponent::zoomToTicks (double from, double to)
  3745	{
  3746	    if (to - from < 1.0)
  3747	        return;
  3748	
  3749	    pixelsPerTick = juce::jlimit (4.0 / (4 * ppq), 800.0 / (4 * ppq), gridArea().getWidth() / (to - from));
  3750	    scrollTicks = juce::jmax (0.0, from);
  3751	    updateScrollBars();
  3752	    repaint();
  3753	}
  3754	
  3755	void PlaylistComponent::zoomIn (int anchorX)
  3756	{
  3757	    const auto anchor = xToTick (anchorX);
  3758	    pixelsPerTick = juce::jlimit (4.0 / (4 * ppq), 800.0 / (4 * ppq), pixelsPerTick * 2.0);
  3759	    scrollTicks = juce::jmax (0.0, anchor - (anchorX - headerWidth) / pixelsPerTick);
  3760	    updateScrollBars();
  3761	    repaint();
  3762	}
  3763	
  3764	void PlaylistComponent::zoomOut (int anchorX)
  3765	{
  3766	    const auto anchor = xToTick (anchorX);
  3767	    pixelsPerTick = juce::jlimit (4.0 / (4 * ppq), 800.0 / (4 * ppq), pixelsPerTick / 2.0);
  3768	    scrollTicks = juce::jmax (0.0, anchor - (anchorX - headerWidth) / pixelsPerTick);
  3769	    updateScrollBars();
  3770	    repaint();
  3771	}
  3772	
  3773	void PlaylistComponent::insertTimeSelection()
  3774	{
  3775	    const auto& song = engine.getSong();
  3776	
  3777	    if (! song.hasLoop())
  3778	        return;
  3779	
  3780	    setSelection ({});
  3781	    engine.editSong ([from = song.loopStart, to = song.loopEnd] (Song& s) { noteedit::insertTime (s.playlist, s.markers(), from, to - from); });
  3782	    sortClips();
  3783	}
  3784	
  3785	void PlaylistComponent::deleteTimeSelection()
  3786	{
  3787	    const auto& song = engine.getSong();
  3788	
  3789	    if (! song.hasLoop())
  3790	        return;
  3791	
  3792	    setSelection ({});
  3793	    engine.editSong ([from = song.loopStart, to = song.loopEnd] (Song& s) { noteedit::deleteTime (s.playlist, s.markers(), from, to); });
  3794	    sortClips();
  3795	}
  3796	
  3797	juce::Rectangle<int> PlaylistComponent::trackLed (int track) const
  3798	{
  3799	    const auto h = heightOf (track);
  3800	    return { headerWidth - 17, trackToY (track) + h / 2 - 6, 12, 12 };
  3801	}
  3802	
  3803	juce::Rectangle<int> PlaylistComponent::trackArrow (int track) const
  3804	{
  3805	    if (! noteedit::isGroupHeader (engine.getSong(), track))
  3806	        return {};
  3807	
  3808	    const auto led = trackLed (track);
  3809	    return { led.getX() - 15, led.getY() - 1, 13, led.getHeight() + 2 };
  3810	}
  3811	
  3812	juce::Rectangle<int> PlaylistComponent::trackArm (int track) const
  3813	{
  3814	    if (trackmodes::armableInsert (engine.getSong(), track) < 0 || noteedit::isFoldedAway (engine.getSong(), track))
  3815	        return {};
  3816	
  3817	    const auto arrow = trackArrow (track);
  3818	    const auto led = trackLed (track);
  3819	    return { (arrow.isEmpty() ? led.getX() : arrow.getX()) - 15, led.getY(), 12, 12 };
  3820	}
  3821	
  3822	juce::Rectangle<int> PlaylistComponent::trackInput (int track) const
  3823	{
  3824	    const auto arm = trackArm (track);
  3825	    if (arm.isEmpty() || heightOf (track) < inputButtonMinHeight)
  3826	        return {};
  3827	    return arm.translated (-15, 0);
  3828	}
  3829	
  3830	bool PlaylistComponent::isTrackSelected (int track) const
  3831	{
  3832	    return track == currentTrack || std::find (selectedTracks.begin(), selectedTracks.end(), track) != selectedTracks.end();
  3833	}
  3834	
  3835	std::vector<int> PlaylistComponent::tracksForMenu (int track) const
  3836	{
  3837	    // the menu's track and, when it is one of them, the other selected tracks
  3838	    if (! isTrackSelected (track) || selectedTracks.size() < 2)
  3839	        return { track };
  3840	    auto out = selectedTracks;
  3841	    if (std::find (out.begin(), out.end(), track) == out.end())
  3842	        out.push_back (track);
  3843	    std::sort (out.begin(), out.end());
  3844	    return out;
  3845	}
  3846	
  3847	void PlaylistComponent::editTrack (int track, std::function<void (PlaylistTrack&)> change)
  3848	{
  3849	    if (track < 0)
  3850	        return;
  3851	
  3852	    engine.editSong ([track, change] (Song& s) {
  3853	        if ((int) s.playlistTracks.size() <= track)
  3854	            s.playlistTracks.resize ((std::size_t) track + 1);
  3855	        change (s.playlistTracks[(std::size_t) track]);
  3856	    });
  3857	}
  3858	
  3859	// The track menu in FL's order (REA round 104): name and colour, size, grouping, its clips, insert / clone /
  3860	// delete, move; track modes and Consolidate since round 146a. FL items LYNX has no counterpart for (icons, performance
  3861	// settings, ghost notes) are left out.
  3862	void PlaylistComponent::showTrackMenu (int track)
  3863	{
  3864	    buildTrackMenu (track).showMenuAsync (juce::PopupMenu::Options().withTargetComponent (this).withMousePosition());
  3865	}
  3866	
  3867	juce::PopupMenu PlaylistComponent::buildTrackMenu (int track)
  3868	{
  3869	    const auto& song = engine.getSong();
  3870	    const auto count = numTracks();
  3871	    const auto info = track < (int) song.playlistTracks.size() ? song.playlistTracks[(std::size_t) track] : PlaylistTrack {};
  3872	    juce::PopupMenu menu;
  3873	    ui::addItemHeader (menu, tr ("Playlist track"), ! info.name.empty() ? juce::String::fromUTF8 (info.name.c_str()) : tr ("Track {0}", { juce::String (track + 1) }),
  3874	                       info.colour != 0 ? juce::Colour (info.colour) : juce::Colour());
  3875	    menu.addItem (theme::mnemonic (tr ("Rename and color..."), 'R'), [this, track] { renameTrack (track); });
  3876	    menu.addSubMenu (tr ("Change color"), ui::colourMenu (info.colour, [this, track] (std::uint32_t colour) {
  3877	                         editTrack (track, [colour] (PlaylistTrack& t) { t.colour = colour; });
  3878	                     }, juce::Rectangle<int> (juce::Desktop::getMousePosition().x, juce::Desktop::getMousePosition().y, 1, 1)));
  3879	    menu.addItem (tr ("Random color"), [this, track] {
  3880	        const auto& palette = lynxPalette();
  3881	        const auto c = palette[(std::size_t) juce::Random::getSystemRandom().nextInt ((int) palette.size())];
  3882	        editTrack (track, [c] (PlaylistTrack& t) { t.colour = c; });
  3883	    });
  3884	    // FL's "Change icon" (EEPLTrackIconMenuClick, REA round 168h); LYNX's own icon set
  3885	    menu.addItem (tr ("Change icon..."), [this, track, current = info.icon] {
  3886	        ui::showTrackIconPicker (juce::Rectangle<int> (juce::Desktop::getMousePosition().x, juce::Desktop::getMousePosition().y, 1, 1), current, [this, track] (int icon) {
  3887	            editTrack (track, [icon] (PlaylistTrack& t) { t.icon = icon; });
  3888	        });
  3889	    });
  3890	    // FL's "Auto name" (EEPLTrackAutoNameMenuClick → FUN_012ef490, round 168f): the name and colour of what the track plays most
  3891	    menu.addItem (theme::mnemonic (tr ("Auto name"), 'A'), [this, tracks = tracksForMenu (track)] {
  3892	        engine.editSong ([tracks] (Song& s) {
  3893	            for (const auto t : tracks)
  3894	                pltracks::autoName (s, t);
  3895	        });
  3896	    });
  3897	    menu.addItem (tr ("Reset"), [this, track] {
  3898	        editTrack (track, [] (PlaylistTrack& t) {
  3899	            t.name.clear();
  3900	            t.colour = 0;
  3901	            t.height = 0;
  3902	            t.muted = false;
  3903	        });
  3904	    });
  3905	    menu.addSeparator();
  3906	    {
  3907	        // FL's "Track mode" (round 146a): unassigned, an audio track (linked to a free mixer track) or an instrument
  3908	        // track (a channel and its mixer track)
  3909	        juce::PopupMenu modes, instruments;
  3910	        modes.addItem (theme::mnemonic (tr ("Unassigned"), 'U'), true, info.mode == PlaylistTrackMode::unassigned,
  3911	                       [this, track] { engine.editSong ([track] (Song& s) { trackmodes::unassign (s, track); }); });
  3912	        modes.addItem (theme::mnemonic (tr ("Audio track"), 'A'), true, info.mode == PlaylistTrackMode::audio, [this, track] {
  3913	            bool ok = false;
  3914	            engine.editSong ([track, &ok] (Song& s) { ok = trackmodes::makeAudioTrack (s, track); });
  3915	            if (! ok)
  3916	                juce::AlertWindow::showMessageBoxAsync (juce::MessageBoxIconType::WarningIcon, tr ("Can't create new audio track"),
  3917	                                                        tr ("There are no available mixer tracks."));
  3918	        });
  3919	        for (int c = 0; c < (int) song.channels.size(); ++c)
  3920	        {
  3921	            const auto kind = song.channels[(std::size_t) c].instrument;
  3922	            if (kind == InstrumentKind::audioClip || kind == InstrumentKind::controller)
  3923	                continue;
  3924	            instruments.addItem (juce::String::fromUTF8 (song.channels[(std::size_t) c].name.c_str()), true,
  3925	                                 info.mode == PlaylistTrackMode::instrument && info.channel == c, [this, track, c] {
  3926	                                     bool ok = false;
  3927	                                     engine.editSong ([track, c, &ok] (Song& s) { ok = trackmodes::makeInstrumentTrack (s, track, c); });
  3928	                                     if (! ok)
  3929	                                         juce::AlertWindow::showMessageBoxAsync (juce::MessageBoxIconType::WarningIcon, tr ("Can't create new instrument track"),
  3930	                                                                                 tr ("There are no available mixer tracks."));
  3931	                                 });
  3932	        }
  3933	        modes.addSubMenu (theme::mnemonic (tr ("Instrument track"), 'I'), instruments, instruments.getNumItems() > 0, nullptr, info.mode == PlaylistTrackMode::instrument);
  3934	        menu.addSubMenu (theme::mnemonic (tr ("Track mode"), 'T'), modes);
  3935	    }
  3936	    {
  3937	        // FL's "Performance settings" (round 165m [DFM]): how the track's clips in the performance zone launch and play
  3938	        using P = TrackPerformance;
  3939	        const auto p = info.performance;
  3940	        juce::PopupMenu perfMenu;
  3941	        // FL's handler (0x1212fe0 → FUN_012ead80) sets the item on every selected track, in one undo step
  3942	        auto set = [this, tracks = tracksForMenu (track)] (std::function<void (TrackPerformance&)> change) {
  3943	            engine.editSong ([tracks, change] (Song& s) {
  3944	                for (const auto t : tracks)
  3945	                {
  3946	                    if ((int) s.playlistTracks.size() <= t)
  3947	                        s.playlistTracks.resize ((std::size_t) t + 1);
  3948	                    change (s.playlistTracks[(std::size_t) t].performance);
  3949	                }
  3950	            });
  3951	        };
  3952	        perfMenu.addSectionHeader (tr ("Press"));
