  3953	        for (auto [value, text] : { std::pair { P::Press::retrigger, TR_NOOP ("Retrigger") }, { P::Press::holdStop, TR_NOOP ("Hold & stop") },
  3954	                                    { P::Press::holdMotion, TR_NOOP ("Hold & motion") }, { P::Press::latch, TR_NOOP ("Latch") } })
  3955	            perfMenu.addItem (tr (text), true, p.press == value, [set, value = value] { set ([value] (P& x) { x.press = value; }); });
  3956	        perfMenu.addSectionHeader (tr ("Motion"));
  3957	        for (auto [value, text] : { std::pair { P::Motion::stay, TR_NOOP ("Stay") }, { P::Motion::oneShot, TR_NOOP ("One shot") },
  3958	                                    { P::Motion::marchWrap, TR_NOOP ("March & wrap") }, { P::Motion::marchStay, TR_NOOP ("March & stay") },
  3959	                                    { P::Motion::marchStop, TR_NOOP ("March & stop") }, { P::Motion::random, TR_NOOP ("Random") },
  3960	                                    { P::Motion::exclusiveRandom, TR_NOOP ("Exclusive random") } })
  3961	            perfMenu.addItem (tr (text), true, p.motion == value, [set, value = value] { set ([value] (P& x) { x.motion = value; }); });
  3962	        const std::pair<P::Sync, const char*> syncs[] = { { P::Sync::off, TR_NOOP ("Off") }, { P::Sync::quarterBeat, TR_NOOP ("1/4 beat") },
  3963	                                                          { P::Sync::halfBeat, TR_NOOP ("1/2 beat") }, { P::Sync::beat, TR_NOOP ("1 beat") },
  3964	                                                          { P::Sync::twoBeats, TR_NOOP ("2 beats") }, { P::Sync::fourBeats, TR_NOOP ("4 beats") },
  3965	                                                          { P::Sync::autoLength, TR_NOOP ("Auto") } };
  3966	        perfMenu.addSectionHeader (tr ("Trigger sync"));
  3967	        for (const auto& [value, text] : syncs)
  3968	            perfMenu.addItem (tr (text), true, p.triggerSync == value, [set, value = value] { set ([value] (P& x) { x.triggerSync = value; }); });
  3969	        perfMenu.addSeparator();
  3970	        perfMenu.addItem (tr ("Queue"), true, p.queue, [set] { set ([] (P& x) { x.queue = ! x.queue; }); });
  3971	        perfMenu.addItem (tr ("Tolerant"), true, p.tolerant, [set] { set ([] (P& x) { x.tolerant = ! x.tolerant; }); });
  3972	        perfMenu.addSectionHeader (tr ("Position sync"));
  3973	        for (const auto& [value, text] : syncs)
  3974	            perfMenu.addItem (tr (text), true, p.positionSync == value, [set, value = value] { set ([value] (P& x) { x.positionSync = value; }); });
  3975	        menu.addSubMenu (theme::mnemonic (tr ("Performance settings"), 'P'), perfMenu);
  3976	    }
  3977	    menu.addSeparator();
  3978	    {
  3979	        // FL's sizes are relative to the default row; dragging the divider under a header sets any size (P18)
  3980	        juce::PopupMenu size, all;
  3981	        const auto current = heightOf (track);
  3982	
  3983	        for (int percent : { 33, 50, 100, 150, 200 })
  3984	        {
  3985	            const auto h = polish::clampTrackHeight ((int) std::lround (trackHeight * percent / 100.0));
  3986	            size.addItem (juce::String (percent) + "%", true, current == scaledHeight (h), [this, track, h] { setTrackHeights ({ track }, h); });
  3987	            all.addItem (juce::String (percent) + "%", [this, count, h] {
  3988	                std::vector<int> tracks;
  3989	                for (int t = 0; t < count; ++t)
  3990	                    tracks.push_back (t);
  3991	                setTrackHeights (tracks, h);
  3992	            });
  3993	        }
  3994	        size.addSeparator();
  3995	        size.addSubMenu (tr ("Every track"), all);
  3996	        menu.addSubMenu (theme::mnemonic (tr ("Size"), 'S'), size);
  3997	    }
  3998	    // FL's "Lock to this size" (EEPLTrackLockMenuClick 0x1228cb0, round 168f): the row keeps the height it has now
  3999	    menu.addItem (theme::mnemonic (tr ("Lock to this size"), 'L'), true, info.lockedHeight > 0, [this, track, tracks = tracksForMenu (track)] {
  4000	        const auto shown = heightOf (track);
  4001	        engine.editSong ([tracks, shown] (Song& s) { pltracks::toggleSizeLock (s, tracks, shown); });
  4002	        refreshTrackLayout();
  4003	        updateScrollBars();
  4004	        repaint();
  4005	    });
  4006	    menu.addSeparator();
  4007	    {
  4008	        const auto header = noteedit::groupHeaderOf (song, track);
  4009	        const auto member = info.grouped && track > 0;
  4010	        const auto folded = header >= 0 && header < (int) song.playlistTracks.size() && song.playlistTracks[(std::size_t) header].collapsed;
  4011	        menu.addItem (theme::mnemonic (tr ("Group with above track"), 'G'), track > 0 && ! member, member, [this, track] {
  4012	            engine.editSong ([track] (Song& s) { noteedit::groupWithAbove (s, track); });
  4013	        });
  4014	        menu.addItem (theme::mnemonic (tr ("Ungroup"), 'n'), member, false, [this, track] { engine.editSong ([track] (Song& s) { noteedit::ungroupTrack (s, track); }); });
  4015	        menu.addItem (tr ("Auto color group"), header >= 0, false, [this, track] { engine.editSong ([track] (Song& s) { noteedit::colourGroup (s, track); }); });
  4016	        menu.addItem (tr ("Collapse grouped tracks"), header >= 0, folded, [this, track, folded] {
  4017	            engine.editSong ([track, folded] (Song& s) { noteedit::setGroupCollapsed (s, track, ! folded); });
  4018	        });
  4019	    }
  4020	    menu.addSeparator();
  4021	    {
  4022	        // the sources of the track's clips: choosing one makes it what the pencil paints (FL's "Current clip source")
  4023	        juce::PopupMenu sources;
  4024	        std::vector<std::pair<ClipKind, int>> seen;
  4025	        for (const auto& c : song.playlist)
  4026	        {
  4027	            if (c.track != track)
  4028	                continue;
  4029	            const auto key = std::pair { c.kind, c.kind == ClipKind::pattern ? c.pattern : c.kind == ClipKind::audio ? c.channel : c.automation };
  4030	            if (std::find (seen.begin(), seen.end(), key) != seen.end())
  4031	                continue;
  4032	            seen.push_back (key);
  4033	            juce::String name;
  4034	            if (key.first == ClipKind::pattern && key.second >= 0 && key.second < (int) song.patterns.size())
  4035	                name = juce::String::fromUTF8 (song.patterns[(std::size_t) key.second].name.c_str());
  4036	            else if (key.first == ClipKind::automation && key.second >= 0 && key.second < (int) song.automations.size())
  4037	                name = juce::String::fromUTF8 (song.automations[(std::size_t) key.second].name.c_str());
  4038	            else if (key.second >= 0 && key.second < (int) song.channels.size())
  4039	                name = juce::String::fromUTF8 (song.channels[(std::size_t) key.second].name.c_str());
  4040	            const auto ticked = brush.kind == key.first && (key.first == ClipKind::pattern ? song.currentPattern == key.second : brush.index == key.second);
  4041	            sources.addItem (name, true, ticked, [this, key] {
  4042	                brush.kind = key.first;
  4043	                brush.index = key.first == ClipKind::pattern ? -1 : key.second;
  4044	                if (key.first == ClipKind::pattern)
  4045	                    engine.editSong ([p = key.second] (Song& s) { s.currentPattern = p; }, false);
  4046	                engine.sendChangeMessage();
  4047	            });
  4048	        }
  4049	        menu.addSubMenu (tr ("Current clip source"), sources, ! seen.empty());
  4050	        // FL's "Lock to content" (EEPLTrackClipLockMenuClick → FUN_012e7a90, round 168f): painting on the track places
  4051	        // its own content
  4052	        menu.addItem (tr ("Lock to content"), true, info.lockedToContent, [this, tracks = tracksForMenu (track)] {
  4053	            engine.editSong ([tracks] (Song& s) { pltracks::toggleLockToContent (s, tracks); });
  4054	        });
  4055	        auto muteAll = [this, track] (bool mute) {
  4056	            engine.editSong ([track, mute] (Song& s) {
  4057	                for (auto& c : s.playlist)
  4058	                    if (c.track == track)
  4059	                        c.muted = mute;
  4060	            });
  4061	        };
  4062	        const auto hasClips = ! seen.empty();
  4063	        {
  4064	            // FL's "Consolidate track(s)" (round 146a): the track's clips rendered into one audio clip
  4065	            juce::PopupMenu consolidate;
  4066	            consolidate.addItem (theme::mnemonic (tr ("From track start"), 'F'), hasClips, false, [this, track] { consolidatePlaylistTrack (engine, track, false); });
  4067	            consolidate.addItem (theme::mnemonic (tr ("From song start"), 's'), hasClips, false, [this, track] { consolidatePlaylistTrack (engine, track, true); });
  4068	            menu.addSubMenu (theme::mnemonic (tr ("Consolidate track"), 'o'), consolidate, hasClips);
  4069	        }
  4070	        menu.addItem (tr ("Merge clips"), hasClips, false, [this, track] {
  4071	            setSelection ({});
  4072	            engine.editSong ([track] (Song& s) { clipops::mergeTrackClips (s, track); });
  4073	            sortClips();
  4074	        });
  4075	        // FL's "Send pattern instances here" (EEPLTrackSendPatMenuClick → FUN_012db4e0, round 168f): the selected pattern
  4076	        // clips, or every clip of the current pattern, move to this track
  4077	        menu.addItem (tr ("Send pattern instances here"), [this, track] {
  4078	            const auto chosen = selection;
  4079	            engine.editSong ([track, chosen] (Song& s) { pltracks::sendPatternInstancesHere (s, track, chosen); });
  4080	            sortClips();
  4081	        });
  4082	        menu.addItem (tr ("Mute all clips"), hasClips, false, [muteAll] { muteAll (true); });
  4083	        menu.addItem (tr ("Unmute all clips"), hasClips, false, [muteAll] { muteAll (false); });
  4084	    }
  4085	    menu.addSeparator();
  4086	    menu.addItem (theme::mnemonic (tr ("Insert one"), 'I'), [this, track] {
  4087	        setSelection ({});
  4088	        engine.editSong ([track] (Song& s) { noteedit::insertTrack (s, track); });
  4089	    });
  4090	    menu.addItem (theme::mnemonic (tr ("Clone..."), 'o'), [this, track] { cloneTracksWithDialog (tracksForMenu (track)); });
  4091	    menu.addItem (theme::mnemonic (tr ("Delete"), 'e'), [this, track] {
  4092	        setSelection ({});
  4093	        engine.editSong ([track] (Song& s) { noteedit::removeTrack (s, track); });
  4094	    });
  4095	    menu.addSeparator();
  4096	    menu.addItem (theme::mnemonic (tr ("Move up"), 'u'), track > 0, false, [this, track] { engine.editSong ([track] (Song& s) { noteedit::swapTracks (s, track, track - 1); }); });
  4097	    menu.addItem (theme::mnemonic (tr ("Move down"), 'd'), track + 1 < count, false, [this, track] { engine.editSong ([track] (Song& s) { noteedit::swapTracks (s, track, track + 1); }); });
  4098	    return menu;
  4099	}
  4100	
  4101	void PlaylistComponent::renameTrack (int track)
  4102	{
  4103	    const auto& tracks = engine.getSong().playlistTracks;
  4104	    const auto current = track < (int) tracks.size() ? juce::String::fromUTF8 (tracks[(std::size_t) track].name.c_str()) : juce::String();
  4105	    const auto colour = track < (int) tracks.size() ? tracks[(std::size_t) track].colour : 0u;
  4106	    showNameAndColourDialog (tr ("Rename track"), current.isNotEmpty() ? current : tr ("Track {0}", { juce::String (track + 1) }), colour,
  4107	                             [this, track] (const juce::String& newName, std::uint32_t newColour) {
  4108	                                 const auto name = newName.trim().toStdString();
  4109	                                 engine.editSong ([track, name, newColour] (Song& s) {
  4110	                                     if ((int) s.playlistTracks.size() <= track)
  4111	                                         s.playlistTracks.resize ((std::size_t) track + 1);
  4112	                                     s.playlistTracks[(std::size_t) track].name = name;
  4113	                                     s.playlistTracks[(std::size_t) track].colour = newColour;
  4114	                                 });
  4115	                             });
  4116	}
  4117	
  4118	juce::String PlaylistComponent::markerLabel (const Marker& m)
  4119	{
  4120	    auto label = juce::String::fromUTF8 (m.name.c_str());
  4121	    if (const auto fraction = juce::String (m.numerator) + "/" + juce::String (m.denominator); m.isSignature() && label != fraction)
  4122	        label = fraction + (label.isEmpty() ? "" : " " + label);
  4123	    // FL's marker actions: a symbol before the name (unnamed: the action's name)
  4124	    if (! m.isSignature() && m.action != 0 && m.action != Marker::punchIn && m.action != Marker::punchOut)
  4125	    {
  4126	        if (label.isEmpty())
  4127	            label = tr (markers::actionName (m.action));
  4128	        label = juce::String::fromUTF8 (markers::actionTag (m.action)) + " " + label;
  4129	    }
  4130	    if (label.isEmpty())
  4131	        label = "*";
  4132	    return label;
  4133	}
  4134	
  4135	int PlaylistComponent::markerTagAt (int x, int y) const
  4136	{
  4137	    // the tags hang at the bottom of the ruler (see paint)
  4138	    if (y < rulerTop + timelineHeight - 12 || y >= headerHeight)
  4139	        return -1;
  4140	    const auto& list = engine.getSong().markers();
  4141	    const auto f = theme::font (theme::type::caption, true);
  4142	    for (int i = (int) list.size(); --i >= 0;) // the later tag is drawn on top
  4143	    {
  4144	        const auto mx = tickToX ((double) list[(std::size_t) i].tick);
  4145	        const auto width = (double) juce::GlyphArrangement::getStringWidthInt (f, markerLabel (list[(std::size_t) i])) + 8.0;
  4146	        if (x >= mx - 2.0 && x <= mx + width)
  4147	            return i;
  4148	    }
  4149	    return -1;
  4150	}
  4151	
  4152	void PlaylistComponent::showRulerMenu (int x)
  4153	{
  4154	    const auto& song = engine.getSong();
  4155	    const auto tick = juce::jmax<std::int64_t> (0, snap (xToTick (x), true));
  4156	
  4157	    // marker near the click (up to 6 px)
  4158	    int near = -1;
  4159	    for (int i = 0; i < (int) song.markers().size(); ++i)
  4160	        if (std::abs (tickToX ((double) song.markers()[(std::size_t) i].tick) - x) <= 6.0)
  4161	            near = i;
  4162	
  4163	    juce::PopupMenu menu;
  4164	    menu.addItem (tr ("Add marker here..."), [this, tick] { editMarkerName (tick, -1); });
  4165	
  4166	    juce::PopupMenu signatures;
  4167	    for (auto [n, d] : { std::pair { 2, 4 }, { 3, 4 }, { 4, 4 }, { 5, 4 }, { 6, 4 }, { 7, 4 }, { 6, 8 }, { 7, 8 }, { 9, 8 }, { 12, 8 } })
  4168	        signatures.addItem (juce::String (n) + "/" + juce::String (d), [this, tick, n = n, d = d] {
  4169	            engine.editSong ([tick, n, d] (Song& s) { s.addMarker ({ tick, {}, n, d }); });
  4170	        });
  4171	    menu.addSubMenu (tr ("Time signature change here"), signatures);
  4172	    // FL's marker actions "Start recording" / "Stop recording" (punch in / out; Record menu › Enable recording markers)
  4173	    menu.addItem (tr ("Add a Start recording marker here"), [this, tick] {
  4174	        engine.editSong ([tick] (Song& s) {
  4175	            Marker m;
  4176	            m.tick = tick;
  4177	            m.name = tr ("Start recording").toStdString();
  4178	            m.action = Marker::punchIn;
  4179	            s.addMarker (m);
  4180	        });
  4181	    });
  4182	    menu.addItem (tr ("Add a Stop recording marker here"), [this, tick] {
  4183	        engine.editSong ([tick] (Song& s) {
  4184	            Marker m;
  4185	            m.tick = tick;
  4186	            m.name = tr ("Stop recording").toStdString();
  4187	            m.action = Marker::punchOut;
  4188	            s.addMarker (m);
  4189	        });
  4190	    });
  4191	    // FL's "Start" marker: the song starts there; in Performance mode the clips before it are the performance zone
  4192	    menu.addItem (tr ("Add a Start marker here (the song starts here; Performance mode launches the clips before it)"), [this, tick] {
  4193	        engine.editSong ([tick] (Song& s) {
  4194	            Marker m;
  4195	            m.tick = tick;
  4196	            m.name = tr ("Start").toStdString();
  4197	            m.action = Marker::songStart;
  4198	            s.addMarker (m);
  4199	        });
  4200	    });
  4201	    if (song.performanceZoneEnd() > 0 && tick < song.performanceZoneEnd())
  4202	    {
  4203	        // a marker of the zone: launch every clip of its column (from this marker to the next one)
  4204	        std::int64_t from = 0, to = song.performanceZoneEnd();
  4205	        for (const auto& m : song.markers())
  4206	        {
  4207	            if (m.tick <= tick)
  4208	                from = juce::jmax (from, m.tick);
  4209	            else
  4210	                to = juce::jmin (to, m.tick);
  4211	        }
  4212	        menu.addItem (tr ("Launch this column's clips"), engine.getTransport().playing.load(), false, [this, from, to] {
  4213	            perf::Command c;
  4214	            c.type = perf::Command::Type::launchColumn;
  4215	            c.columnStart = from;
  4216	            c.columnEnd = to;
  4217	            engine.performanceCommand (c);
  4218	        });
  4219	        menu.addItem (tr ("Stop all performance clips"), engine.getTransport().playing.load(), false, [this] {
  4220	            perf::Command c;
  4221	            c.type = perf::Command::Type::stopAll;
  4222	            engine.performanceCommand (c);
  4223	        });
  4224	    }
  4225	
  4226	    // FL's Playlist menu › Time markers (REA_rodada165f-markers §1.4)
  4227	    {
  4228	        juce::PopupMenu timeMarkers;
  4229	        juce::PopupMenu every;
  4230	        for (auto [bars, text] : { std::pair { 1, TR_NOOP ("Bar") }, { 2, TR_NOOP ("2 bars") }, { 4, TR_NOOP ("4 bars") }, { 8, TR_NOOP ("8 bars") } })
  4231	            every.addItem (tr (text), [this, bars = bars] {
  4232	                engine.editSong ([bars] (Song& s) { markers::addEvery (s, bars); });
  4233	            });
  4234	        timeMarkers.addItem (tr ("Add one...") + "\tAlt+T", [this] { runAction ("playlist.markerAdd"); });
  4235	        timeMarkers.addItem (tr ("Add auto") + "\tCtrl+T", [this] { runAction ("playlist.markerAuto"); });
  4236	        timeMarkers.addSubMenu (tr ("Add one every"), every);
  4237	        timeMarkers.addItem (tr ("Add jump to next bar"), [this, tick] {
  4238	            engine.editSong ([tick] (Song& s) { markers::addJumpToNextBar (s, tick); });
  4239	        });
  4240	        timeMarkers.addItem (tr ("Add time signature change...") + "\tShift+Alt+T", [this] { runAction ("playlist.markerSignature"); });
  4241	        timeMarkers.addItem (tr ("Place loop") + "\tShift+T", [this] { runAction ("playlist.markerLoop"); });
  4242	        timeMarkers.addItem (tr ("Change to marker loop"), song.hasLoop(), false, [this] {
  4243	            engine.editSong ([] (Song& s) { markers::changeToMarkerLoop (s); });
  4244	        });
  4245	        menu.addSubMenu (tr ("Time markers"), timeMarkers);
  4246	
  4247	        juce::PopupMenu quantize;
  4248	        const auto current = engine.quantizeSongJumps.load();
  4249	        for (auto [value, text] : { std::pair { 0, TR_NOOP ("Off") }, { 1, TR_NOOP ("Beats") }, { 2, TR_NOOP ("Bars") }, { 3, TR_NOOP ("2 bars") },
  4250	                                    { 4, TR_NOOP ("4 bars") }, { 5, TR_NOOP ("8 bars") } })
  4251	            quantize.addItem (tr (text), true, current == value, [this, value = value] { engine.quantizeSongJumps.store (value); });
  4252	        menu.addSubMenu (tr ("Quantize song jumps"), quantize);
  4253	    }
  4254	
  4255	    if (near >= 0)
  4256	    {
  4257	        menu.addSeparator();
  4258	        menu.addSectionHeader (markerLabel (song.markers()[(std::size_t) near]));
  4259	        menu.addItem (tr ("Rename marker..."), [this, near] { editMarkerName (0, near); });
  4260	        menu.addItem (tr ("Place loop (from this marker to the next)"), [this, near] {
  4261	            engine.editSong ([near] (Song& s) {
  4262	                if (near >= (int) s.markers().size())
  4263	                    return;
  4264	                const auto from = s.markers()[(std::size_t) near].tick;
  4265	                std::int64_t to = s.songLength();
  4266	                for (const auto& m : s.markers())
  4267	                    if (m.tick > from)
  4268	                    {
  4269	                        to = m.tick;
  4270	                        break;
  4271	                    }
  4272	                if (to > from)
  4273	                {
  4274	                    s.loopStart = from;
  4275	                    s.loopEnd = to;
  4276	                }
  4277	            }, false);
  4278	        });
  4279	        {
  4280	            // FL's marker "Action" list, in FL's order (the values are FL's .flp marker types)
  4281	            juce::PopupMenu action;
  4282	            const auto& marker = song.markers()[(std::size_t) near];
  4283	            const auto current = marker.isSignature() ? Marker::timeSignature : marker.action;
  4284	            // (FL's Playlist shows these 9; Pattern length, Bar override and Key/Scale are its Piano Roll markers' actions)
  4285	            for (auto [value, text] : { std::pair { Marker::none, TR_NOOP ("None (plain marker)") }, { Marker::start, TR_NOOP ("Start") },
  4286	                                        { Marker::songLoop, TR_NOOP ("Loop") }, { Marker::markerLoop, TR_NOOP ("Marker loop") },
  4287	                                        { Marker::markerSkip, TR_NOOP ("Marker skip") }, { Marker::markerPause, TR_NOOP ("Marker pause") },
  4288	                                        { Marker::timeSignature, TR_NOOP ("Time signature") }, { Marker::punchIn, TR_NOOP ("Start recording") },
  4289	                                        { Marker::punchOut, TR_NOOP ("Stop recording") } })
  4290	                action.addItem (tr (text), true, current == value, [this, near, value = value] {
  4291	                    engine.editSong ([near, value] (Song& s) {
  4292	                        if (near < (int) s.markers().size())
  4293	                            markers::setAction (s, s.markers()[(std::size_t) near], value);
  4294	                    });
  4295	                });
  4296	            menu.addSubMenu (tr ("Marker action"), action);
  4297	        }
  4298	        menu.addItem (tr ("Remove marker"), [this, near] {
  4299	            engine.editSong ([near] (Song& s) {
  4300	                if (near < (int) s.markers().size())
  4301	                    s.markers().erase (s.markers().begin() + near);
  4302	            });
  4303	        });
  4304	    }
  4305	
  4306	    if (song.hasLoop())
  4307	    {
  4308	        menu.addSeparator();
  4309	        menu.addItem (tr ("Remove loop"), [this] { engine.editSong ([] (Song& s) { s.loopStart = s.loopEnd = 0; }, false); });
  4310	    }
  4311	
  4312	    menu.addSeparator();
  4313	    {
  4314	        // FL's Select submenu (REA round 153)
  4315	        juce::PopupMenu select;
  4316	        const auto overlapping = clipops::overlappingClips (song), stacked = clipops::stackedClips (song), muted = clipops::mutedClips (song);
  4317	        select.addItem (tr ("Overlapping clips ({0})", { juce::String ((int) overlapping.size()) }), ! overlapping.empty(), false,
  4318	                        [this, overlapping] { setSelection (overlapping); });
  4319	        select.addItem (tr ("Stacked clips: hidden duplicates ({0})", { juce::String ((int) stacked.size()) }), ! stacked.empty(), false,
  4320	                        [this, stacked] { setSelection (stacked); });
  4321	        select.addItem (tr ("Muted clips ({0})", { juce::String ((int) muted.size()) }), ! muted.empty(), false, [this, muted] { setSelection (muted); });
  4322	        menu.addSubMenu (tr ("Select"), select);
  4323	    }
  4324	    menu.addItem (tr ("Ripple editing (resizing and deleting move the later clips)") + "\tShift+S", true, ripple, [this] { setRipple (! ripple); });
  4325	    {
  4326	        auto anyAudio = false;
  4327	        for (auto i : selection)
  4328	            anyAudio = anyAudio || (i >= 0 && i < (int) song.playlist.size() && song.playlist[(std::size_t) i].kind == ClipKind::audio);
  4329	        menu.addItem (tr ("Pitch selected audio clips one semitone down") + "\t7", anyAudio, false, [this] { runAction ("playlist.pitchClipsDown"); });
  4330	        menu.addItem (tr ("Pitch selected audio clips one semitone up") + "\t8", anyAudio, false, [this] { runAction ("playlist.pitchClipsUp"); });
  4331	        menu.addItem (tr ("Reverse selected audio clips") + "\t9", anyAudio, false, [this] { runAction ("playlist.reverseClips"); });
  4332	    }
  4333	    menu.addItem (tr ("Automatic crossfades (current project)"), true, engine.getSong().autoCrossfades, [this] { runAction ("playlist.autoCrossfades"); });
  4334	    // FL's legacy Pattern Blocks (round 170f)
  4335	    menu.addItem (tr ("Pattern blocks (current project)"), true, engine.getSong().patternBlocksOn, [this] { runAction ("playlist.patternBlocks"); });
  4336	    menu.addItem (tr ("Turn pattern blocks into clips"), ! engine.getSong().blocks.empty(), false, [this] { runAction ("playlist.blocksToClips"); });
  4337	    {
  4338	        juce::PopupMenu zoom;
  4339	        zoom.addItem (tr ("Zoom in") + "\tPageUp", [this] { runAction ("playlist.zoomIn"); });
  4340	        zoom.addItem (tr ("Zoom out") + "\tPageDown", [this] { runAction ("playlist.zoomOut"); });
  4341	        zoom.addItem (tr ("Zoom: one bar") + "\tShift+1", [this] { runAction ("playlist.zoom1"); });
  4342	        zoom.addItem (tr ("Zoom: four bars") + "\tShift+2", [this] { runAction ("playlist.zoom2"); });
  4343	        zoom.addItem (tr ("Zoom: sixteen bars") + "\tShift+3", [this] { runAction ("playlist.zoom3"); });
  4344	        zoom.addItem (tr ("Zoom to show everything") + "\tShift+4", [this] { runAction ("playlist.zoomAll"); });
  4345	        zoom.addItem (tr ("Zoom to the selection") + "\tShift+5", [this] { runAction ("playlist.zoomSelection"); });
  4346	        zoom.addItem (tr ("Centre the view on the playhead") + "\tShift+0", [this] { runAction ("playlist.centrePlayhead"); });
  4347	        menu.addSubMenu (tr ("Zoom"), zoom);
  4348	    }
  4349	
  4350	    menu.showMenuAsync (juce::PopupMenu::Options().withTargetComponent (this).withMousePosition());
  4351	}
  4352	
  4353	void PlaylistComponent::editMarkerName (std::int64_t tick, int existing)
  4354	{
  4355	    const auto& markers = engine.getSong().markers();
  4356	    const auto current = existing >= 0 && existing < (int) markers.size() ? juce::String::fromUTF8 (markers[(std::size_t) existing].name.c_str())
  4357	                                                                          : tr ("Marker");
  4358	    auto* window = new juce::AlertWindow (existing >= 0 ? tr ("Rename marker") : tr ("New marker"), {}, juce::MessageBoxIconType::NoIcon);
  4359	    window->addTextEditor ("name", current);
  4360	    window->addButton (tr ("OK"), 1, juce::KeyPress (juce::KeyPress::returnKey));
  4361	    window->addButton (tr ("Cancel"), 0, juce::KeyPress (juce::KeyPress::escapeKey));
  4362	    window->enterModalState (true, juce::ModalCallbackFunction::create ([this, tick, existing, window] (int result) {
  4363	                                 if (result != 1)
  4364	                                     return;
  4365	
  4366	                                 const auto name = window->getTextEditorContents ("name").trim().toStdString();
  4367	                                 engine.editSong ([tick, existing, name] (Song& s) {
  4368	                                     if (existing < 0)
  4369	                                         s.addMarker ({ tick, name, 0, 4 });
  4370	                                     else if (existing < (int) s.markers().size())
  4371	                                         s.markers()[(std::size_t) existing].name = name;
  4372	                                 });
  4373	                             }),
  4374	                             true);
  4375	}
  4376	
  4377	void PlaylistComponent::showClipMenu (int index)
  4378	{
  4379	    auto menu = buildClipMenu (index, getMouseXYRelative().x);
  4380	    if (menu.getNumItems() > 0)
  4381	        menu.showMenuAsync (juce::PopupMenu::Options().withTargetComponent (this).withMousePosition());
  4382	}
  4383	
  4384	juce::PopupMenu PlaylistComponent::getClipMenuForSnapshot (int index)
  4385	{
  4386	    if (index < 0 || index >= (int) engine.getSong().playlist.size())
  4387	        return {};
  4388	    const auto& c = engine.getSong().playlist[(std::size_t) index];
  4389	    return buildClipMenu (index, (int) tickToX ((double) c.start + (double) c.length / 2.0));
  4390	}
  4391	
  4392	juce::PopupMenu PlaylistComponent::buildClipMenu (int index, int mouseX)
  4393	{
  4394	    const auto& song = engine.getSong();
  4395	    juce::PopupMenu menu;
  4396	    if (index < 0 || index >= (int) song.playlist.size())
  4397	        return menu;
  4398	
  4399	    const auto clip = song.playlist[(std::size_t) index];
  4400	    const auto targets = isSelected (index) ? selection : std::vector<int> { index };
  4401	    const auto many = targets.size() > 1;
  4402	
  4403	    // --- the source: name, colour, uniqueness -------------------------------------------------------
  4404	    juce::String sourceName;
  4405	    if (clip.kind == ClipKind::pattern && clip.pattern >= 0 && clip.pattern < (int) song.patterns.size())
  4406	        sourceName = juce::String::fromUTF8 (song.patterns[(std::size_t) clip.pattern].name.c_str());
  4407	    else if (clip.kind == ClipKind::audio && clip.channel >= 0 && clip.channel < (int) song.channels.size())
  4408	        sourceName = juce::String::fromUTF8 (song.channels[(std::size_t) clip.channel].name.c_str());
  4409	    else if (clip.kind == ClipKind::automation && clip.automation >= 0 && clip.automation < (int) song.automations.size())
  4410	        sourceName = juce::String::fromUTF8 (song.automations[(std::size_t) clip.automation].name.c_str());
  4411	    {
  4412	        // the title band in the clip's colour
  4413	        auto colour = theme::colour::automation;
  4414	        auto kind = tr ("Automation clip");
  4415	        if (clip.kind == ClipKind::pattern)
  4416	        {
  4417	            colour = patternColour (song, clip.pattern);
  4418	            kind = tr ("Pattern clip");
  4419	        }
  4420	        else if (clip.kind == ClipKind::audio && clip.channel >= 0 && clip.channel < (int) song.channels.size())
  4421	        {
  4422	            colour = audioClipColour (song.channels[(std::size_t) clip.channel]);
  4423	            kind = tr ("Audio clip");
  4424	        }
  4425	        ui::addItemHeader (menu, many ? kind + " (" + juce::String ((int) targets.size()) + ")" : kind, sourceName, colour);
  4426	    }
  4427	    menu.addItem (tr ("Properties..."), [this, index, targets] {
  4428	        showClipProperties (engine, index, targets, [safe = juce::Component::SafePointer<PlaylistComponent> (this)] {
  4429	            if (safe != nullptr)
  4430	                safe->sortClips();
  4431	        });
  4432	    });
  4433	
  4434	    if (clip.kind == ClipKind::pattern && onRenamePattern)
  4435	        menu.addItem (theme::mnemonic (tr ("Rename and color..."), 'R'), [this, p = clip.pattern] { onRenamePattern (p); });
  4436	    else if (clip.kind == ClipKind::audio)
  4437	        menu.addItem (theme::mnemonic (tr ("Rename and color..."), 'R'), [this, ch = clip.channel] {
  4438	            const auto& c = engine.getSong().channels[(std::size_t) ch];
  4439	            showNameAndColourDialog (tr ("Rename audio clip"), juce::String::fromUTF8 (c.name.c_str()), c.colour,
  4440	                                     [this, ch] (const juce::String& name, std::uint32_t colour) {
  4441	                                         if (ch < (int) engine.getSong().channels.size())
  4442	                                             engine.editSong ([ch, n = name.toStdString(), colour] (Song& s) {
  4443	                                                 s.channels[(std::size_t) ch].name = n;
  4444	                                                 s.channels[(std::size_t) ch].colour = colour;
  4445	                                             });
  4446	                                     });
  4447	        });
  4448	
  4449	    // FL's "Change color..." / "Random color" (the clip's source: its pattern, or its audio channel)
  4450	    if (clip.kind != ClipKind::automation)
  4451	    {
  4452	        auto setColour = [this, kind = clip.kind, src = clip.kind == ClipKind::pattern ? clip.pattern : clip.channel] (std::uint32_t colour) {
  4453	            engine.editSong ([kind, src, colour] (Song& s) {
  4454	                if (kind == ClipKind::pattern && src >= 0 && src < (int) s.patterns.size())
  4455	                    s.patterns[(std::size_t) src].colour = colour;
  4456	                else if (kind == ClipKind::audio && src >= 0 && src < (int) s.channels.size())
  4457	                    s.channels[(std::size_t) src].colour = colour;
  4458	            });
  4459	        };
  4460	        const auto current = clip.kind == ClipKind::pattern ? (clip.pattern >= 0 && clip.pattern < (int) song.patterns.size() ? song.patterns[(std::size_t) clip.pattern].colour : 0u)
  4461	                                                            : (clip.channel >= 0 && clip.channel < (int) song.channels.size() ? song.channels[(std::size_t) clip.channel].colour : 0u);
  4462	        menu.addSubMenu (tr ("Change color"), ui::colourMenu (current, setColour, juce::Rectangle<int> (juce::Desktop::getMousePosition().x, juce::Desktop::getMousePosition().y, 1, 1)));
  4463	        menu.addItem (tr ("Random color"), [setColour] {
  4464	            const auto& palette = lynxPalette();
  4465	            setColour (palette[(std::size_t) juce::Random::getSystemRandom().nextInt ((int) palette.size())]);
  4466	        });
  4467	    }
  4468	    menu.addSeparator();
  4469	
  4470	    // FL's "Select source pattern / channel": the clip plays another source (patterns; audio clip channels; automations)
  4471	    {
  4472	        juce::PopupMenu sources;
  4473	        if (clip.kind == ClipKind::pattern)
  4474	            for (int p = 0; p < (int) song.patterns.size(); ++p)
  4475	                sources.addItem (juce::String::fromUTF8 (song.patterns[(std::size_t) p].name.c_str()), true, p == clip.pattern,
  4476	                                 [this, targets, p] { engine.editSong ([targets, p] (Song& s) { clipops::setClipSource (s, targets, ClipKind::pattern, p); }); });
  4477	        else if (clip.kind == ClipKind::audio)
  4478	        {
  4479	            for (int ch = 0; ch < (int) song.channels.size(); ++ch)
  4480	                if (song.channels[(std::size_t) ch].instrument == InstrumentKind::audioClip)
  4481	                    sources.addItem (juce::String::fromUTF8 (song.channels[(std::size_t) ch].name.c_str()), true, ch == clip.channel,
  4482	                                     [this, targets, ch] { engine.editSong ([targets, ch] (Song& s) { clipops::setClipSource (s, targets, ClipKind::audio, ch); }); });
  4483	        }
  4484	        else
  4485	            for (int a = 0; a < (int) song.automations.size(); ++a)
  4486	                sources.addItem (juce::String::fromUTF8 (song.automations[(std::size_t) a].name.c_str()), true, a == clip.automation,
  4487	                                 [this, targets, a] { engine.editSong ([targets, a] (Song& s) { clipops::setClipSource (s, targets, ClipKind::automation, a); }); });
  4488	        menu.addSubMenu (clip.kind == ClipKind::pattern ? tr ("Select source pattern") : tr ("Select source channel"), sources, sources.getNumItems() > 1);
  4489	    }
  4490	    if (clip.kind == ClipKind::audio && clip.channel >= 0 && clip.channel < (int) song.channels.size())
  4491	        menu.addItem (tr ("Channel settings"), [this, ch = clip.channel] { openChannelSettings (engine, ch); });
  4492	
  4493	    if (clip.kind == ClipKind::pattern)
  4494	        menu.addItem (tr ("Make unique") + "\tShift+V+drag", [this, targets] {
  4495	            engine.editSong ([targets] (Song& s) { polish::makeClipsUnique (s, targets); });
  4496	        });
  4497	    else if (clip.kind == ClipKind::audio)
  4498	        menu.addItem (tr ("Make unique"), [this, targets] {
  4499	            engine.editSong ([targets] (Song& s) { clipops::makeAudioUnique (s, targets); });
  4500	        });
  4501	    else if (clip.kind == ClipKind::automation)
  4502	        menu.addItem (tr ("Make unique"), [this, index] {
  4503	            engine.editSong ([index] (Song& s) {
  4504	                auto& c = s.playlist[(std::size_t) index];
  4505	                auto copy = s.automations[(std::size_t) c.automation];
  4506	                copy.name += " #2";
  4507	                s.automations.push_back (std::move (copy));
  4508	                c.automation = (int) s.automations.size() - 1;
  4509	            });
  4510	        });
  4511	
  4512	    menu.addItem (tr ("Select all clips of this source"), [this, index] { setSelection (clipops::sameSource (engine.getSong(), index)); });
  4513	    menu.addSeparator();
  4514	
  4515	    // --- comping (round 163): a take lane's clip onto its comp track (the group header above) ----------
  4516	    if (const auto comp = takes::compTrackOf (song, clip.track); comp >= 0 && clip.kind == ClipKind::audio)
  4517	    {
  4518	        // the loop region where it covers the clip (comping a phrase), else the whole clip
  4519	        const auto useLoop = song.hasLoop() && song.loopStart < clip.end() && song.loopEnd > clip.start;
  4520	        const auto from = useLoop ? std::max (song.loopStart, clip.start) : clip.start;
  4521	        const auto to = useLoop ? std::min (song.loopEnd, clip.end()) : clip.end();
  4522	        menu.addItem (useLoop ? tr ("Promote this take in the loop region to the comp track") : tr ("Promote this take to the comp track"), [this, index, comp, from, to] {
  4523	            setSelection ({});
  4524	            engine.editSong ([index, comp, from, to] (Song& s) { takes::promoteTake (s, index, comp, from, to); });
  4525	            sortClips();
  4526	        });
  4527	        menu.addSeparator();
  4528	    }
  4529	
  4530	    // --- cutting and joining -------------------------------------------------------------------------
  4531	    const auto splitAt = snap (xToTick (mouseX) + gridTicks() / 2.0, true);
  4532	    menu.addItem (tr ("Split here"), splitAt > clip.start && splitAt < clip.end(), false, [this, index, splitAt] {
  4533	        setSelection ({});
  4534	        engine.editSong ([index, splitAt] (Song& s) {
  4535	            if (index < (int) s.playlist.size() && splitAt > s.playlist[(std::size_t) index].start && splitAt < s.playlist[(std::size_t) index].end())
  4536	                s.playlist.push_back (splitClip (s.playlist[(std::size_t) index], splitAt));
  4537	        });
  4538	        sortClips();
  4539	    });
  4540	    {
  4541	        // FL's "Chop" submenu: to the grid, or in bars / beats / parts of a beat
  4542	        juce::PopupMenu chop;
  4543	        auto chopBy = [this, targets] (std::int64_t grid) {
  4544	            setSelection ({});
  4545	            engine.editSong ([targets, grid] (Song& s) {
  4546	                for (auto i : targets) // chopping adds clips at the end: indices below stay valid
  4547	                    noteedit::chopClip (s.playlist, i, grid);
  4548	            });
  4549	            sortClips();
  4550	        };
  4551	        chop.addItem (tr ("Chop to grid"), [chopBy, grid = gridTicks()] { chopBy (grid); });
  4552	        chop.addSeparator();
  4553	        const auto bar = (std::int64_t) ppq * juce::jmax (1, song.beatsPerBar);
  4554	        const std::pair<const char*, std::int64_t> sizes[] = { { TR_NOOP ("Chop in bars"), bar },
  4555	                                                               { TR_NOOP ("Chop in beats"), (std::int64_t) ppq },
  4556	                                                               { TR_NOOP ("Chop in half beats"), (std::int64_t) ppq / 2 },
  4557	                                                               { TR_NOOP ("Chop in quarter beats"), (std::int64_t) ppq / 4 },
  4558	                                                               { TR_NOOP ("Chop in 8th beats"), (std::int64_t) ppq / 8 } };
  4559	        for (const auto& [label, ticks] : sizes)
  4560	            chop.addItem (tr (label), [chopBy, t = ticks] { chopBy (t); });
  4561	        menu.addSubMenu (tr ("Chop"), chop);
  4562	    }
  4563	    menu.addItem (tr ("Merge clips"), clipops::canMerge (song, targets), false, [this, targets] {
  4564	        setSelection ({});
  4565	        engine.editSong ([targets] (Song& s) { clipops::mergeClips (s, targets); });
  4566	        sortClips();
  4567	    });
  4568	
  4569	    // --- audio ---------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
  4570	    if (clip.kind == ClipKind::audio && clip.channel >= 0 && clip.channel < (int) song.channels.size())
  4571	    {
  4572	        menu.addSeparator();
  4573	        const auto& channel = song.channels[(std::size_t) clip.channel];
  4574	        const auto path = juce::String::fromUTF8 (channel.samplePath.c_str());
  4575	        const auto sample = engine.getSampleLibrary().load (path);
  4576	        const auto frames = sample != nullptr ? (double) sample->frames() : 0.0;
  4577	        const auto rate = sample != nullptr ? sample->sampleRate : 44100.0;
  4578	
  4579	        menu.addItem (tr ("Reverse"), sample != nullptr, channel.sampler.reverse, [this, index, frames, rate] {
  4580	            engine.editSong ([index, frames, rate] (Song& s) {
  4581	                const auto ch = s.playlist[(std::size_t) index].channel;
  4582	                clipops::reverseAudio (s, index, clipops::fileTicks (s.channels[(std::size_t) ch], frames, rate, s.bpm));
  4583	            });
  4584	        });
  4585	
  4586	        menu.addItem (tr ("Extract stems from sample"), sample != nullptr, false,
  4587	                      [this, index] { extractStemsFromClip (engine, index, [this] { sortClips(); }); });
  4588	
  4589	        menu.addItem (tr ("Pitch... (semitones, cents, keep length)"), sample != nullptr, false,
  4590	                      [this, ch = clip.channel] { showSamplePitchDialog (engine, ch); });
  4591	        menu.addItem (tr ("Normalize"), [this, targets] { normalizeClips (targets); });
  4592	
  4593	        juce::PopupMenu gains;
  4594	        for (auto db : { 6.0f, 3.0f, 0.0f, -3.0f, -6.0f, -12.0f })
  4595	        {
  4596	            const auto g = juce::Decibels::decibelsToGain (db);
  4597	            gains.addItem ((db > 0 ? "+" : "") + juce::String (db, 0) + " dB", true, std::abs (clip.gain - g) < 0.01f, [this, targets, g] {
  4598	                engine.editSong ([targets, g] (Song& s) {
  4599	                    for (auto i : targets)
  4600	                        if (i < (int) s.playlist.size() && s.playlist[(std::size_t) i].kind == ClipKind::audio)
  4601	                            s.playlist[(std::size_t) i].gain = g;
  4602	                });
  4603	            });
  4604	        }
  4605	        menu.addSubMenu (tr ("Clip volume"), gains);
  4606	
  4607	        // stretching the channel's file to a whole number of beats at the project tempo
  4608	        juce::PopupMenu fit;
  4609	        const auto beats = tempodetect::fitBeatsOf (sample.get(), song.bpm);
  4610	        auto fitTo = [this, index, frames, rate] (float toBeats, bool keepPitch) {
  4611	            engine.editSong ([index, frames, rate, toBeats, keepPitch] (Song& s) {
  4612	                const auto ch = s.playlist[(std::size_t) index].channel;
  4613	                auto& channel = s.channels[(std::size_t) ch];
  4614	                auto& sampler = channel.sampler;
  4615	                const auto before = clipops::fileTicks (channel, frames, rate, s.bpm);
  4616	                sampler.stretchBeats = toBeats;
  4617	                // "Original speed" keeps a pitched clip's "keep length" (round 163)
  4618	                sampler.keepPitch = keepPitch && (toBeats > 0.0f || sampler.pitch != 0.0f || channel.misc.fineTune != 0.0f);
  4619	                // every clip of the file keeps showing the same audio: offsets and lengths scale with it
  4620	                clipops::rescaleClipsOf (s, ch, before, clipops::fileTicks (channel, frames, rate, s.bpm));
  4621	            });
  4622	            sortClips();
  4623	        };
  4624	        if (beats > 0.0f)
  4625	        {
  4626	            const auto whole = std::abs (beats - std::round (beats)) < 0.005f;
  4627	            const auto label = juce::String (beats, whole ? 0 : 2) + " " + (beats == 1.0f ? tr ("beat") : tr ("beats"));
  4628	            fit.addItem (tr ("Fit to tempo, keep pitch ({0})", { label }), true, channel.sampler.stretchBeats == beats && channel.sampler.keepPitch,
  4629	                         [fitTo, beats] { fitTo (beats, true); });
  4630	            fit.addItem (tr ("Fit to tempo, resample ({0})", { label }), true, channel.sampler.stretchBeats == beats && ! channel.sampler.keepPitch,
  4631	                         [fitTo, beats] { fitTo (beats, false); });
  4632	            fit.addItem (tr ("Half as long"), channel.sampler.stretchBeats > 0.0f, false,
  4633	                         [fitTo, b = channel.sampler.stretchBeats, kp = channel.sampler.keepPitch] { fitTo (juce::jmax (0.25f, b * 0.5f), kp); });
  4634	            fit.addItem (tr ("Twice as long"), channel.sampler.stretchBeats > 0.0f, false,
  4635	                         [fitTo, b = channel.sampler.stretchBeats, kp = channel.sampler.keepPitch] { fitTo (juce::jmin (1024.0f, b * 2.0f), kp); });
  4636	            fit.addSeparator();
  4637	        }
  4638	        // FL's TIME presets (r168s c_f20): the whole file lasts exactly this long
  4639	        for (const auto preset : clipops::timePresetBeats (song.beatsPerBar))
  4640	        {
  4641	            const auto bars = song.beatsPerBar > 0 && preset >= (float) song.beatsPerBar ? preset / (float) song.beatsPerBar : 0.0f;
  4642	            const auto label = bars > 0.0f ? (bars == 1.0f ? tr ("1 bar") : tr ("{0} bars", { juce::String (bars, 0) }))
  4643	                                           : (preset == 1.0f ? tr ("1 beat") : tr ("{0} beats", { juce::String (preset, 0) }));
  4644	            fit.addItem (label, true, channel.sampler.stretchBeats == preset, [fitTo, preset, kp = channel.sampler.keepPitch] { fitTo (preset, kp); });
  4645	        }
  4646	        fit.addSeparator();
  4647	        fit.addItem (tr ("Original speed (no stretch)"), true, channel.sampler.stretchBeats <= 0.0f, [fitTo, kp = channel.sampler.keepPitch] { fitTo (0.0f, kp); });
  4648	        menu.addSubMenu (tr ("Fit to tempo"), fit, sample != nullptr);
  4649	
  4650	        // FL's "Detect tempo" (REA round 153; LYNX's own detector, core/TempoDetect): a range to fold the result
  4651	        // into, then fit the clip to the project tempo or take its tempo for the project
  4652	        {
  4653	            juce::PopupMenu detect;
  4654	            struct Range { double low, high; };
  4655	            for (const auto r : { Range { 50.0, 100.0 }, Range { 75.0, 150.0 }, Range { 100.0, 200.0 }, Range { 150.0, 300.0 } }) // FL's ranges (r168s)
  4656	                detect.addItem (tr ("{0}-{1} BPM", { juce::String (r.low, 0), juce::String (r.high, 0) }), [this, sample, fitTo, r] {
  4657	                    if (sample == nullptr)
  4658	                        return;
  4659	                    tempodetect::Options options;
  4660	                    options.minBpm = r.low;
  4661	                    options.maxBpm = r.high;
  4662	                    const auto found = tempodetect::detect (sample->leftData(), sample->isStereo() ? sample->rightData() : nullptr,
  4663	                                                            sample->frames(), sample->sampleRate, options);
  4664	                    if (found.bpm <= 0.0)
  4665	                    {
  4666	                        juce::AlertWindow::showMessageBoxAsync (juce::MessageBoxIconType::InfoIcon, tr ("Detect tempo"),
  4667	                                                                tr ("No steady tempo was found in this sample."));
  4668	                        return;
  4669	                    }
  4670	                    const auto songBpm = engine.getSong().bpm;
  4671	                    auto* window = new juce::AlertWindow (tr ("Detect tempo"),
  4672	                                                          tr ("Detected tempo: {0} BPM ({1} beats in the sample). The project plays at {2} BPM.",
  4673	                                                              { juce::String (found.bpm, 2), juce::String (found.beats, 2), juce::String (songBpm, 2) }),
  4674	                                                          juce::MessageBoxIconType::NoIcon);
  4675	                    window->addButton (tr ("Fit the clip to the project tempo"), 1);
  4676	                    window->addButton (tr ("Use it as the project tempo"), 2);
  4677	                    window->addButton (tr ("Use it, rounded to a whole BPM"), 3);
  4678	                    window->addButton (tr ("Cancel"), 0, juce::KeyPress (juce::KeyPress::escapeKey));
  4679	                    window->enterModalState (true, juce::ModalCallbackFunction::create ([this, found, fitTo] (int result) {
  4680	                                                 if (result == 1)
  4681	                                                     fitTo ((float) found.beats, true); // exact (issue #32 #7: was rounded to 1/4 beat)
  4682	                                                 else if (result == 2 || result == 3)
  4683	                                                 {
  4684	                                                     const auto bpm = juce::jlimit (minTempo, maxTempo, result == 3 ? std::round (found.bpm) : found.bpm);
  4685	                                                     engine.editSong ([bpm] (Song& s) { s.bpm = bpm; });
  4686	                                                 }
  4687	                                             }),
  4688	                                             true);
  4689	                });
  4690	            menu.addSubMenu (tr ("Detect tempo"), detect, sample != nullptr);
  4691	        }
  4692	
  4693	        if (clip.fadeIn > 0 || clip.fadeOut > 0)
  4694	            menu.addItem (tr ("Remove fades"), [this, targets] {
  4695	                engine.editSong ([targets] (Song& s) {
  4696	                    for (auto i : targets)
  4697	                        if (i < (int) s.playlist.size())
  4698	                            s.playlist[(std::size_t) i].fadeIn = s.playlist[(std::size_t) i].fadeOut = 0;
  4699	                });
  4700	            });
  4701	
  4702	        menu.addItem (tr ("Open in a new Sampler channel"), sample != nullptr, false, [this, ch = clip.channel] {
  4703	            if (onOpenInSampler)
  4704	                onOpenInSampler (ch);
  4705	        });
  4706	        menu.addItem (tr ("Edit in the audio editor..."), sample != nullptr, false, [this, ch = clip.channel] { openAudioEditorForChannel (engine, ch); });
  4707	        menu.addItem (tr ("Show the file in Explorer"), juce::File (path).existsAsFile(), false, [path] { juce::File (path).revealToUser(); });
  4708	    }
  4709	
  4710	    // --- pattern -------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
  4711	    if (clip.kind == ClipKind::pattern)
  4712	    {
  4713	        menu.addSeparator();
  4714	        menu.addItem (tr ("Open in Channel Rack"), [this, p = clip.pattern] {
  4715	            engine.editSong ([p] (Song& s) { if (p < (int) s.patterns.size()) s.currentPattern = p; }, false);
  4716	            if (onOpenPattern)
  4717	                onOpenPattern();
  4718	        });
  4719	        menu.addItem (tr ("Open in Piano Roll"), [this, p = clip.pattern] {
  4720	            engine.editSong ([p] (Song& s) { if (p < (int) s.patterns.size()) s.currentPattern = p; }, false);
  4721	            if (onOpenPianoRoll)
  4722	                onOpenPianoRoll();
  4723	        });
  4724	    }
  4725	
  4726	    // --- automation ----------------------------------------------------------------------------------
  4727	    if (clip.kind == ClipKind::automation && clip.automation >= 0 && clip.automation < (int) song.automations.size())
  4728	    {
  4729	        menu.addSeparator();
  4730	        menu.addItem (tr ("Edit points..."), [this, a = clip.automation] { openAutomationEditor (engine, a); });
  4731	        menu.addItem (tr ("Delete automation (all of its clips)"), [this, a = clip.automation] {
  4732	            setSelection ({});
  4733	            engine.editSong ([a] (Song& s) { s.removeAutomation (a); });
  4734	        });
  4735	    }
  4736	
  4737	    // --- mute and the clipboard ----------------------------------------------------------------------
  4738	    menu.addSeparator();
  4739	    menu.addItem (clip.muted ? tr ("Unmute") : tr ("Mute"), [this, targets, m = ! clip.muted] {
  4740	        engine.editSong ([targets, m] (Song& s) {
  4741	            for (auto i : targets)
  4742	                if (i < (int) s.playlist.size())
  4743	                    s.playlist[(std::size_t) i].muted = m;
  4744	        });
  4745	    });
  4746	    menu.addItem (theme::mnemonic (tr ("Cut"), 't') + "\tCtrl+X", [this, targets] {
  4747	        setSelection (targets);
  4748	        copySelection();
  4749	        deleteSelection();
  4750	    });
  4751	    menu.addItem (theme::mnemonic (tr ("Copy"), 'C') + "\tCtrl+C", [this, targets] {
  4752	        setSelection (targets);
  4753	        copySelection();
  4754	    });
  4755	    menu.addItem (theme::mnemonic (tr ("Paste here"), 'P') + "\tCtrl+V", ! clipboard.empty(), false, [this, at = snap (xToTick (mouseX), true)] { pasteClips (at); });
  4756	    menu.addItem (theme::mnemonic (many ? tr ("Delete selected") : tr ("Delete"), 'D') + "\tDel", [this, targets] {
  4757	        setSelection (targets);
  4758	        deleteSelection();
  4759	    });
  4760	
  4761	    return menu;
  4762	}
  4763	
  4764	void PlaylistComponent::normalizeClips (const std::vector<int>& targets)
  4765	{
  4766	    // clip gain so that the peak of the played segment reaches 0 dB (at most +24 dB)
  4767	    const auto& song = engine.getSong();
  4768	    std::vector<std::pair<int, float>> gainsFor;
  4769	
  4770	    for (auto i : targets)
  4771	    {
  4772	        if (i < 0 || i >= (int) song.playlist.size())
  4773	            continue;
  4774	        const auto& c = song.playlist[(std::size_t) i];
  4775	
  4776	        if (c.kind != ClipKind::audio || c.channel < 0 || c.channel >= (int) song.channels.size())
  4777	            continue;
  4778	
  4779	        const auto& channel = song.channels[(std::size_t) c.channel];
  4780	        const auto sample = engine.getSampleLibrary().load (juce::String::fromUTF8 (channel.samplePath.c_str()));
  4781	
  4782	        if (sample == nullptr || sample->frames() == 0)
  4783	            continue;
  4784	
  4785	        const auto total = (double) sample->frames();
  4786	        const auto perTick = clipops::framesPerTick (channel, c, total, sample->sampleRate, song.bpm);
  4787	        auto from = juce::jlimit (0.0, total, (double) c.offset * perTick);
  4788	        auto to = juce::jlimit (from, total, (double) (c.offset + c.length) * perTick);
  4789	        if (clipops::playsBackwards (channel, c)) // the clip's offset counts from the file's end
  4790	        {
  4791	            const auto f = total - to, t = total - from;
  4792	            from = f;
  4793	            to = t;
  4794	        }
  4795	        float peak = 0.0f;
  4796	
  4797	        for (auto* data : { sample->leftData(), sample->rightData() })
  4798	            if (data != nullptr)
  4799	                for (auto f = (std::size_t) from; f < (std::size_t) to; ++f)
  4800	                    peak = juce::jmax (peak, std::abs (data[f]));
  4801	
  4802	        if (peak > 1.0e-6f)
  4803	            gainsFor.push_back ({ i, juce::jmin (1.0f / peak, juce::Decibels::decibelsToGain (24.0f)) });
  4804	    }
  4805	
  4806	    if (! gainsFor.empty())
  4807	        engine.editSong ([gainsFor] (Song& s) {
  4808	            for (auto [i, g] : gainsFor)
  4809	                if (i < (int) s.playlist.size())
  4810	                    s.playlist[(std::size_t) i].gain = g;
  4811	        });
  4812	}
  4813	
  4814	void PlaylistComponent::visibilityChanged()
  4815	{
  4816	    // hidden while a clip preview or the Listen tool is sounding (no mouse up will come here): stop it
  4817	    if (! isShowing() && (drag == Drag::preview || drag == Drag::listen))
  4818	    {
  4819	        if (drag == Drag::preview)
  4820	            stopPreview();
  4821	        else
  4822	            engine.stop();
  4823	        drag = Drag::none;
  4824	    }
  4825	}
  4826	
  4827	void PlaylistComponent::startPreview (int clip, int x)
  4828	{
  4829	    const auto& song = engine.getSong();
  4830	
  4831	    if (clip < 0 || clip >= (int) song.playlist.size())
  4832	        return;
  4833	
  4834	    const auto c = song.playlist[(std::size_t) clip];
  4835	    previewPositionBefore = engine.getTransport().positionTicks.load();
  4836	    previewPatternBefore = -1;
  4837	    previewRestoreSong = false;
  4838	    drag = Drag::preview;
  4839	    const auto at = juce::jlimit ((double) c.start, (double) c.end(), xToTick (x));
  4840	
  4841	    if (c.kind == ClipKind::pattern && c.pattern >= 0 && c.pattern < (int) song.patterns.size())
  4842	    {
  4843	        // a pattern clip plays its pattern alone, from the clicked point
  4844	        const auto length = (double) juce::jmax<std::int64_t> (1, song.patterns[(std::size_t) c.pattern].effectiveLength (song.beatsPerBar));
  4845	        previewRestoreSong = song.mode == PlayMode::song;
  4846	        previewPatternBefore = song.currentPattern;
  4847	        engine.editSong ([p = c.pattern] (Song& s) {
  4848	            s.mode = PlayMode::pattern;
  4849	            s.currentPattern = p;
  4850	        }, false);
  4851	        engine.seek (std::fmod (at - (double) c.start + (double) c.offset, length));
  4852	    }
  4853	    else
  4854	    {
  4855	        if (song.mode != PlayMode::song)
  4856	            engine.editSong ([] (Song& s) { s.mode = PlayMode::song; }, false);
  4857	        engine.seek (at);
  4858	    }
  4859	
  4860	    engine.play();
  4861	    setMouseCursor (editorCursor (gesture::Cursor::listen));
  4862	    ui::postHint ({ tr ("Previewing the clip: release the mouse to stop"), {}, false, false }, 60000);
  4863	}
  4864	
  4865	void PlaylistComponent::stopPreview()
  4866	{
  4867	    engine.stop();
  4868	
  4869	    if (previewPatternBefore >= 0)
  4870	        engine.editSong ([this] (Song& s) {
  4871	            if (previewPatternBefore < (int) s.patterns.size())
  4872	                s.currentPattern = previewPatternBefore;
  4873	            if (previewRestoreSong)
  4874	                s.mode = PlayMode::song;
  4875	        }, false);
  4876	
  4877	    engine.seek (previewPositionBefore);
  4878	    previewPatternBefore = -1;
  4879	    ui::clearPostedHint();
  4880	}
  4881	
  4882	void PlaylistComponent::setRipple (bool on)
  4883	{
  4884	    ripple = on;
  4885	    repaint();
  4886	}
  4887	
  4888	void PlaylistComponent::rippleDeleteSelection()
  4889	{
  4890	    if (selection.empty())
  4891	        return;
  4892	
  4893	    auto doomed = selection;
  4894	    setSelection ({});
  4895	    engine.editSong ([&doomed] (Song& s) { noteedit::rippleDelete (s.playlist, doomed); });
  4896	}
  4897	
  4898	void PlaylistComponent::zoomAroundCentre (double factor)
  4899	{
  4900	    const auto grid = gridArea();
  4901	    const auto centre = xToTick (grid.getCentreX());
  4902	    pixelsPerTick = juce::jlimit (4.0 / (4 * ppq), 800.0 / (4 * ppq), pixelsPerTick * factor);
  4903	    scrollTicks = juce::jmax (0.0, centre - (grid.getCentreX() - headerWidth) / pixelsPerTick);
  4904	    updateScrollBars();
  4905	    repaint();
  4906	}
  4907	
  4908	void PlaylistComponent::zoomToBars (int bars)
  4909	{
  4910	    const auto bar = (double) ppq * juce::jmax (1, engine.getSong().beatsPerBar);
  4911	    const auto from = std::floor (scrollTicks / bar) * bar;
  4912	    zoomToTicks (from, from + bars * bar);
  4913	}
  4914	
  4915	void PlaylistComponent::zoomAll()
  4916	{
  4917	    zoomToTicks (0.0, (double) engine.getSong().songLength());
  4918	}
  4919	
  4920	void PlaylistComponent::zoomToSelection()
  4921	{
  4922	    const auto& song = engine.getSong();
  4923	
  4924	    if (song.hasLoop() && selection.empty())
  4925	        return zoomToTicks ((double) song.loopStart, (double) song.loopEnd);
  4926	
  4927	    if (selection.empty())
  4928	        return zoomAll();
  4929	
  4930	    std::int64_t from = INT64_MAX, to = 0;
  4931	    int top = 1 << 20;
  4932	    for (auto i : selection)
  4933	        if (i >= 0 && i < (int) song.playlist.size())
  4934	        {
  4935	            from = juce::jmin (from, song.playlist[(std::size_t) i].start);
  4936	            to = juce::jmax (to, song.playlist[(std::size_t) i].end());
  4937	            top = juce::jmin (top, song.playlist[(std::size_t) i].track);
  4938	        }
  4939	
  4940	    zoomToTicks ((double) from, (double) to);
  4941	    scrollY = juce::jmax (0, trackToY (top) - headerHeight + scrollY);
  4942	    updateScrollBars();
  4943	    repaint();
  4944	}
  4945	
  4946	void PlaylistComponent::centreOnPlayhead()
  4947	{
  4948	    const auto tick = engine.songPlayheadTicks();
  4949	    scrollTicks = juce::jmax (0.0, tick - gridArea().getWidth() / pixelsPerTick / 2.0);
  4950	    updateScrollBars();
  4951	    repaint();
  4952	}
  4953	
  4954	bool PlaylistComponent::runAction (const std::string& id)
  4955	{
  4956	    auto is = [&id] (const char* name) { return id == name; };
  4957	
  4958	    if (is ("playlist.zoomIn")) zoomAroundCentre (1.25);
  4959	    else if (is ("playlist.zoomOut")) zoomAroundCentre (1.0 / 1.25);
  4960	    else if (is ("playlist.zoom1")) zoomToBars (1);
  4961	    else if (is ("playlist.zoom2")) zoomToBars (4);
  4962	    else if (is ("playlist.zoom3")) zoomToBars (16);
  4963	    else if (is ("playlist.zoomAll")) zoomAll();
  4964	    else if (is ("playlist.zoomSelection")) zoomToSelection();
  4965	    else if (is ("playlist.centrePlayhead")) centreOnPlayhead();
  4966	    else if (is ("playlist.rippleDelete")) rippleDeleteSelection();
  4967	    else if (is ("playlist.ripple")) setRipple (! ripple);
  4968	    else if (is ("playlist.autoCrossfades")) engine.editSong ([] (Song& s) { s.autoCrossfades = ! s.autoCrossfades; });
  4969	    else if (is ("playlist.patternBlocks")) togglePatternBlocks();
  4970	    else if (is ("playlist.blocksToClips")) turnBlocksIntoClips();
  4971	    else if (is ("playlist.follow")) EditorGlobals::get().setFollowing (false, ! EditorGlobals::get().isFollowing (false));
  4972	    else if (is ("playlist.selectTimeAround")) selectTimeAroundSelection();
  4973	    else if (is ("playlist.timePrevious")) moveTimeSelection (-1);
  4974	    else if (is ("playlist.timeNext")) moveTimeSelection (1);
  4975	    else if (is ("playlist.markerAdd")) addMarkerByKey();
  4976	    else if (is ("playlist.markerAuto")) addAutoMarker();
  4977	    else if (is ("playlist.markerSignature")) addSignatureChangeByKey();
  4978	    else if (is ("playlist.markerLoop")) placeLoopMarker();
  4979	    else if (is ("playlist.pitchClipsDown")) pitchSelectedAudioClips (-1);
  4980	    else if (is ("playlist.pitchClipsUp")) pitchSelectedAudioClips (1);
  4981	    else if (is ("playlist.reverseClips")) reverseSelectedAudioClips();
  4982	    else if (is ("playlist.quantizeStarts")) quantizeClips (1.0);
  4983	    else if (is ("playlist.quantizeHalf")) quantizeClips (0.5);
  4984	    else if (is ("playlist.performanceMode")) togglePerformanceMode();
  4985	    else if (is ("playlist.zoomPerformance")) zoomOnPerformanceClips();
  4986	    else if (is ("playlist.perfClipProgress")) { showClipProgress = ! showClipProgress; repaint(); }
  4987	    else if (is ("playlist.perfTrackProgress")) { showTrackProgress = ! showTrackProgress; repaint(); }
  4988	    else if (is ("playlist.muteClips") || is ("playlist.unmuteClips"))
  4989	    {
  4990	        // FL limits it to the time selection when there is one
  4991	        const auto& song = engine.getSong();
  4992	        const auto from = song.hasLoop() ? song.loopStart : (std::int64_t) 0;
  4993	        const auto to = song.hasLoop() ? song.loopEnd : std::numeric_limits<std::int64_t>::max();
  4994	        engine.editSong ([chosen = selection, mute = is ("playlist.muteClips"), from, to] (Song& s) { clipops::setClipsMuted (s, chosen, mute, from, to); });
  4995	    }
  4996	    else if (is ("playlist.mergePatternClips")) mergePatternClipsByKey();
  4997	    else if (is ("playlist.cloneTracks")) cloneTracksWithDialog (! selectedTracks.empty() ? selectedTracks : std::vector<int> { juce::jmax (0, currentTrack) });
  4998	    else if (is ("playlist.makeUnique")) makeSelectionUnique();
  4999	    else if (is ("playlist.stretchMode")) setStretchOnResize (! EditorGlobals::get().stretchOnResize);
  5000	    else if (is ("playlist.createVariants"))
  5001	    {
  5002	        EditorGlobals::get().createVariants = ! EditorGlobals::get().createVariants;
  5003	        if (EditorGlobals::get().onOptionsChanged)
  5004	            EditorGlobals::get().onOptionsChanged();
  5005	    }
  5006	    else if (is ("playlist.lockAllToContent")) lockAllTracks (true);
  5007	    else if (is ("playlist.unlockAllFromContent")) lockAllTracks (false);
  5008	    else if (is ("playlist.resetEmptyTracks")) resetEmptyTracksAsked();
  5009	    else if (is ("playlist.collapseAllGroups")) engine.editSong ([] (Song& s) { pltracks::setAllGroupsCollapsed (s, true); });
  5010	    else if (is ("playlist.expandAllGroups")) engine.editSong ([] (Song& s) { pltracks::setAllGroupsCollapsed (s, false); });
  5011	    else if (is ("playlist.purgeUnusedAudio"))
  5012	    {
  5013	        setSelection ({});
  5014	        engine.editSong ([] (Song& s) { pltracks::purgeUnusedAudioClips (s); });
  5015	        sortClips();
  5016	    }
  5017	    else
  5018	        return false;
  5019	
  5020	    return true;
  5021	}
  5022	
  5023	namespace
  5024	{
  5025	// FL's Clone window (FUN_012edbe0 [DECOMP]: TaskDialog "Clone" / "What do you want to clone along with the track?",
  5026	// six check boxes, the first four ticked; "Grouped tracks" ticked when a group header is selected)
  5027	class CloneTracksWindow final : public juce::AlertWindow
  5028	{
  5029	public:
  5030	    explicit CloneTracksWindow (bool groupHeaderSelected)
  5031	        : juce::AlertWindow (tr ("Clone"), tr ("What do you want to clone along with the track?"), juce::MessageBoxIconType::QuestionIcon)
  5032	    {
  5033	        const std::pair<juce::ToggleButton*, const char*> boxes[] = {
  5034	            { &patterns, TR_NOOP ("Patterns") }, { &audio, TR_NOOP ("Audio clips") }, { &automations, TR_NOOP ("Automations") },
  5035	            { &effects, TR_NOOP ("Effects") },   { &routing, TR_NOOP ("Mixer track routing") }, { &grouped, TR_NOOP ("Grouped tracks") }
  5036	        };
  5037	        for (const auto& [box, text] : boxes)
  5038	        {
  5039	            box->setButtonText (tr (text));
  5040	            box->setSize (300, 24);
  5041	            addCustomComponent (box);
  5042	        }
  5043	        for (auto* b : { &patterns, &audio, &automations, &effects })
  5044	            b->setToggleState (true, juce::dontSendNotification);
  5045	        grouped.setToggleState (groupHeaderSelected, juce::dontSendNotification);
  5046	        addButton (tr ("OK"), 1, juce::KeyPress (juce::KeyPress::returnKey));
  5047	        addButton (tr ("Cancel"), 0, juce::KeyPress (juce::KeyPress::escapeKey));
  5048	    }
  5049	
  5050	    pltracks::CloneOptions options() const
  5051	    {
  5052	        pltracks::CloneOptions o;
  5053	        o.patterns = patterns.getToggleState();
  5054	        o.audioClips = audio.getToggleState();
  5055	        o.automations = automations.getToggleState();
  5056	        o.effects = effects.getToggleState();
  5057	        o.routing = routing.getToggleState();
  5058	        o.groupedTracks = grouped.getToggleState();
  5059	        return o;
  5060	    }
  5061	
  5062	private:
  5063	    juce::ToggleButton patterns, audio, automations, effects, routing, grouped;
  5064	};
  5065	} // namespace
  5066	
  5067	std::unique_ptr<juce::AlertWindow> PlaylistComponent::makeCloneTracksDialog (bool groupHeaderSelected)
  5068	{
  5069	    return std::make_unique<CloneTracksWindow> (groupHeaderSelected);
  5070	}
  5071	
  5072	void PlaylistComponent::cloneTracksWithDialog (std::vector<int> tracks)
  5073	{
  5074	    const auto& song = engine.getSong();
  5075	    tracks.erase (std::remove_if (tracks.begin(), tracks.end(), [] (int t) { return t < 0; }), tracks.end());
  5076	    if (tracks.empty())
  5077	        return;
  5078	    // FL: at most 250 at once; all empty → "Nothing to clone"
  5079	    if (tracks.size() > 250)
  5080	    {
  5081	        juce::AlertWindow::showMessageBoxAsync (juce::MessageBoxIconType::WarningIcon, tr ("Error"),
  5082	                                                tr ("It isn't possible to clone more than {0} playlist tracks at once. Please select fewer tracks and try again.",
  5083	                                                    { juce::String (250) }));
  5084	        return;
  5085	    }
  5086	    const auto header = std::any_of (tracks.begin(), tracks.end(), [&song] (int t) { return noteedit::isGroupHeader (song, t); });
  5087	    {
  5088	        auto probe = song;
  5089	        if (pltracks::cloneTracks (probe, tracks, {}) < 0)
  5090	        {
  5091	            juce::AlertWindow::showMessageBoxAsync (juce::MessageBoxIconType::InfoIcon, tr ("Nothing to clone"),
  5092	                                                    tr ("All selected playlist tracks are empty. Since empty tracks can't be cloned, there is nothing to do."));
  5093	            return;
  5094	        }
  5095	    }
  5096	    auto* window = new CloneTracksWindow (header);
  5097	    juce::Component::SafePointer<PlaylistComponent> safe (this);
  5098	    window->enterModalState (true, juce::ModalCallbackFunction::create ([safe, window, tracks] (int result) {
  5099	                                 if (result != 1 || safe == nullptr)
  5100	                                     return;
  5101	                                 const auto options = window->options();
  5102	                                 auto run = [safe, tracks, options] {
  5103	                                     if (safe == nullptr)
  5104	                                         return;
  5105	                                     safe->setSelection ({});
  5106	                                     safe->engine.editSong ([tracks, options] (Song& s) {
  5107	                                         pltracks::cloneTracks (s, pltracks::tracksToClone (s, tracks, options.groupedTracks), options);
  5108	                                     });
  5109	                                     safe->sortClips();
  5110	                                     safe->refreshTrackLayout();
  5111	                                     safe->repaint();
  5112	                                 };
  5113	                                 // FL asks before cloning more than four tracks
  5114	                                 if (tracks.size() > 4)
  5115	                                     juce::AlertWindow::showOkCancelBox (juce::MessageBoxIconType::QuestionIcon, tr ("This may take a while"),
  5116	                                                                         tr ("Are you sure you want to clone {0} playlist tracks at once? Cloning multiple tracks may take a long time.",
  5117	                                                                             { juce::String ((int) tracks.size()) }),
  5118	                                                                         tr ("Yes"), tr ("No"), nullptr,
  5119	                                                                         juce::ModalCallbackFunction::create ([run] (int r) { if (r != 0) run(); }));
  5120	                                 else
  5121	                                     run();
  5122	                             }),
  5123	                             true);
  5124	}
  5125	
  5126	void PlaylistComponent::mergePatternClipsByKey()
  5127	{
  5128	    const auto& song = engine.getSong();
  5129	    const auto clips = pltracks::mergeCandidates (song, selection);
  5130	    // FL merges only pattern clips; a selection of other kinds is refused, an unselected playlist uses its pattern clips
  5131	    std::vector<int> patterns;
  5132	    for (const auto i : clips)
  5133	        if (i >= 0 && i < (int) song.playlist.size() && song.playlist[(std::size_t) i].kind == ClipKind::pattern)
  5134	            patterns.push_back (i);
  5135	    if (! selection.empty() && patterns.size() != clips.size())
  5136	    {
  5137	        juce::AlertWindow::showMessageBoxAsync (juce::MessageBoxIconType::WarningIcon, tr ("Can't merge"),
  5138	                                                tr ("The selection contains different clip types, can't merge."));
  5139	        return;
  5140	    }
  5141	    if (patterns.size() < 2)
  5142	    {
  5143	        juce::AlertWindow::showMessageBoxAsync (juce::MessageBoxIconType::WarningIcon, tr ("Error"),
  5144	                                                tr ("Could not find enough valid pattern clips or instances to merge."));
  5145	        return;
  5146	    }
  5147	    int added = -1;
  5148	    setSelection ({});
  5149	    engine.editSong ([patterns, &added] (Song& s) { pltracks::mergePatternClips (s, patterns, &added); });
  5150	    sortClips();
  5151	}
  5152	
  5153	void PlaylistComponent::makeSelectionUnique()
  5154	{
  5155	    if (selection.empty())
  5156	        return;
  5157	    auto run = [this, chosen = selection] {
  5158	        engine.editSong ([chosen] (Song& s) {
  5159	            std::vector<int> patterns, audio;
  5160	            for (const auto i : chosen)
  5161	                if (i >= 0 && i < (int) s.playlist.size())
  5162	                {
  5163	                    const auto kind = s.playlist[(std::size_t) i].kind;
  5164	                    (kind == ClipKind::audio ? audio : patterns).push_back (i);
  5165	                }
  5166	            polish::makeClipsUnique (s, patterns);
  5167	            // each audio source of the selection gets its own channel copy
  5168	            std::vector<int> channels;
  5169	            for (const auto i : audio)
  5170	                if (const auto ch = s.playlist[(std::size_t) i].channel; std::find (channels.begin(), channels.end(), ch) == channels.end())
  5171	                    channels.push_back (ch);
  5172	            for (const auto ch : channels)
  5173	            {
  5174	                std::vector<int> group;
  5175	                for (const auto i : audio)
  5176	                    if (s.playlist[(std::size_t) i].channel == ch)
  5177	                        group.push_back (i);
  5178	                clipops::makeAudioUnique (s, group);
  5179	            }
  5180	        });
  5181	    };
  5182	    // FL (FUN_0123d640): asks from 20 selected clips on
  5183	    if (selection.size() >= 20)
  5184	    {
  5185	        juce::Component::SafePointer<PlaylistComponent> safe (this);
  5186	        juce::AlertWindow::showOkCancelBox (juce::MessageBoxIconType::QuestionIcon, tr ("Make all selected clips unique?"),
  5187	                                            tr ("Are you sure you want to make all {0} selected clips unique?", { juce::String ((int) selection.size()) }),
  5188	                                            tr ("Yes"), tr ("No"), nullptr,
  5189	                                            juce::ModalCallbackFunction::create ([safe, run] (int r) { if (r != 0 && safe != nullptr) run(); }));
  5190	        return;
  5191	    }
  5192	    run();
  5193	}
  5194	
  5195	void PlaylistComponent::lockAllTracks (bool lock)
  5196	{
  5197	    // FL (FUN_011c2190): "Resize tracks?|Also resize all playlist tracks to their minimum / default size?" Yes / No / Cancel
  5198	    juce::Component::SafePointer<PlaylistComponent> safe (this);
  5199	    juce::AlertWindow::showYesNoCancelBox (juce::MessageBoxIconType::QuestionIcon, tr ("Resize tracks?"),
  5200	                                           lock ? tr ("Also resize all playlist tracks to their minimum size?")
  5201	                                                : tr ("Also resize all playlist tracks to their default size?"),
  5202	                                           tr ("Yes"), tr ("No"), tr ("Cancel"), nullptr,
  5203	                                           juce::ModalCallbackFunction::create ([safe, lock] (int result) {
  5204	                                               if (result == 0 || safe == nullptr) // 1 = yes, 2 = no, 0 = cancel
  5205	                                                   return;
  5206	                                               const auto count = safe->numTracks();
  5207	                                               safe->engine.editSong ([count, lock, resize = result == 1] (Song& s) { pltracks::lockAll (s, count, lock, resize); });
  5208	                                               safe->refreshTrackLayout();
  5209	                                               safe->updateScrollBars();
  5210	                                               safe->repaint();
  5211	                                           }));
  5212	}

  5214	void PlaylistComponent::resetEmptyTracksAsked()
  5215	{
  5216	    juce::Component::SafePointer<PlaylistComponent> safe (this);
  5217	    juce::AlertWindow::showOkCancelBox (juce::MessageBoxIconType::QuestionIcon, tr ("Reset empty tracks?"),
  5218	                                        tr ("Reset all empty playlist tracks in the current arrangement?"), tr ("OK"), tr ("Cancel"), nullptr,
  5219	                                        juce::ModalCallbackFunction::create ([safe] (int result) {
  5220	                                            if (result == 0 || safe == nullptr)
  5221	                                                return;
  5222	                                            safe->engine.editSong ([] (Song& s) { pltracks::resetEmptyTracks (s); });
  5223	                                            safe->refreshTrackLayout();
  5224	                                            safe->repaint();
  5225	                                        }));
  5226	}

  5231	    stretchButton.setToggleState (on, juce::dontSendNotification);
