  5236	std::int64_t PlaylistComponent::songPositionTick() const
  5237	{
  5238	    return (std::int64_t) engine.songPlayheadTicks();
  5239	}
  5240	
  5241	void PlaylistComponent::addMarkerByKey()
  5242	{
  5243	    // FL (FUN_01229d20): the song position when it is in view, else the bar line nearest the view's left edge; the
  5244	    // time selection's start wins over both. Then the name dialog.
  5245	    const auto& song = engine.getSong();
  5246	    const auto left = (std::int64_t) std::max (0.0, scrollTicks);
  5247	    const auto right = (std::int64_t) (scrollTicks + gridArea().getWidth() / pixelsPerTick);
  5248	    auto tick = songPositionTick();
  5249	    if (tick < left || tick > right)
  5250	    {
  5251	        const auto bar = song.barAt (left);
  5252	        tick = left - bar.tick > bar.length / 2 ? bar.tick + bar.length : bar.tick;
  5253	    }
  5254	    if (song.hasLoop())
  5255	        tick = song.loopStart;
  5256	    editMarkerName (tick, -1);
  5257	}
  5258	
  5259	void PlaylistComponent::addAutoMarker()
  5260	{
  5261	    // FL (FUN_00e546a0 with FUN_00e43fa0 (snap)): the song position on the grid
  5262	    const auto tick = snap ((double) songPositionTick(), true);
  5263	    for (const auto& m : engine.getSong().markers())
  5264	        if (m.tick == tick)
  5265	            return; // FL keeps the marker that is there (and no undo step)
  5266	    engine.editSong ([tick] (Song& s) { markers::addAuto (s, tick); });
  5267	}
  5268	
  5269	void PlaylistComponent::addSignatureChangeByKey()
  5270	{
  5271	    // FL (FUN_0122bd20): the time selection (start, and its end gets the old signature back), else the song position
  5272	    const auto& song = engine.getSong();
  5273	    const auto from = song.hasLoop() ? song.loopStart : snap ((double) songPositionTick(), true);
  5274	    const auto to = song.hasLoop() ? song.loopEnd : (std::int64_t) -1;
  5275	    const auto current = markers::signatureAt (song, from);
  5276	
  5277	    auto* window = new juce::AlertWindow (tr ("Add time signature change..."), {}, juce::MessageBoxIconType::NoIcon, this);
  5278	    juce::StringArray numerators, denominators;
  5279	    for (int n = 1; n <= 32; ++n)
  5280	        numerators.add (juce::String (n));
  5281	    for (int d : { 1, 2, 4, 8, 16, 32 })
  5282	        denominators.add (juce::String (d));
  5283	    window->addComboBox ("numerator", numerators, tr ("Numerator"));
  5284	    window->addComboBox ("denominator", denominators, tr ("Denominator"));
  5285	    window->getComboBoxComponent ("numerator")->setText (juce::String (current.first), juce::dontSendNotification);
  5286	    window->getComboBoxComponent ("denominator")->setText (juce::String (current.second), juce::dontSendNotification);
  5287	    window->addButton (tr ("OK"), 1, juce::KeyPress (juce::KeyPress::returnKey));
  5288	    window->addButton (tr ("Cancel"), 0, juce::KeyPress (juce::KeyPress::escapeKey));
  5289	    window->enterModalState (true, juce::ModalCallbackFunction::create ([this, from, to, window] (int result) {
  5290	                                 if (result != 1)
  5291	                                     return;
  5292	                                 const auto n = window->getComboBoxComponent ("numerator")->getText().getIntValue();
  5293	                                 const auto d = window->getComboBoxComponent ("denominator")->getText().getIntValue();
  5294	                                 engine.editSong ([from, to, n, d] (Song& s) { markers::addSignatureChange (s, from, to, n, d); });
  5295	                             }),
  5296	                             true);
  5297	}
  5298	
  5299	void PlaylistComponent::selectTimeAroundSelection()
  5300	{
  5301	    // FL (FUN_00eb1b90 → FUN_00e4ea50): the time selection spans the selected clips, or the view when none is
  5302	    // selected; the same span again clears it
  5303	    const auto& song = engine.getSong();
  5304	    std::int64_t from = -1, to = -1;
  5305	    for (auto i : selection)
  5306	        if (i >= 0 && i < (int) song.playlist.size())
  5307	        {
  5308	            const auto& c = song.playlist[(std::size_t) i];
  5309	            from = from < 0 ? c.start : std::min (from, c.start);
  5310	            to = std::max (to, c.end());
  5311	        }
  5312	    if (from < 0)
  5313	    {
  5314	        from = (std::int64_t) std::llround (std::max (0.0, scrollTicks));
  5315	        to = (std::int64_t) std::llround (std::max (0.0, scrollTicks) + gridArea().getWidth() / pixelsPerTick);
  5316	    }
  5317	    const auto same = song.hasLoop() && song.loopStart == from && song.loopEnd == to;
  5318	    engine.editSong ([from, to, same] (Song& s) {
  5319	        s.loopStart = same ? 0 : from;
  5320	        s.loopEnd = same ? 0 : to;
  5321	    }, false);
  5322	    repaint();
  5323	}
  5324	
  5325	void PlaylistComponent::moveTimeSelection (int direction)
  5326	{
  5327	    // FL (FUN_00e43ea0, the action's Tag ±1): the time selection moves by its own length (not before the song's
  5328	    // start) and the song position goes to its start
  5329	    const auto& song = engine.getSong();
  5330	    if (! song.hasLoop())
  5331	        return;
  5332	    const auto length = song.loopEnd - song.loopStart;
  5333	    const auto from = song.loopStart + direction * length;
  5334	    const auto songMode = song.mode == PlayMode::song;
  5335	    if (from < 0)
  5336	        return;
  5337	    engine.editSong ([from, length] (Song& s) {
  5338	        s.loopStart = from;
  5339	        s.loopEnd = from + length;
  5340	    }, false);
  5341	    if (! songMode)
  5342	        engine.editSong ([] (Song& s) { s.mode = PlayMode::song; }, false);
  5343	    engine.jumpTo ((double) from);
  5344	    playheadTick = (double) from;
  5345	    // keep it in view
  5346	    const auto visible = gridArea().getWidth() / pixelsPerTick;
  5347	    if ((double) from < scrollTicks || (double) (from + length) > scrollTicks + visible)
  5348	    {
  5349	        scrollTicks = std::max (0.0, (double) from - visible * 0.1);
  5350	        updateScrollBars();
  5351	    }
  5352	    repaint();
  5353	}
  5354	
  5355	void PlaylistComponent::placeLoopMarker()
  5356	{
  5357	    // FL (FUN_0122bb80): the bar line nearest the view's left edge, or the time selection's start
  5358	    const auto& song = engine.getSong();
  5359	    const auto left = (std::int64_t) std::max (0.0, scrollTicks);
  5360	    const auto bar = song.barAt (left);
  5361	    auto tick = left - bar.tick > bar.length / 2 ? bar.tick + bar.length : bar.tick;
  5362	    if (song.hasLoop())
  5363	        tick = song.loopStart;
  5364	    engine.editSong ([tick] (Song& s) { markers::placeSongLoop (s, tick); });
  5365	}
  5366	
  5367	void PlaylistComponent::pitchSelectedAudioClips (int semitones)
  5368	{
  5369	    // FL (FUN_00e2d7b0): only the selected clips that are audio clips; nothing selected, nothing changes
  5370	    const auto targets = selection;
  5371	    engine.editSong ([targets, semitones] (Song& s) { clipops::pitchAudioClips (s, targets, semitones); });
  5372	    repaint();
  5373	}
  5374	
  5375	void PlaylistComponent::reverseSelectedAudioClips()
  5376	{
  5377	    const auto targets = selection;
  5378	    // each clip's file length in ticks at its own speed, read here (the sample library is not for the edit lambda)
  5379	    std::vector<std::int64_t> ticks;
  5380	    {
  5381	        const auto& song = engine.getSong();
  5382	        for (auto i : targets)
  5383	        {
  5384	            std::int64_t t = 0;
  5385	            if (i >= 0 && i < (int) song.playlist.size())
  5386	                if (const auto& c = song.playlist[(std::size_t) i]; c.kind == ClipKind::audio && c.channel >= 0 && c.channel < (int) song.channels.size())
  5387	                {
  5388	                    const auto& channel = song.channels[(std::size_t) c.channel];
  5389	                    if (const auto sample = engine.getSampleLibrary().load (juce::String::fromUTF8 (channel.samplePath.c_str())); sample != nullptr)
  5390	                        t = clipops::fileTicks (channel, c, (double) sample->frames(), sample->sampleRate, song.bpm);
  5391	                }
  5392	            ticks.push_back (t);
  5393	        }
  5394	    }
  5395	    engine.editSong ([targets, ticks] (Song& s) {
  5396	        clipops::reverseAudioClips (s, targets, [&] (int clip) {
  5397	            for (std::size_t k = 0; k < targets.size(); ++k)
  5398	                if (targets[k] == clip)
  5399	                    return ticks[k];
  5400	            return (std::int64_t) 0;
  5401	        });
  5402	    });
  5403	    repaint();
  5404	}
  5405	
  5406	void PlaylistComponent::quantizeClips (double strength)
  5407	{
  5408	    std::vector<int> targets = selection;
  5409	
  5410	    if (targets.empty())
  5411	        for (int i = 0; i < (int) engine.getSong().playlist.size(); ++i)
  5412	            targets.push_back (i);
  5413	
  5414	    if (targets.empty())
  5415	        return;
  5416	
  5417	    const auto grid = gridTicks();
  5418	    engine.editSong ([&targets, grid, strength] (Song& s) { transportedit::quantizeClipStarts (s.playlist, targets, grid, strength); });
  5419	    sortClips();
  5420	}
  5421	
  5422	void PlaylistComponent::deleteSelection()
  5423	{
  5424	    if (selection.empty())
  5425	        return;
  5426	
  5427	    auto doomed = selection;
  5428	    setSelection ({});
  5429	    engine.editSong ([&doomed] (Song& s) {
  5430	        std::sort (doomed.rbegin(), doomed.rend());
  5431	
  5432	        for (auto i : doomed)
  5433	            if (i >= 0 && i < (int) s.playlist.size())
  5434	                s.playlist.erase (s.playlist.begin() + i);
  5435	    });
  5436	}
  5437	
  5438	void PlaylistComponent::copySelection()
  5439	{
  5440	    clipboard.clear();
  5441	
  5442	    for (auto i : selection)
  5443	        clipboard.push_back (engine.getSong().playlist[(std::size_t) i]);
  5444	}
  5445	
  5446	void PlaylistComponent::pasteClips (std::int64_t at)
  5447	{
  5448	    if (clipboard.empty())
  5449	        return;
  5450	
  5451	    // pastes at `at` (the clip menu's click), else at the playback cursor (or where the first clip was),
  5452	    // keeping tracks and spacing
  5453	    std::int64_t first = clipboard.front().start;
  5454	    for (const auto& c : clipboard)
  5455	        first = juce::jmin (first, c.start);
  5456	
  5457	    const auto target = at >= 0 ? at : playheadTick >= 0.0 ? (std::int64_t) std::llround (playheadTick) : first;
  5458	    std::vector<int> added;
  5459	
  5460	    engine.editSong ([&added, target, first] (Song& s) {
  5461	        for (auto c : clipboard)
  5462	        {
  5463	            if (c.kind == ClipKind::pattern && c.pattern >= (int) s.patterns.size())
  5464	                continue;
  5465	            if (c.kind == ClipKind::audio && c.channel >= (int) s.channels.size())
  5466	                continue;
  5467	            if (c.kind == ClipKind::automation && c.automation >= (int) s.automations.size())
  5468	                continue;
  5469	
  5470	            c.start = c.start - first + target;
  5471	            added.push_back ((int) s.playlist.size());
  5472	            s.playlist.push_back (c);
  5473	        }
  5474	    });
  5475	    setSelection (added);
  5476	    sortClips (added);
  5477	}
  5478	
  5479	void PlaylistComponent::duplicateSelection()
  5480	{
  5481	    const auto& song = engine.getSong();
  5482	
  5483	    // with a time selection: repeats it right after itself (the selected clips that start inside it, or
  5484	    // all the clips that do), and the time selection moves on so the next Ctrl+B continues
  5485	    if (song.hasLoop())
  5486	    {
  5487	        const auto from = song.loopStart, to = song.loopEnd;
  5488	        std::vector<int> sources;
  5489	
  5490	        for (auto i : selection)
  5491	            if (song.playlist[(std::size_t) i].start >= from && song.playlist[(std::size_t) i].start < to)
  5492	                sources.push_back (i);
  5493	
  5494	        std::vector<int> added;
  5495	        engine.editSong ([&] (Song& s) {
  5496	            added = noteedit::repeatTime (s.playlist, from, to, sources);
  5497	            s.loopStart = to;
  5498	            s.loopEnd = to + (to - from);
  5499	        });
  5500	        setSelection (added);
  5501	        sortClips (added);
  5502	        return;
  5503	    }
  5504	
  5505	    if (selection.empty())
  5506	        return;
  5507	
  5508	    const auto bar = (std::int64_t) ppq * juce::jmax (1, song.beatsPerBar);
  5509	    std::int64_t from = INT64_MAX, to = 0;
  5510	
  5511	    for (auto i : selection)
  5512	    {
  5513	        from = juce::jmin (from, song.playlist[(std::size_t) i].start);
  5514	        to = juce::jmax (to, song.playlist[(std::size_t) i].end());
  5515	    }
  5516	
  5517	    const auto offset = juce::jmax (bar, (to - from + bar - 1) / bar * bar); // like FL's Ctrl+B
  5518	    const auto source = selection;
  5519	    std::vector<int> added;
  5520	
  5521	    engine.editSong ([&added, &source, offset] (Song& s) {
  5522	        for (auto i : source)
  5523	        {
  5524	            auto c = s.playlist[(std::size_t) i];
  5525	            c.start += offset;
  5526	            added.push_back ((int) s.playlist.size());
  5527	            s.playlist.push_back (c);
  5528	        }
  5529	    });
  5530	    setSelection (added);
  5531	    sortClips (added);
  5532	}
  5533	
  5534	bool PlaylistComponent::handleKey (const juce::KeyPress& key)
  5535	{
  5536	    const auto mods = key.getModifiers();
  5537	    const auto cmd = mods.isCommandDown();
  5538	    const auto code = key.getKeyCode();
  5539	    const auto letter = keys::letterOfKeyCode (code); // (0 for F-keys, numpad, arrows: issue #12)
  5540	
  5541	    if (code == juce::KeyPress::backspaceKey)
  5542	    {
  5543	        EditorGlobals::get().toggleGrid();
  5544	        return true;
  5545	    }
  5546	
  5547	    // Ctrl+Ins / Ctrl+Del: insert / remove the time selection's length across all tracks
  5548	    if (cmd && ! mods.isShiftDown() && code == juce::KeyPress::insertKey) // (Ctrl+Shift+Ins: pattern.insert)
  5549	    {
  5550	        insertTimeSelection();
  5551	        return true;
  5552	    }
  5553	
  5554	    if (cmd && ! mods.isShiftDown() && code == juce::KeyPress::deleteKey) // (Ctrl+Shift+Del: pattern.delete)
  5555	    {
  5556	        deleteTimeSelection();
  5557	        return true;
  5558	    }
  5559	
  5560	    if (code == juce::KeyPress::deleteKey && ! mods.isShiftDown())
  5561	    {
  5562	        if (ripple)
  5563	            rippleDeleteSelection();
  5564	        else
  5565	            deleteSelection();
  5566	        return true;
  5567	    }
  5568	
  5569	    if (code == juce::KeyPress::escapeKey)
  5570	    {
  5571	        setSelection ({});
  5572	        return true;
  5573	    }
  5574	
  5575	    if (cmd && letter == 'A')
  5576	    {
  5577	        std::vector<int> all ((std::size_t) engine.getSong().playlist.size());
  5578	        for (std::size_t i = 0; i < all.size(); ++i)
  5579	            all[i] = (int) i;
  5580	        setSelection (all);
  5581	        return true;
  5582	    }
  5583	
  5584	    if (cmd && ! mods.isShiftDown() && ! mods.isAltDown() && letter == 'C') // (Ctrl+Shift+C: pattern.clone; Ctrl+Alt+C: consolidate)
  5585	    {
  5586	        copySelection();
  5587	        return true;
  5588	    }
  5589	
  5590	    if (cmd && letter == 'X')
  5591	    {
  5592	        copySelection();
  5593	        deleteSelection();
  5594	        return true;
  5595	    }
  5596	
  5597	    if (cmd && ! mods.isShiftDown() && letter == 'V') // (Ctrl+Shift+V: playlist.makeUnique)
  5598	    {
  5599	        pasteClips();
  5600	        return true;
  5601	    }
  5602	
  5603	    if (cmd && ! mods.isShiftDown() && letter == 'B') // (Ctrl+Shift+B: playlist.cloneTracks)
  5604	    {
  5605	        duplicateSelection();
  5606	        return true;
  5607	    }
  5608	
  5609	    // (the tool letters are key map actions now: runKeyAction, issue #12)
  5610	    return false;
  5611	}
  5612	
  5613	bool PlaylistComponent::runKeyAction (const std::string& id)
  5614	{
  5615	    for (const auto& t : editTools())
  5616	        if (t.tool != EditTool::drumPaint && id == "playlist.tool." + std::string (editToolId (t.tool)))
  5617	        {
  5618	            tools.setTool (t.tool);
  5619	            return true;
  5620	        }
  5621	    return false;
  5622	}
  5623	
  5624	void PlaylistComponent::refreshToolKeys (const std::function<juce::String (const std::string&)>& keyOf)
  5625	{
  5626	    tools.refreshKeys ([&keyOf] (EditTool t) { return keyOf ("playlist.tool." + std::string (editToolId (t))); });
  5627	}
  5628	
  5629	void PlaylistComponent::mouseMove (const juce::MouseEvent& e)
  5630	{
  5631	    updateHover (e.getPosition(), e.mods);
  5632	}
  5633	
  5634	void PlaylistComponent::mouseExit (const juce::MouseEvent&)
  5635	{
  5636	    lastMouse = { -1, -1 };
  5637	
  5638	    if (! (hover == Hover {}))
  5639	    {
  5640	        hover = {};
  5641	        repaint();
  5642	    }
  5643	}
  5644	
  5645	void PlaylistComponent::modifierKeysChanged (const juce::ModifierKeys& mods)
  5646	{
  5647	    if (drag == Drag::none && lastMouse.x >= 0)
  5648	        updateCursor (mods);
  5649	}
  5650	
  5651	juce::Rectangle<int> PlaylistComponent::clipBounds (int index) const
  5652	{
  5653	    const auto& c = engine.getSong().playlist[(std::size_t) index];
  5654	    const auto x0 = (float) tickToX ((double) c.start), x1 = (float) tickToX ((double) c.end());
  5655	    return juce::Rectangle<float> (x0, (float) trackToY (c.track) + 1.0f, juce::jmax (3.0f, x1 - x0 - 1.0f), (float) heightOf (c.track) - 3.0f)
  5656	        .getSmallestIntegerContainer();
  5657	}
  5658	
  5659	juce::Rectangle<int> PlaylistComponent::clipMenuButton (int index) const
  5660	{
  5661	    const auto r = clipBounds (index);
  5662	
  5663	    if (r.getWidth() < 40)
  5664	        return {};
  5665	
  5666	    return { r.getX() + 2, r.getY() + 1, 13, (int) clipTitleHeight - 2 };
  5667	}
  5668	
  5669	PlaylistComponent::Hover PlaylistComponent::hoverAt (juce::Point<int> p) const
  5670	{
  5671	    Hover h;
  5672	
  5673	    if (p.x < headerWidth && p.y >= headerHeight && p.y < gridArea().getBottom())
  5674	    {
  5675	        h.header = true;
  5676	        h.track = yToTrack (p.y);
  5677	        h.divider = dividerAt (p);
  5678	        const auto folded = noteedit::isFoldedAway (engine.getSong(), h.track);
  5679	        h.led = ! folded && h.divider < 0 && trackLed (h.track).expanded (3).contains (p);
  5680	        h.arrow = ! folded && h.divider < 0 && trackArrow (h.track).contains (p);
  5681	        h.arm = ! folded && h.divider < 0 && trackArm (h.track).expanded (2).contains (p);
  5682	        h.input = ! folded && h.divider < 0 && trackInput (h.track).expanded (2).contains (p);
  5683	        return h;
  5684	    }
  5685	
  5686	    if (! gridArea().contains (p))
  5687	        return h;
  5688	
  5689	    h.track = yToTrack (p.y);
  5690	    bool rightEdge = false, leftEdge = false;
  5691	    h.clip = clipAt (p.x, p.y, rightEdge, leftEdge);
  5692	
  5693	    if (h.clip < 0)
  5694	        return h;
  5695	
  5696	    const auto& c = engine.getSong().playlist[(std::size_t) h.clip];
  5697	    h.target = rightEdge ? gesture::Target::rightEdge : leftEdge ? gesture::Target::leftEdge : gesture::Target::item;
  5698	
  5699	    if (clipMenuButton (h.clip).contains (p))
  5700	        h.target = gesture::Target::clipMenu;
  5701	    else if (fadeHandleAt (h.clip, p) != 0)
  5702	        h.target = gesture::Target::fadeHandle;
  5703	    else if (gainHandle (h.clip).expanded (2.0f).contains (p.toFloat()))
  5704	        h.target = gesture::Target::gainHandle;
  5705	    else if (c.kind == ClipKind::automation && automationPointAt (h.clip, p) >= 0)
  5706	        h.target = gesture::Target::autoPoint;
  5707	    else if (c.kind == ClipKind::automation && automationHandleAt (h.clip, p) >= 0)
  5708	        h.target = gesture::Target::autoTension;
  5709	    else if (c.kind == ClipKind::automation && isOnAutomationCurve (h.clip, p))
  5710	        h.target = gesture::Target::autoCurve;
  5711	
  5712	    return h;
  5713	}
  5714	
  5715	void PlaylistComponent::updateHover (juce::Point<int> p, juce::ModifierKeys mods)
  5716	{
  5717	    lastMouse = p;
  5718	    const auto h = hoverAt (p);
  5719	
  5720	    if (! (h == hover))
  5721	    {
  5722	        // repaint the old and new track rows (header and lane)
  5723	        for (auto t : { hover.track, h.track })
  5724	            if (t >= 0)
  5725	                repaint (0, trackToY (t) - 4, getWidth(), heightOf (t) + 8);
  5726	        hover = h;
  5727	    }
  5728	
  5729	    if (const auto onZoom = trackZoomHandle().contains (p); onZoom != trackZoomHover)
  5730	    {
  5731	        trackZoomHover = onZoom;
  5732	        repaint (trackZoomHandle());
  5733	    }
  5734	
  5735	    if (drag == Drag::none)
  5736	        updateCursor (mods);
  5737	}
  5738	
  5739	void PlaylistComponent::updateCursor (juce::ModifierKeys mods)
  5740	{
  5741	    const auto p = lastMouse;
  5742	    auto cursor = gesture::Cursor::normal;
  5743	
  5744	    if ((hover.header && hover.divider >= 0) || trackZoomHandle().contains (p))
  5745	    {
  5746	        setMouseCursor (juce::MouseCursor::UpDownResizeCursor);
  5747	        return;
  5748	    }
  5749	    if (hover.header)
  5750	        cursor = hover.led || hover.arrow || hover.arm || hover.input ? gesture::Cursor::point : gesture::Cursor::normal;
  5751	    else if (gridArea().contains (p))
  5752	    {
  5753	        const gesture::Mods m { mods.isShiftDown(), flmods::ctrl (mods), mods.isAltDown() };
  5754	        const auto tool = tools.getTool();
  5755	        cursor = gesture::cursorFor (gesture::leftAction (tool, hover.target, m, true), tool);
  5756	    }
  5757	    else if (p.y >= rulerTop && p.y < headerHeight && p.x >= headerWidth)
  5758	        cursor = flmods::ctrl (mods) ? gesture::Cursor::select : gesture::Cursor::point;
  5759	
  5760	    setMouseCursor (editorCursor (cursor));
  5761	}
  5762	
  5763	ui::Hint PlaylistComponent::hintAt (juce::Point<int> p)
  5764	{
  5765	    ui::Hint hint;
  5766	    const auto mods = juce::ModifierKeys::currentModifiers;
  5767	    const auto& song = engine.getSong();
  5768	    const auto h = hoverAt (p);
  5769	    auto trackName = [&song] (int t) {
  5770	        return t < (int) song.playlistTracks.size() && ! song.playlistTracks[(std::size_t) t].name.empty()
  5771	                   ? juce::String::fromUTF8 (song.playlistTracks[(std::size_t) t].name.c_str())
  5772	                   : tr ("Track {0}", { juce::String (t + 1) });
  5773	    };
  5774	
  5775	    if (trackZoomHandle().contains (p))
  5776	    {
  5777	        hint.text = tr ("Track height (vertical zoom): [LMB] drag up / down  [Alt][Wheel] over the Playlist  [2xLMB] 100%");
  5778	        hint.value = juce::String (juce::roundToInt (trackZoom * 100.0)) + "%";
  5779	        return hint;
  5780	    }
  5781	
  5782	    if (h.header && h.divider >= 0)
  5783	    {
  5784	        hint.text = tr ("Track divider: [LMB] drag resizes this track  [Shift][LMB] drag: all tracks  [2xLMB] default height");
  5785	        hint.value = juce::String (heightOf (h.divider)) + " px";
  5786	        return hint;
  5787	    }
  5788	
  5789	    if (h.header)
  5790	    {
  5791	        hint.text = h.arm     ? tr ("Audio track: [LMB] arm / disarm recording (its mixer track)")
  5792	                    : h.input ? tr ("Audio track: [LMB] audio input source and monitoring (its mixer track)")
  5793	                    : h.led   ? tr ("Track: [LMB] mute / unmute  [Ctrl][LMB] or [RMB] solo")
  5794	                    : h.arrow ? tr ("Track group: [LMB] collapse / expand the grouped tracks")
  5795	                              : tr ("Track: [LMB] select  [Ctrl][LMB] add / remove  [Shift][LMB] range  [RMB] menu  [2xLMB] open the clip source  [Shift][Wheel] move up / down");
  5796	        hint.value = trackName (h.track);
  5797	        if (const auto mode = trackmodes::describe (song, h.track); ! mode.empty()) // FL's "(Instrument track)" / "(Audio track, ...)"
  5798	            hint.value += " " + (mode.rfind ("(Audio", 0) == 0 ? tr ("(Audio track, linked to mixer track {0})", { juce::String (song.playlistTracks[(std::size_t) h.track].mixerTrack) })
  5799	                                                                 : tr ("(Instrument track)"));
  5800	        hint.hasMenu = ! h.led && ! h.arrow && ! h.arm && ! h.input;
  5801	        return hint;
  5802	    }
  5803	
  5804	    if (p.y >= rulerTop && p.y < headerHeight && p.x >= headerWidth)
  5805	    {
  5806	        // over a marker's tag: its action, and that a click jumps there (FL)
  5807	        if (const auto m = markerTagAt (p.x, p.y); m >= 0)
  5808	        {
  5809	            const auto& marker = song.markers()[(std::size_t) m];
  5810	            hint.text = tr ("Marker: [LMB] jump to it (while playing: at the next Quantize song jumps step)  [Ctrl][LMB] select up to the next marker  [RMB] action, rename, delete");
  5811	            hint.value = markerLabel (marker);
  5812	            if (const auto* action = markers::actionName (marker.isSignature() ? Marker::timeSignature : marker.action); *action != 0)
  5813	                hint.value += " (" + tr (action) + ")";
  5814	            hint.hasMenu = true;
  5815	            return hint;
  5816	        }
  5817	        hint.text = tr ("Ruler: [LMB] song position  [RMB] or [Ctrl][LMB] or [2xLMB] drag: time selection (loop)  [Shift][LMB] drag: move it  [Ctrl][RMB] drag or [Ctrl][Wheel] or a pinch: zoom  [RMB] click: menu  [Alt] no snap");
  5818	        hint.hasMenu = true;
  5819	        return hint;
  5820	    }
  5821	
  5822	    if (! gridArea().contains (p))
  5823	        return hint;
  5824	
  5825	    const gesture::Mods m { mods.isShiftDown(), flmods::ctrl (mods), mods.isAltDown() };
  5826	    hint.text = actionHint (gesture::leftAction (tools.getTool(), h.target, m, true), true);
  5827	
  5828	    if (h.clip >= 0)
  5829	    {
  5830	        const auto onHeader = p.y < clipBounds (h.clip).getY() + (int) clipTitleHeight;
  5831	        if (h.target == gesture::Target::gainHandle)
  5832	            hint.text = actionHint (gesture::Action::clipGain, true);
  5833	        else if (h.target == gesture::Target::clipMenu)
  5834	        {
  5835	#if JUCE_ANDROID
  5836	            hint.text = tr ("Clip menu: tap the arrow to open it; hold the clip title for actions");
  5837	#else
  5838	            hint.text = song.playlist[(std::size_t) h.clip].kind == ClipKind::audio
  5839	                            ? tr ("Clip menu: [LMB] or [RMB] opens it (pitch, fit to tempo, reverse, chop, make unique...)")
  5840	                            : tr ("Clip menu: [LMB] or [RMB] opens it (make unique, rename and colour, chop, select source...)");
  5841	#endif
  5842	        }
  5843	#if JUCE_ANDROID
  5844	        else if (h.target == gesture::Target::item && ! m.ctrl && automationEditable (h.clip)
  5845	                 && automationBody (song.playlist[(std::size_t) h.clip]).contains (p.toFloat()))
  5846	            hint.text << "  " << tr ("Tap the curve to add a point; drag to edit it");
  5847	        else if (onHeader && ! m.ctrl)
  5848	            hint.text = tr ("Drag to move; hold the clip title for actions");
  5849	        else if (h.target == gesture::Target::item && ! m.ctrl)
  5850	            hint.text = tr ("Drag to move; press and hold to delete");
  5851	#else
  5852	        else if (onHeader && ! m.ctrl)
  5853	            hint.text << "  " << tr ("The icon at the top left opens the clip menu (make unique, rename...)");
  5854	        else if (h.target == gesture::Target::item && ! m.ctrl && automationEditable (h.clip)
  5855	                 && automationBody (song.playlist[(std::size_t) h.clip]).contains (p.toFloat())) // round 165
  5856	            hint.text << "  " << tr ("[RMB] adds a point here (hold and drag to place it)  [Shift][RMB] on the curve");
  5857	        else if (h.target == gesture::Target::item && ! m.ctrl)
  5858	            hint.text << "  " << tr ("[Shift][V] + drag: a unique copy  [Alt][RMB] hold: hear it");
  5859	#endif
  5860	        const auto& c = song.playlist[(std::size_t) h.clip];
  5861	        hint.hasMenu = true;
  5862	        if (c.kind == ClipKind::pattern && c.pattern >= 0 && c.pattern < (int) song.patterns.size())
  5863	            hint.value = juce::String::fromUTF8 (song.patterns[(std::size_t) c.pattern].name.c_str());
  5864	        else if (c.kind == ClipKind::audio && c.channel >= 0 && c.channel < (int) song.channels.size())
  5865	            hint.value = h.target == gesture::Target::gainHandle ? tr ("Gain: {0}", { gainText (c.gain) })
  5866	                                                                 : juce::String::fromUTF8 (song.channels[(std::size_t) c.channel].name.c_str());
  5867	        else if (c.kind == ClipKind::automation && c.automation >= 0 && c.automation < (int) song.automations.size())
  5868	            hint.value = juce::String::fromUTF8 (song.automations[(std::size_t) c.automation].name.c_str());
  5869	    }
  5870	    else
  5871	        hint.value = trackName (h.track);
  5872	
  5873	    return hint;
  5874	}
  5875	
  5876	void PlaylistComponent::mouseDoubleClick (const juce::MouseEvent& e)
  5877	{
  5878	    if (trackZoomHandle().contains (e.getPosition())) // the vertical zoom handle: back to 100%
  5879	    {
  5880	        setTrackZoom (1.0, headerHeight);
  5881	        return;
  5882	    }
  5883	
  5884	    // double-click a track divider: back to the default height
  5885	    if (const auto divider = dividerAt (e.getPosition()); divider >= 0)
  5886	    {
  5887	        setTrackHeights ({ divider }, trackHeight);
  5888	        return;
  5889	    }
  5890	
  5891	    // double-click a track header: open the source of the track's first clip (FL); empty tracks: rename
  5892	    if (e.x < headerWidth && e.y >= headerHeight && e.y < gridArea().getBottom() && e.mods.isLeftButtonDown())
  5893	    {
  5894	        const auto track = yToTrack (e.y);
  5895	
  5896	        if (trackLed (track).expanded (3).contains (e.getPosition()) || trackArrow (track).contains (e.getPosition())
  5897	            || trackArm (track).expanded (2).contains (e.getPosition()) || trackInput (track).expanded (2).contains (e.getPosition()))
  5898	            return;
  5899	
  5900	        const auto& playlist = engine.getSong().playlist;
  5901	        int first = -1;
  5902	        for (int i = 0; i < (int) playlist.size(); ++i)
  5903	            if (playlist[(std::size_t) i].track == track && (first < 0 || playlist[(std::size_t) i].start < playlist[(std::size_t) first].start))
  5904	                first = i;
  5905	
  5906	        if (first >= 0)
  5907	            openClipSource (first, e.mods.isAltDown());
  5908	        else
  5909	            renameTrack (track);
  5910	        return;
  5911	    }
  5912	
  5913	    bool edge = false;
  5914	    const auto hit = gridArea().contains (e.getPosition()) ? clipAt (e.x, e.y, edge) : -1;
  5915	
  5916	    if (hit < 0)
  5917	        return;
  5918	
  5919	    openClipSource (hit, e.mods.isAltDown());
  5920	}
  5921	
  5922	void PlaylistComponent::openClipSource (int hit, bool other)
  5923	{
  5924	    if (hit < 0 || hit >= (int) engine.getSong().playlist.size())
  5925	        return;
  5926	
  5927	    const auto clip = engine.getSong().playlist[(std::size_t) hit];
  5928	
  5929	    if (clip.kind == ClipKind::pattern && clip.pattern != engine.getSong().currentPattern
  5930	        && clip.pattern < (int) engine.getSong().patterns.size())
  5931	        engine.editSong ([p = clip.pattern] (Song& s) { s.currentPattern = p; }, false);
  5932	
  5933	    if (clip.kind == ClipKind::automation)
  5934	        openAutomationEditor (engine, clip.automation);
  5935	    else if (clip.kind == ClipKind::audio) // round 170h2: was nothing (the hint said "opens")
  5936	    {
  5937	        // FL's "Double-click on audio clips" (Manage shortcuts, ShortcutEditorForm AudioClipsPanel): the channel
  5938	        // settings, Alt = the clip properties (LYNX's Alt+double-click since round 153)
  5939	        if (other)
  5940	            showClipProperties (engine, hit, { hit }, [safe = juce::Component::SafePointer<PlaylistComponent> (this)] {
  5941	                if (safe != nullptr)
  5942	                    safe->sortClips();
  5943	            });
  5944	        else if (clip.channel >= 0 && clip.channel < (int) engine.getSong().channels.size())
  5945	            openChannelSettings (engine, clip.channel);
  5946	    }
  5947	    else if (clip.kind == ClipKind::pattern)
  5948	    {
  5949	        auto pianoRoll = editPatternsIn == 2
  5950	                         || (editPatternsIn == 0 && clip.pattern < (int) engine.getSong().patterns.size()
  5951	                             && hasPianoRollNotes (engine.getSong().patterns[(std::size_t) clip.pattern]));
  5952	        if (other)
  5953	            pianoRoll = ! pianoRoll;
  5954	
  5955	        if (pianoRoll && onOpenPianoRoll)
  5956	            onOpenPianoRoll();
  5957	        else if (! pianoRoll && onOpenPattern)
  5958	            onOpenPattern();
  5959	    }
  5960	}
  5961	
  5962	bool PlaylistComponent::isInterestedInFileDrag (const juce::StringArray& files)
  5963	{
  5964	    for (const auto& f : files)
  5965	        if (SampleLibrary::isSampleFile (juce::File (f)))
  5966	            return true;
  5967	
  5968	    return false;
  5969	}
  5970	
  5971	namespace
  5972	{
  5973	// An audio clip of `file` at `start` on `track` (one undo step); `reuse`: an existing audio-clip channel of the same
  5974	// file plays it instead of a new one. False when the file can't be read.
  5975	bool addAudioClip (AudioEngine& engine, const juce::File& file, std::int64_t start, int track, bool reuse)
  5976	{
  5977	    if (! SampleLibrary::isSampleFile (file))
  5978	        return false;
  5979	
  5980	    const auto data = engine.getSampleLibrary().load (file.getFullPathName());
  5981	
  5982	    if (data == nullptr)
  5983	        return false;
  5984	
  5985	    // audio length converted to ticks at the current BPM
  5986	    const auto seconds = (double) data->frames() / data->sampleRate;
  5987	    const auto length = juce::jmax<std::int64_t> (ppq / 4, (std::int64_t) (seconds * engine.getSong().bpm / 60.0 * ppq));
  5988	    const auto path = file.getFullPathName().toStdString();
  5989	    const auto name = file.getFileNameWithoutExtension().toStdString();
  5990	
  5991	    engine.editSong ([=] (Song& s) {
  5992	        int channel = -1;
  5993	        if (reuse)
  5994	            for (int c = 0; c < (int) s.channels.size(); ++c)
  5995	                if (s.channels[(std::size_t) c].instrument == InstrumentKind::audioClip && s.channels[(std::size_t) c].samplePath == path)
  5996	                    channel = c;
  5997	
  5998	        if (channel < 0)
  5999	        {
  6000	            ChannelDef c;
  6001	            c.name = name;
  6002	            c.instrument = InstrumentKind::audioClip;
  6003	            c.samplePath = path;
  6004	            // dropped on an audio track: the new channel plays through its mixer track (FL [DECOMP FUN_012f2a10])
  6005	            const auto linked = trackmodes::armableInsert (s, track);
  6006	            c.mixerTrack = linked > 0 ? linked : s.claimFreeInsert();
  6007	            s.channels.push_back (c);
  6008	            channel = (int) s.channels.size() - 1;
  6009	        }
  6010	
  6011	        Clip clip;
  6012	        clip.kind = ClipKind::audio;
  6013	        clip.channel = channel;
  6014	        clip.track = track;
  6015	        clip.start = start;
  6016	        clip.length = length;
  6017	        s.playlist.push_back (clip);
  6018	        trackmodes::followAudioTracks (s, { (int) s.playlist.size() - 1 }); // (a reused channel)
  6019	        s.mode = PlayMode::song;
  6020	    });
  6021	    return true;
  6022	}
  6023	} // namespace
  6024	
  6025	void PlaylistComponent::filesDropped (const juce::StringArray& files, int x, int y)
  6026	{
  6027	    auto start = juce::jmax<std::int64_t> (0, snap (xToTick (x), true));
  6028	    auto track = yToTrack (y);
  6029	
  6030	    for (const auto& f : files)
  6031	        if (addAudioClip (engine, juce::File (f), start, track, false))
  6032	            ++track; // multiple files: one below the other
  6033	}
  6034	
  6035	void PlaylistComponent::addAudioClipAtPlayhead (const juce::File& file)
  6036	{
  6037	    const auto& song = engine.getSong();
  6038	    const auto start = song.mode == PlayMode::song ? juce::jmax<std::int64_t> (0, snap ((std::int64_t) engine.getTransport().positionTicks.load(), false))
  6039	                                                   : std::int64_t (0);
  6040	    // the first track with nothing at the start
  6041	    int track = 0;
  6042	    for (;; ++track)
  6043	    {
  6044	        const auto busy = std::any_of (song.playlist.begin(), song.playlist.end(), [&] (const Clip& c) {
  6045	            return c.track == track && c.start <= start && c.end() > start;
  6046	        });
  6047	        if (! busy)
  6048	            break;
  6049	    }
  6050	    addAudioClip (engine, file, start, track, true);
  6051	}
  6052	
  6053	void PlaylistComponent::glideBy (double ticks, double pixels, bool immediate)
  6054	{
  6055	    if (! gliding)
  6056	    {
  6057	        glideX.jumpTo (scrollTicks);
  6058	        glideY.jumpTo (scrollY);
  6059	        gliding = true;
  6060	    }
  6061	
  6062	    const auto grid = gridArea();
  6063	    glideX.target = juce::jlimit (0.0, juce::jmax (0.0, hBar.getMaximumRangeLimit() - grid.getWidth() / pixelsPerTick), glideX.target + ticks);
  6064	    if (ticks != 0.0) // (a whole number of pixels from the start: the cached picture shifts, round 174e2)
  6065	        glideX.target = std::round (glideX.target * pixelsPerTick) / pixelsPerTick;
  6066	    glideY.target = juce::jlimit (0.0, juce::jmax (0.0, (double) tracksHeight() - grid.getHeight()), glideY.target + pixels);
  6067	    if (immediate)
  6068	    {
  6069	        scrollTicks = glideX.target;
  6070	        scrollY = (int) std::round (glideY.target);
  6071	        gliding = false;
  6072	        updateScrollBars();
  6073	        glideX.jumpTo (scrollTicks); // the scroll bar may have clamped it
  6074	        glideY.jumpTo (scrollY);
  6075	        repaintScrolled();
  6076	        return;
  6077	    }
  6078	    glideFrame();
  6079	}
  6080	
  6081	void PlaylistComponent::glideFrame()
  6082	{
  6083	    if (! gliding)
  6084	        return;
  6085	
  6086	    // someone else moved the view (scroll bar, zoom, pan, playback follow): the glide gives way
  6087	    if (std::abs (scrollTicks - glideX.current) > 1e-6 || std::abs ((double) scrollY - std::round (glideY.current)) > 0.5)
  6088	    {
  6089	        gliding = false;
  6090	        return;
  6091	    }
  6092	
  6093	    // whole pixels sideways (round 174e2: the cached picture then shifts instead of being painted again), at least one
  6094	    // pixel towards the target each frame
  6095	    const auto fromPixels = scrollTicks * pixelsPerTick;
  6096	    const auto steppedPixels = glideX.step (0.45, 0.5 / pixelsPerTick) * pixelsPerTick;
  6097	    scrollTicks = (glideX.target * pixelsPerTick > fromPixels ? std::ceil (steppedPixels - 1.0e-6) : std::floor (steppedPixels + 1.0e-6)) / pixelsPerTick;
  6098	    if ((glideX.target - scrollTicks) * (glideX.target * pixelsPerTick - fromPixels) < 0.0) // (never past the target)
  6099	        scrollTicks = glideX.target;
  6100	    scrollY = (int) std::round (glideY.step (0.45, 1.0));
  6101	    glideY.current = scrollY;
  6102	    updateScrollBars();
  6103	    glideX.current = scrollTicks;
  6104	    gliding = glideX.isMoving() || glideY.isMoving();
  6105	    repaintScrolled();
  6106	}
  6107	
  6108	void PlaylistComponent::cancelQuickTouch()
  6109	{
  6110	#if JUCE_ANDROID
  6111	    cancelTouchHold();
  6112	#endif
  6113	    drag = Drag::none;
  6114	    dragClip = -1;
  6115	    dragIndices.clear();
  6116	    dragOriginals.clear();
  6117	    rubberBand = {};
  6118	    hover = {};
  6119	    repaint();
  6120	}
  6121	
  6122	void PlaylistComponent::mouseMagnify (const juce::MouseEvent& e, float scale)
  6123	{
  6124	    gliding = false;
  6125	    const auto factor = gesture::pinchZoomFactor (scale);
  6126	    if (e.mods.isAltDown() || touchgestures::isVerticalPinch()) // the track heights, around the pointer
  6127	    {
  6128	        setTrackZoom (trackZoom * factor, e.y);
  6129	        return;
  6130	    }
  6131	    const auto anchorPixels = (double) juce::jmax (0, e.x - headerWidth);
  6132	    const auto zoomed = juce::jlimit (4.0 / (4 * ppq), 800.0 / (4 * ppq), pixelsPerTick * factor);
  6133	    scrollTicks = gesture::zoomAnchored (scrollTicks, pixelsPerTick, zoomed, anchorPixels);
  6134	    pixelsPerTick = zoomed;
  6135	    zoomBeforeAll = 0.0;
  6136	    updateScrollBars();
  6137	    repaint();
  6138	}
  6139	
  6140	void PlaylistComponent::mouseWheelMove (const juce::MouseEvent& e, const juce::MouseWheelDetails& rawWheel)
  6141	{
  6142	    const auto wheel = flmods::normalisedWheel (rawWheel); // Windows touchpads: pieces of a notch (round 170e)
  6143	    const auto ctrl = flmods::ctrl (e.mods), alt = e.mods.isAltDown(), shift = e.mods.isShiftDown();
  6144	
  6145	    // Shift+wheel over a track header: moves the track up / down
  6146	    if (shift && ! ctrl && e.x < headerWidth && e.y >= headerHeight && wheel.deltaY != 0.0f)
  6147	    {
  6148	        const auto track = yToTrack (e.y);
  6149	        const auto other = track + (wheel.deltaY > 0 ? -1 : 1);
  6150	
  6151	        if (other >= 0 && other < numTracks())
  6152	        {
  6153	            setSelection ({});
  6154	            engine.editSong ([track, other] (Song& s) { noteedit::swapTracks (s, track, other); });
  6155	            // the mouse follows the track, so the next wheel step moves it further
  6156	            refreshTrackLayout();
  6157	            juce::Desktop::setMousePosition (localPointToGlobal (juce::Point<int> (e.x, trackToY (other) + heightOf (other) / 2)));
  6158	        }
  6159	        return;
  6160	    }
  6161	
  6162	    if (shift && alt) // nudge the selected clips by the grid
  6163	    {
  6164	        if (wheel.deltaY != 0.0f && ! selection.empty())
  6165	        {
  6166	            const auto now = juce::Time::getMillisecondCounter();
  6167	            const auto newBurst = now - lastWheelEdit > 700; // one undo step per burst
  6168	            lastWheelEdit = now;
  6169	            const auto ticks = (wheel.deltaY > 0 ? 1 : -1) * gridTicks();
  6170	            engine.editSong ([this, ticks] (Song& s) {
  6171	                auto delta = ticks;
  6172	                for (auto i : selection)
  6173	                    delta = juce::jmax (delta, -s.playlist[(std::size_t) i].start);
  6174	                for (auto i : selection)
  6175	                    s.playlist[(std::size_t) i].start += delta;
  6176	            }, newBurst);
  6177	            sortClips();
  6178	        }
  6179	        return;
  6180	    }
  6181	
  6182	    if (alt && ! shift) // Alt+wheel (and Ctrl+Alt+wheel, as in the Piano Roll) zooms vertically: the track heights, around the mouse
  6183	    {
  6184	        gliding = false;
  6185	        setTrackZoom (trackZoom * gesture::wheelZoomFactor (wheel.deltaY, wheel.isSmooth), e.y);
  6186	        return;
  6187	    }
  6188	
  6189	    if (ctrl)
  6190	    {
  6191	        gliding = false;
  6192	        const auto anchorPixels = (double) juce::jmax (0, e.x - headerWidth);
  6193	        const auto zoomed = juce::jlimit (4.0 / (4 * ppq), 800.0 / (4 * ppq), pixelsPerTick * gesture::wheelZoomFactor (wheel.deltaY, wheel.isSmooth));
  6194	        scrollTicks = gesture::zoomAnchored (scrollTicks, pixelsPerTick, zoomed, anchorPixels);
  6195	        pixelsPerTick = zoomed;
  6196	        zoomBeforeAll = 0.0;
  6197	        updateScrollBars();
  6198	        repaint();
  6199	        return;
  6200	    }
  6201	
  6202	    // plain wheel: up/down (Shift: left/right); a horizontal wheel or trackpad swipe scrolls sideways (a trackpad at once)
  6203	    if (shift)
  6204	        glideBy (gesture::wheelScrollPixels (wheel.deltaY, wheel.isSmooth, 120.0) / pixelsPerTick, 0.0, wheel.isSmooth);
  6205	    else
  6206	        glideBy (gesture::wheelScrollPixels (wheel.deltaX, wheel.isSmooth, 80.0) / pixelsPerTick,
  6207	                 gesture::wheelScrollPixels (wheel.deltaY, wheel.isSmooth, 2.0 * defaultRowHeight()), wheel.isSmooth);
  6208	}
  6209	
  6210	void PlaylistComponent::scrollBarMoved (juce::ScrollBar* bar, double start)
  6211	{
  6212	    if (bar == &hBar)
  6213	        scrollTicks = start;
  6214	    else
  6215	        scrollY = (int) start;
  6216	
  6217	    repaintScrolled();
  6218	}
  6219	
  6220	void PlaylistComponent::changeListenerCallback (juce::ChangeBroadcaster* source)
  6221	{
  6222	    if (source == ui::WaveformCache::getInstanceWithoutCreating())
  6223	    {
  6224	        repaint();
  6225	        return;
  6226	    }
  6227	
  6228	    // the clip list changed externally (undo, pattern removal...): the selection no longer applies
  6229	    const auto count = engine.getSong().playlist.size();
  6230	
  6231	    if (count != knownClipCount && drag == Drag::none)
  6232	    {
  6233	        knownClipCount = count;
  6234	        selection.clear();
  6235	    }
  6236	
  6237	    // switching pattern (top bar, Channel Rack) sets the pencil back to the pattern
  6238	    if (engine.getSong().currentPattern != knownCurrentPattern)
  6239	    {
  6240	        knownCurrentPattern = engine.getSong().currentPattern;
  6241	        brush = {};
  6242	    }
  6243	
  6244	    followButton.setToggleState (EditorGlobals::get().isFollowing (false), juce::dontSendNotification);
  6245	    refreshArrangementBox();
  6246	    refreshTrackLayout();
  6247	    if (engine.getSong().patternBlocksOn != blocksShown) // Pattern Blocks turned on / off (menu, undo, another project)
  6248	        resized();
  6249	    blocksView->refresh();
  6250	    updateScrollBars();
  6251	    repaint();
  6252	}
  6253	
  6254	bool PlaylistComponent::liveFrame (double nowMs)
  6255	{
  6256	    auto& transport = engine.getTransport();
  6257	    const auto& song = engine.getSong();
  6258	    const auto playing = transport.playing.load();
  6259	    auto tick = -1.0;
  6260	
  6261	    // (between two audio blocks the playhead moves on at the song's tempo: smooth at 144 Hz, round 174e)
  6262	    if (song.mode == PlayMode::song && (playing || transport.positionTicks.load() > 0.0))
  6263	        tick = engine.songPlayheadTicks() + engine.playheadAdvanceTicks();
  6264	
  6265	    // whenever it moved: the line is anti-aliased, so even a fraction of a pixel shows (zoomed out, a playhead crossing
  6266	    // a few pixels a second glides at the display's rate instead of stepping, as FL's does)
  6267	    const auto moved = (tick < 0.0) != (playheadTick < 0.0) || std::abs ((tick - playheadTick) * pixelsPerTick) >= 0.002;
  6268	    if (moved && isShowing())
  6269	    {
  6270	        // only the cursor column (old and new) is repainted: large projects stay cheap. One rectangle for both: the
  6271	        // two strips overlap (a frame moves the line by a pixel or less), and each rectangle of a repaint is a paint
  6272	        auto column = [this] (double t) {
  6273	            return t >= 0.0 ? juce::Rectangle<int> ((int) tickToX (t) - 8, 0, 16, getHeight()) : juce::Rectangle<int>();
  6274	        };
  6275	        auto dirty = [this] (juce::Rectangle<int> r) {
  6276	            if (r.isEmpty())
  6277	                return;
  6278	            playheadRepaintOnly = playheadOverlay; // (the cached image under it stays valid)
  6279	            repaint (r);
  6280	            playheadRepaintOnly = false;
  6281	        };
  6282	
  6283	        const auto before = column (playheadTick);
  6284	        playheadTick = tick;
  6285	
  6286	        // Follow: if the cursor leaves the screen while playing, scrolls one page (off: the view stays put)
  6287	        const auto grid = gridArea();
  6288	        const auto newStart = transportedit::followViewStart (scrollTicks, grid.getWidth() / pixelsPerTick, tick, 20.0 / pixelsPerTick,
  6289	                                                              20.0 / pixelsPerTick);
  6290	
  6291	        if (EditorGlobals::get().isFollowing (false) && playing && newStart != scrollTicks)
  6292	        {
  6293	            scrollTicks = newStart;
  6294	            updateScrollBars();
  6295	            repaint();
  6296	        }
  6297	        else if (const auto after = column (tick); ! before.isEmpty() && ! after.isEmpty() && std::abs (after.getX() - before.getX()) <= 16)
  6298	            dirty (before.getUnion (after));
  6299	        else
  6300	        {
  6301	            dirty (before);
  6302	            dirty (after);
  6303	        }
  6304	    }
  6305	    else if (moved)
  6306	        playheadTick = tick;
  6307	
  6308	    // Performance mode: the launched and waiting clips (frames, progress) and the headers' track progress
  6309	    auto live = false;
  6310	    if (song.performanceMode && isShowing())
  6311	    {
  6312	        const auto& perfState = engine.performanceState();
  6313	        const auto zoneEnd = song.performanceZoneEnd();
  6314	        auto changed = false;
  6315	        for (int t = 0; t < (int) perfShown.size(); ++t)
  6316	        {
  6317	            const auto v = perfState.tracks[(std::size_t) t].playing.load (std::memory_order_relaxed) * 2 + 1
  6318	                           + (perfState.tracks[(std::size_t) t].pending.load (std::memory_order_relaxed) + 1) * 7919;
  6319	            if (v != perfShown[(std::size_t) t])
  6320	            {
  6321	                perfShown[(std::size_t) t] = v;
  6322	                changed = true;
  6323	            }
  6324	        }
  6325	        live = perfState.anyPlaying.load (std::memory_order_relaxed);
  6326	        // (the clips' progress and the waiting clips' blink cover the whole zone: 30 times a second, as before)
  6327	        const auto due = live && nowMs - perfRepaintMs >= 1000.0 / 30.0;
  6328	        if (zoneEnd > 0 && (changed || due))
  6329	        {
  6330	            perfRepaintMs = nowMs;
  6331	            const auto grid = gridArea();
  6332	            const auto x1 = juce::jmin (grid.getRight(), (int) tickToX ((double) zoneEnd) + 2);
  6333	            if (x1 > grid.getX())
  6334	                repaint (juce::Rectangle<int> (grid.getX(), grid.getY(), x1 - grid.getX(), grid.getHeight()));
  6335	            if (showTrackProgress)
  6336	                repaint (0, headerHeight, headerWidth, getHeight() - headerHeight);
  6337	        }
  6338	    }
  6339	    return playing || moved || live;
  6340	}
  6341	
  6342	// Performance mode (round 165m): the launched clips (a bright frame and, with "Performance clip progress", how far the
  6343	// pass has gone), the clips waiting for their sync point (a blinking frame) and, with "Performance track progress",
  6344	// the playing clip's progress on the track header.
  6345	void PlaylistComponent::paintPerformance (juce::Graphics& g, juce::Rectangle<int> grid)
  6346	{
  6347	    using namespace theme;
  6348	    const auto& song = engine.getSong();
  6349	    const auto& live = engine.performanceState();
  6350	    const auto zoneEnd = song.performanceZoneEnd();
  6351	    const auto any = live.anyPlaying.load (std::memory_order_relaxed);
  6352	    const auto blink = 0.45f + 0.45f * std::sin ((float) juce::Time::getMillisecondCounter() * 0.012f);
  6353	    for (int i = 0; i < (int) song.playlist.size(); ++i)
  6354	    {
  6355	        const auto& c = song.playlist[(std::size_t) i];
  6356	        if (c.start >= zoneEnd || c.track < 0 || c.track >= perf::maxTracks)
  6357	            continue;
  6358	        const auto& view = live.tracks[(std::size_t) c.track];
  6359	        const auto playing = any && view.playing.load (std::memory_order_relaxed) == c.start;
  6360	        const auto pending = any && view.pending.load (std::memory_order_relaxed) == c.start;
  6361	        const auto r = clipBounds (i).toFloat();
  6362	        if (! r.intersects (grid.toFloat()))
  6363	            continue;
  6364	        if (! playing && ! pending)
  6365	        {
  6366	            // as in FL, the zone's idle clips are dimmed: what plays stands out
  6367	            g.setColour (colour::bg0.withAlpha (0.42f));
  6368	            g.fillRoundedRectangle (r, radius::sm);
  6369	            continue;
  6370	        }
  6371	        if (playing)
  6372	        {
  6373	            // a play mark at its left edge (FL: ▸) besides the bright frame
  6374	            juce::Path mark;
  6375	            const auto mx = r.getX() + 5.0f, my = r.getCentreY() + 3.0f;
  6376	            mark.addTriangle (mx, my - 4.0f, mx, my + 4.0f, mx + 6.0f, my);
  6377	            g.setColour (colour::ledOn);
  6378	            g.fillPath (mark);
  6379	            g.setColour (colour::ledOn);
  6380	            g.drawRoundedRectangle (r.reduced (1.0f), radius::sm, 2.0f);
  6381	            if (showClipProgress)
  6382	            {
  6383	                // as in FL, the part already played is lighter, up to a thin playhead line
  6384	                const auto p = juce::jlimit (0.0f, 1.0f, view.progress.load (std::memory_order_relaxed));
  6385	                const auto played = r.reduced (1.0f).withWidth (juce::jmax (0.0f, (r.getWidth() - 2.0f) * p));
  6386	                g.setColour (colour::textPrimary.withAlpha (0.16f));
  6387	                g.fillRect (played);
  6388	                g.setColour (colour::ledOn.withAlpha (0.85f));
  6389	                g.fillRect (played.withX (played.getRight() - 1.0f).withWidth (1.5f));
  6390	            }
  6391	        }
  6392	        if (pending)
  6393	        {
  6394	            g.setColour (colour::warning.withAlpha (blink));
  6395	            g.drawRoundedRectangle (r.reduced (1.0f), radius::sm, 2.0f);
  6396	        }
  6397	    }
  6398	    (void) showTrackProgress; // (drawn on the headers: paintOverChildren)
  6399	}
  6400	
  6401	void PlaylistComponent::togglePerformanceMode()
  6402	{
  6403	    const auto on = ! engine.getSong().performanceMode;
  6404	    engine.editSong ([on] (Song& s) { s.performanceMode = on; });
  6405	    if (on && engine.getSong().performanceZoneEnd() <= 0)
  6406	        ui::postHint ({ tr ("Performance mode: put a Start marker where the song starts (right-click the ruler); the clips before it are launched with a click while playing"), {} }, 6000);
  6407	    repaint();
  6408	}

  6417	bool PlaylistComponent::isInterestedInDragSource (const SourceDetails& details)
  6418	{

  6421	
  6422	void PlaylistComponent::itemDropped (const SourceDetails& details)
  6423	{
  6424	    // a pattern from the Browser's "Current project": a clip of it where it is dropped
  6425	    if (const auto pattern = BrowserComponent::draggedPattern (details); pattern >= 0 && pattern < (int) engine.getSong().patterns.size())
  6426	    {
  6427	        const auto start = juce::jmax<std::int64_t> (0, snap (xToTick (details.localPosition.x), true));
  6428	        const auto track = juce::jmax (0, yToTrack (details.localPosition.y));
  6429	        engine.editSong ([=] (Song& s) {
  6430	            Clip clip;
  6431	            clip.kind = ClipKind::pattern;
  6432	            clip.pattern = pattern;
  6433	            clip.track = track;
  6434	            clip.start = start;
  6435	            clip.length = juce::jmax<std::int64_t> (ppq, s.patterns[(std::size_t) pattern].effectiveLength (s.beatsPerBar));
  6436	            s.playlist.push_back (clip);
  6437	            s.mode = PlayMode::song;
  6438	        });
  6439	        return;
  6440	    }
  6441	
  6442	    if (const auto file = BrowserComponent::draggedFile (details); file.existsAsFile())
  6443	        filesDropped ({ file.getFullPathName() }, details.localPosition.x, details.localPosition.y);
  6444	}

  6448	    : engine (e),
  6449	      playlist (p)

  6459	
  6460	void PatternPicker::changeListenerCallback (juce::ChangeBroadcaster*)
  6461	{
  6462	    // the pencil's source changed elsewhere (a clip clicked, the track menu): show its kind, as FL does
  6463	    if (playlist.brush.kind != seenBrush)
  6464	    {
  6465	        seenBrush = playlist.brush.kind;
  6466	        view = seenBrush == ClipKind::audio ? 1 : seenBrush == ClipKind::automation ? 2 : 0;
  6467	        scroll = 0;
  6468	    }
  6469	    repaint();
  6470	}
  6471	
  6472	void PatternPicker::setView (int newView)
  6473	{
  6474	    view = juce::jlimit (0, 2, newView);
  6475	    scroll = 0;
  6476	    repaint();
  6477	}
  6478	
  6479	std::vector<PatternPicker::Row> PatternPicker::rows() const
  6480	{
  6481	    const auto& song = engine.getSong();
  6482	    std::vector<Row> list;
  6483	
  6484	    if (view == 0)
  6485	        for (int i = 0; i < (int) song.patterns.size(); ++i)
  6486	            list.push_back ({ ClipKind::pattern, i, juce::String::fromUTF8 (song.patterns[(std::size_t) i].name.c_str()),
  6487	                              PlaylistComponent::patternColour (song, i) });
  6488	
  6489	    if (view == 1)
  6490	        for (int c = 0; c < (int) song.channels.size(); ++c)
  6491	            if (song.channels[(std::size_t) c].instrument == InstrumentKind::audioClip)
  6492	                list.push_back ({ ClipKind::audio, c, juce::String::fromUTF8 (song.channels[(std::size_t) c].name.c_str()),
  6493	                                  PlaylistComponent::audioClipColour (song.channels[(std::size_t) c]) });
  6494	
  6495	    if (view == 2)
  6496	        for (int a = 0; a < (int) song.automations.size(); ++a)
  6497	            list.push_back ({ ClipKind::automation, a, juce::String::fromUTF8 (song.automations[(std::size_t) a].name.c_str()), theme::colour::automation });
  6498	
  6499	    return list;
  6500	}
  6501	
  6502	juce::Rectangle<int> PatternPicker::tabBounds (int tab) const
  6503	{
  6504	    const auto w = (getWidth() - 8) / 3;
  6505	    return { 4 + tab * w, 2, w, headerHeight - 4 };
  6506	}
  6507	
  6508	int PatternPicker::listBottom() const
  6509	{
  6510	    return view == 0 ? getHeight() - footerHeight : getHeight();
  6511	}
  6512	
  6513	juce::Rectangle<int> PatternPicker::addBounds() const
  6514	{
  6515	    return view == 0 ? juce::Rectangle<int> (4, getHeight() - footerHeight + 2, getWidth() - 8, footerHeight - 4) : juce::Rectangle<int>();
  6516	}
  6517	
  6518	int PatternPicker::rowAt (int y) const
  6519	{
  6520	    if (y < headerHeight || y >= listBottom())
  6521	        return -1;
  6522	
  6523	    const auto index = (y - headerHeight) / rowHeight + scroll;
  6524	    return index < (int) rows().size() ? index : -1;
  6525	}
  6526	
  6527	void PatternPicker::paint (juce::Graphics& g)
  6528	{
  6529	    const auto& song = engine.getSong();
  6530	    const auto list = rows();
  6531	    using namespace theme;
  6532	    g.fillAll (colour::bg2);
  6533	    g.setColour (colour::bg3);
  6534	    g.fillRect (0, 0, getWidth(), headerHeight);
  6535	    g.setColour (colour::border);
  6536	    g.fillRect (0, headerHeight - 1, getWidth(), 1);
  6537	
  6538	    // the three tabs: patterns, audio clips, automation clips
  6539	    const Icon tabIcons[] = { Icon::pattern, Icon::audioFile, Icon::automate };
  6540	    for (int t = 0; t < 3; ++t)
  6541	    {
  6542	        const auto r = tabBounds (t).toFloat();
  6543	        if (t == view || t == hoverTab)
  6544	        {
  6545	            g.setColour (t == view ? colour::bg5 : colour::bg4);
  6546	            g.fillRoundedRectangle (r, radius::sm);
  6547	        }
  6548	        icons::draw (g, tabIcons[t], r.withSizeKeepingCentre (16.0f, 16.0f), t == view ? colour::accent : t == hoverTab ? colour::textPrimary : colour::textSecondary);
  6549	    }
  6550	
  6551	    {
  6552	        juce::Graphics::ScopedSaveState state (g);
  6553	        g.reduceClipRegion (0, headerHeight, getWidth(), listBottom() - headerHeight);
  6554	
  6555	        for (int i = scroll; i < (int) list.size(); ++i)
  6556	        {
  6557	            const auto y = headerHeight + (i - scroll) * rowHeight;
  6558	
  6559	            if (y > listBottom())
  6560	                break;
  6561	
  6562	            const auto& row = list[(std::size_t) i];
  6563	            const auto r = juce::Rectangle<int> (4, y + 2, getWidth() - 8, rowHeight - 4);
  6564	            const auto current = row.kind == ClipKind::pattern ? (playlist.brush.kind == ClipKind::pattern && row.index == song.currentPattern)
  6565	                                                               : (playlist.brush.kind == row.kind && playlist.brush.index == row.index);
  6566	            const auto rowColour = tame (row.colour);
  6567	            const auto box = r.toFloat();
  6568	            auto fill = rowColour.interpolatedWith (colour::bg3, current ? 0.25f : 0.55f);
  6569	            if (i == hoverRow && ! current)
  6570	                fill = fill.interpolatedWith (colour::bg5, 0.35f);
  6571	            g.setColour (fill);
  6572	            g.fillRoundedRectangle (box, radius::sm);
  6573	
  6574	            // a faint miniature of the pattern's notes behind the name (FL's picker shows the same)
  6575	            if (row.kind == ClipKind::pattern && row.index < (int) song.patterns.size())
  6576	            {
  6577	                const auto& pattern = song.patterns[(std::size_t) row.index];
  6578	                if (! pattern.notes.empty())
  6579	                {
  6580	                    int low = 127, high = 0;
  6581	                    for (const auto& n : pattern.notes)
  6582	                    {
  6583	                        low = juce::jmin (low, n.key);
  6584	                        high = juce::jmax (high, n.key);
  6585	                    }
  6586	                    const auto body = box.reduced (3.0f, 3.0f);
  6587	                    const auto span = (float) juce::jmax (6, high - low + 2);
  6588	                    const auto length = (double) juce::jmax<std::int64_t> (1, pattern.effectiveLength (song.beatsPerBar));
  6589	                    g.setColour (clipContentColour (rowColour).withAlpha (0.35f));
  6590	                    for (const auto& n : pattern.notes)
  6591	                    {
  6592	                        const auto nx = body.getX() + (float) (n.start / length) * body.getWidth();
  6593	                        const auto nw = juce::jmax (1.5f, (float) (n.length / length) * body.getWidth());
  6594	                        const auto ny = body.getBottom() - ((float) (n.key - low) + 1.0f) / span * body.getHeight();
  6595	                        g.fillRect (nx, ny, juce::jmin (nw, body.getRight() - nx), 1.5f);
  6596	                    }
  6597	                }
  6598	            }
  6599	
  6600	            if (row.kind == ClipKind::pattern && picked.contains (row.index) && picked.items.size() > 1) // picked with others
  6601	            {
  6602	                g.setColour (colour::accent.withAlpha (0.8f));
  6603	                g.drawRoundedRectangle (box.reduced (0.5f), radius::sm, 1.2f);
  6604	            }
  6605	
  6606	            if (current) // the current source: the accent strip
  6607	            {
  6608	                g.setColour (colour::accent);
  6609	                g.fillRoundedRectangle (box.withWidth (3.0f), 1.5f);
  6610	            }
  6611	
  6612	            g.setColour (clipTitleText (fill));
  6613	            g.setFont (font (type::small, current));
  6614	            textcache::drawText (g, row.name, r.withTrimmedLeft (9).withTrimmedRight (4), juce::Justification::centredLeft, true);
  6615	        }
  6616	    }
  6617	
  6618	    if (const auto add = addBounds(); ! add.isEmpty())
  6619	    {
  6620	        g.setColour (hoverAdd ? colour::bg4 : colour::bg3);
  6621	        g.fillRoundedRectangle (add.toFloat(), radius::sm);
  6622	        icons::draw (g, Icon::plus, add.toFloat().withSizeKeepingCentre (14.0f, 14.0f), hoverAdd ? colour::textPrimary : colour::textSecondary);
  6623	    }
  6624	
  6625	    g.setColour (colour::border);
  6626	    g.drawVerticalLine (getWidth() - 1, 0.0f, (float) getHeight());
  6627	}
  6628	
  6629	void PatternPicker::mouseDown (const juce::MouseEvent& e)
  6630	{
  6631	    for (int t = 0; t < 3; ++t)
  6632	        if (tabBounds (t).contains (e.getPosition()))
  6633	        {
  6634	            setView (t);
  6635	            return;
  6636	        }
  6637	
  6638	    if (addBounds().contains (e.getPosition()))
  6639	    {
  6640	        if (onNewPattern)
  6641	            onNewPattern();
  6642	        return;
  6643	    }
  6644	
  6645	    const auto list = rows();
  6646	    const auto i = rowAt (e.y);
  6647	
  6648	    if (i < 0)
  6649	        return;
  6650	
  6651	    const auto& row = list[(std::size_t) i];
  6652	    playlist.brush = { row.kind, row.index };
  6653	    seenBrush = row.kind;
  6654	
  6655	    if (row.kind == ClipKind::pattern && row.index != engine.getSong().currentPattern)
  6656	        engine.editSong ([p = row.index] (Song& s) { s.currentPattern = p; }, false);
  6657	
  6658	    // FL's picker selection (PickerPanelMouseDown FUN_00e4d5d0, REA_rodada169k §2.2)
  6659	    pressedRow = -1;
  6660	    if (row.kind == ClipKind::pattern)
  6661	    {
  6662	        pickersel::clamp (picked, (int) engine.getSong().patterns.size());
  6663	        if (flmods::popup (e.mods))
  6664	            pickersel::rightDown (picked, row.index, flmods::ctrl (e.mods));
  6665	        else
  6666	        {
  6667	            deselectOnUp = pickersel::leftDownDeselects (picked, row.index, flmods::ctrl (e.mods));
  6668	            pressedRow = i;
  6669	        }
  6670	    }
  6671	
  6672	    if (flmods::popup (e.mods) && row.kind == ClipKind::pattern && onPatternMenu)
  6673	        onPatternMenu();
  6674	
  6675	    repaint();
  6676	}
  6677	
  6678	void PatternPicker::mouseUp (const juce::MouseEvent& e)
  6679	{
  6680	    // FL's PickerPanelMouseUp FUN_00e4e080: plain = only this one, Ctrl = toggle, Shift = the range from the first picked
  6681	    const auto i = rowAt (e.y);
  6682	    if (pressedRow < 0 || i != pressedRow)
  6683	    {
  6684	        pressedRow = -1;
  6685	        return;
  6686	    }
  6687	    pressedRow = -1;
  6688	    const auto list = rows();
  6689	    if (i >= (int) list.size() || list[(std::size_t) i].kind != ClipKind::pattern)
  6690	        return;
  6691	    pickersel::leftUp (picked, list[(std::size_t) i].index, flmods::ctrl (e.mods), e.mods.isShiftDown(), deselectOnUp);
  6692	    deselectOnUp = false;
  6693	    repaint();
  6694	}
  6695	
  6696	std::vector<int> PatternPicker::pickedPatterns() const
  6697	{
  6698	    auto s = picked;
  6699	    pickersel::clamp (s, (int) engine.getSong().patterns.size());
  6700	    return s.items;
  6701	}

  6703	void PatternPicker::mouseDoubleClick (const juce::MouseEvent& e)

  6708	    if (i >= 0 && list[(std::size_t) i].kind == ClipKind::pattern && onOpenPattern)
  6709	        onOpenPattern();

  6712	void PatternPicker::mouseMove (const juce::MouseEvent& e)
  6713	{
  6714	    int tab = -1;
  6715	    for (int t = 0; t < 3; ++t)
  6716	        if (tabBounds (t).contains (e.getPosition()))
  6717	            tab = t;
  6718	    const auto row = rowAt (e.y);
  6719	    const auto add = addBounds().contains (e.getPosition());
  6720	
  6721	    if (tab != hoverTab || row != hoverRow || add != hoverAdd)
  6722	    {
  6723	        hoverTab = tab;
  6724	        hoverRow = row;
  6725	        hoverAdd = add;
  6726	        repaint();
  6727	    }
  6728	}
  6729	
  6730	void PatternPicker::mouseExit (const juce::MouseEvent&)
  6731	{
  6732	    hoverTab = hoverRow = -1;
  6733	    hoverAdd = false;
  6734	    repaint();
  6735	}
  6736	
  6737	ui::Hint PatternPicker::hintAt (juce::Point<int> p)
  6738	{
  6739	    ui::Hint hint;
  6740	    const char* tabs[] = { TR_NOOP ("Picker: patterns"), TR_NOOP ("Picker: audio clips"), TR_NOOP ("Picker: automation clips") };
  6741	
  6742	    for (int t = 0; t < 3; ++t)
  6743	        if (tabBounds (t).contains (p))
  6744	        {
  6745	            hint.text = tr (tabs[t]) + "  " + tr ("[LMB] show");
  6746	            return hint;
  6747	        }
  6748	
  6749	    if (addBounds().contains (p))
  6750	    {
  6751	        hint.text = tr ("New pattern");
  6752	        return hint;
  6753	    }
  6754	
  6755	    const auto list = rows();
  6756	    if (const auto i = rowAt (p.y); i >= 0)
  6757	    {
  6758	        hint.value = list[(std::size_t) i].name;
  6759	        hint.text = list[(std::size_t) i].kind == ClipKind::pattern
  6760	                        ? tr ("Pattern: [LMB] paint it with the pencil  [Ctrl+LMB] / [Shift+LMB] pick several  [2xLMB] open  [RMB] menu")
  6761	                        : tr ("Clip source: [LMB] paint it with the pencil");
  6762	        hint.hasMenu = list[(std::size_t) i].kind == ClipKind::pattern;
  6763	    }
  6764	
  6765	    return hint;
  6766	}
  6767	
  6768	void PatternPicker::mouseWheelMove (const juce::MouseEvent&, const juce::MouseWheelDetails& wheel)
  6769	{
  6770	    const auto rows = (int) this->rows().size();
  6771	    const auto visible = juce::jmax (1, (listBottom() - headerHeight) / rowHeight);
  6772	    scroll = juce::jlimit (0, juce::jmax (0, rows - visible), scroll - (int) std::round (wheel.deltaY * 6.0f));
  6773	    repaint();
  6774	}
