  1680	                        // a Transistor Bass step's note reads as a note name (60 = C5, FL's naming; round 168q2)
  1681	                        static const char* const noteNames[] = { "C", "C#", "D", "D#", "E", "F", "F#", "G", "G#", "A", "A#", "B" };
  1682	                        const auto whole = (int) std::lround (k->getValue());
  1683	                        const auto* units = unitParam (*k); // (round 169h: a 0…1 number box in whole units)
  1684	                        const auto text = units != nullptr ? juce::String (units->unitString (units->unitOf ((float) k->getValue())))
  1685	                                        : juce::String (item.param).containsIgnoreCase ("oversampling") ? juce::String (1 << juce::jlimit (0, 8, (int) k->getValue())) + "x"
  1686	                                        : juce::String (item.param).endsWith (" note") ? juce::String (noteNames[((whole % 12) + 12) % 12]) + juce::String (whole / 12)
  1687	                                                                                       : juce::String (whole);
  1688	                        g.drawFittedText (text, box.withTrimmedRight (8.0f * f).toNearestInt(), juce::Justification::centred, 1, 0.7f);
  1689	                        g.setColour (colour::textSecondary);
  1690	                        g.drawText (juce::String::fromUTF8 ("\xe2\x96\xb4"), box.withTrimmedLeft (box.getWidth() - 10.0f * f).withHeight (box.getHeight() * 0.5f), juce::Justification::centred, false);
  1691	                        g.drawText (juce::String::fromUTF8 ("\xe2\x96\xbe"), box.withTrimmedLeft (box.getWidth() - 10.0f * f).withTrimmedTop (box.getHeight() * 0.5f), juce::Justification::centred, false);
  1692	                    }
  1693	                    break;
  1694	                case plugui::Item::display:
  1695	                    skin::well (g, r, radius::md * f);
  1696	                    // round 168q: BeepMap's bitmap, its file row, the Granulizer's sample and grains, Transistor Bass's
  1697	                    // program and sequence
  1698	                    if (juce::String (item.display) == "beepmapimage")
  1699	                        paintBeepMapImage (g, r.reduced (3.0f * f));
  1700	                    if (juce::String (item.display) == "beepmapfile")
  1701	                        paintBeepMapFile (g, r, f);
  1702	                    if (juce::String (item.display) == "granulizerwave")
  1703	                        paintGranulizerWave (g, r.reduced (4.0f * f), f);
  1704	                    if (juce::String (item.display) == "granulizergrains")
  1705	                        paintGranulizerGrains (g, r.reduced (5.0f * f), f);
  1706	                    if (juce::String (item.display) == "tbprogram")
  1707	                        paintTransistorBass (g, r, f);
  1708	                    if (juce::String (item.display) == "tbsteps")
  1709	                        paintTransistorBassSteps (g, r, f);
  1710	                    if (juce::String (item.display) == "tbseqtools")
  1711	                        paintTransistorBassTools (g, r, f);
  1712	                    if (juce::String (item.display) == "xy")
  1713	                        if (auto* kx = knobFor (item.param); kx != nullptr)
  1714	                            if (auto* ky = knobFor (item.param2); ky != nullptr)
  1715	                            {
  1716	                                // FL's Ogun pad (TQuickXYZ, round 169h shots fl-ogun-*.png): a 4 × 4 grid and a small ring
  1717	                                // whose centre is the value, drawn clipped to the pad — at X = 0 only its inner half shows
  1718	                                const juce::Graphics::ScopedSaveState clip (g);
  1719	                                g.reduceClipRegion (r.toNearestInt());
  1720	                                for (int k = 1; k < 4; ++k)
  1721	                                {
  1722	                                    g.setColour (k == 2 ? colour::gridBar : colour::gridBeat);
  1723	                                    g.drawVerticalLine ((int) (r.getX() + r.getWidth() * (float) k / 4.0f), r.getY(), r.getBottom());
  1724	                                    g.drawHorizontalLine ((int) (r.getY() + r.getHeight() * (float) k / 4.0f), r.getX(), r.getRight());
  1725	                                }
  1726	                                const juce::Point<float> pt { r.getX() + r.getWidth() * normalised (*kx), r.getBottom() - r.getHeight() * normalised (*ky) };
  1727	                                g.setColour (accent);
  1728	                                g.drawEllipse (juce::Rectangle<float> (10.0f * f, 10.0f * f).withCentre (pt), juce::jmax (1.5f, 1.5f * f));
  1729	                            }
  1730	                    if (juce::String (item.display) == "toxicwave" && item.param != nullptr) // round 168n: the wave's cycle
  1731	                    {
  1732	                        const juce::String name (item.param);
  1733	                        const auto n = name.fromFirstOccurrenceOf (" ", false, false).getIntValue();
  1734	                        const auto slot = name.startsWith ("LFO") ? 5 + n : n - 1;
  1735	                        const auto inner = r.reduced (5.0f * f, 6.0f * f);
  1736	                        const auto shape = toxicWaveShape (def.pluginState, def.samplePath, slot, 96);
  1737	                        if (shape.empty())
  1738	                        {
  1739	                            g.setColour (colour::textSecondary);
  1740	                            g.setFont (font (juce::jmax (8.0f, 9.5f * f)));
  1741	                            g.drawFittedText (tr ("Needs FL Studio's files"), inner.toNearestInt(), juce::Justification::centred, 2, 0.7f);
  1742	                        }
  1743	                        else
  1744	                        {
  1745	                            g.setColour (colour::gridBeat);
  1746	                            g.drawHorizontalLine ((int) inner.getCentreY(), inner.getX(), inner.getRight());
  1747	                            juce::Path wave;
  1748	                            for (int i = 0; i < (int) shape.size(); ++i)
  1749	                            {
  1750	                                const juce::Point<float> pt { inner.getX() + inner.getWidth() * (float) i / (float) (shape.size() - 1),
  1751	                                                              inner.getCentreY() - inner.getHeight() * 0.5f * shape[(std::size_t) i] };
  1752	                                if (i == 0)
  1753	                                    wave.startNewSubPath (pt);
  1754	                                else
  1755	                                    wave.lineTo (pt);
  1756	                            }
  1757	                            g.setColour (accent.withAlpha (0.25f));
  1758	                            g.strokePath (wave, juce::PathStrokeType (3.0f * f));
  1759	                            g.setColour (accent.brighter (0.3f));
  1760	                            g.strokePath (wave, juce::PathStrokeType (1.3f * f));
  1761	                        }
  1762	                    }
  1763	                    paintDrumDisplay (g, item, r, def, f);
  1764	                    // round 168o: the envelopes of the synth group's pages, drawn from their knobs (attack, decay, sustain, release;
  1765	                    // a missing stage is skipped, a missing sustain is the level the decay falls to: none)
  1766	                    {
  1767	                        struct Env
  1768	                        {
  1769	                            const char* id;
  1770	                            const char* a;
  1771	                            const char* d;
  1772	                            const char* s;
  1773	                            const char* r;
  1774	                            const char* start; // a modulator's initial level (DX10)
  1775	                        };
  1776	                        static const Env envs[] = {
  1777	                            { "dx10amp", "Attack", "Decay", nullptr, "Release", nullptr },
  1778	                            { "dx10mod1", nullptr, "Mod 1 time", "Mod 1 sustain", "Mod 1 release", "Mod 1 init" },
  1779	                            { "dx10mod2", nullptr, "Mod 2 time", "Mod 2 sustain", "Mod 2 release", "Mod 2 init" },
  1780	                            { "simamp", "Attack", "Decay", "Sustain", "Release", nullptr },
  1781	                            { "simfilt", "Filter attack", "Filter decay", "Filter sustain", "Filter release", nullptr },
  1782	                            { "ministenv", "Attack", "Decay", "Sustain", "Release", nullptr },
  1783	                        };
  1784	                        for (const auto& e : envs)
  1785	                            if (juce::String (item.display) == e.id)
  1786	                            {
  1787	                                const auto value = [&] (const char* name, float otherwise) {
  1788	                                    auto* kn = name != nullptr ? knobFor (name) : nullptr;
  1789	                                    return kn != nullptr ? juce::jlimit (0.0f, 1.0f, normalised (*kn)) : otherwise;
  1790	                                };
  1791	                                const auto inner = r.reduced (5.0f * f, 5.0f * f);
  1792	                                const auto a = value (e.a, 0.0f), d = value (e.d, 0.5f), su = value (e.s, 0.0f), rl = value (e.r, 0.3f);
  1793	                                const auto peak = e.start != nullptr ? value (e.start, 1.0f) : 1.0f;
  1794	                                const auto unit = inner.getWidth() / 4.0f;
  1795	                                const auto y = [&] (float level) { return inner.getBottom() - inner.getHeight() * level; };
  1796	                                juce::Path env;
  1797	                                auto x = inner.getX();
  1798	                                env.startNewSubPath (x, y (e.start != nullptr ? peak : 0.0f));
  1799	                                x += unit * a;
  1800	                                env.lineTo (x, y (peak));
  1801	                                // DX10's amp decay ≥ 0.98 holds the note
  1802	                                const auto hold = e.s != nullptr ? su : (d >= 0.98f ? peak : 0.0f);
  1803	                                x += unit * (0.15f + 0.85f * d);
  1804	                                env.lineTo (x, y (hold));
  1805	                                env.lineTo (inner.getRight() - unit * (0.15f + 0.85f * rl), y (hold));
  1806	                                env.lineTo (inner.getRight(), y (0.0f));
  1807	                                g.setColour (colour::gridBeat);
  1808	                                g.drawHorizontalLine ((int) inner.getBottom(), inner.getX(), inner.getRight());
  1809	                                g.setColour (accent.withAlpha (0.25f));
  1810	                                g.strokePath (env, juce::PathStrokeType (3.0f * f));
  1811	                                g.setColour (accent.brighter (0.3f));
  1812	                                g.strokePath (env, juce::PathStrokeType (1.3f * f));
  1813	                            }
  1814	                    }
  1815	                    if (juce::String (item.display) == "gmswaves") // round 168o: GMS's waves are read from FL Studio's folder
  1816	                    {
  1817	                        g.setColour (colour::textSecondary);
  1818	                        g.setFont (font (juce::jmax (8.0f, 9.5f * f)));
  1819	                        const auto text = def.samplePath.empty() ? tr ("Needs FL Studio's files")
  1820	                                                                 : juce::File (juce::String::fromUTF8 (def.samplePath.c_str())).getFileName();
  1821	                        g.drawFittedText (text, r.reduced (6.0f * f).toNearestInt(), juce::Justification::centred, 3, 0.7f);
  1822	                    }
  1823	                    if (juce::String (item.display) == "flkeysname")
  1824	                        if (const auto st = flkeys::parseState (def.pluginState))
  1825	                        {
  1826	                            g.setColour (colour::textPrimary);
  1827	                            g.setFont (font (juce::jmax (9.0f, 11.0f * f)));
  1828	                            g.drawFittedText (juce::String::fromUTF8 (st->wavetable.c_str()), r.reduced (6.0f * f, 0.0f).toNearestInt(),
  1829	                                              juce::Justification::centredLeft, 1, 0.8f);
  1830	                        }
  1831	                    break;
  1832	                default:
  1833	                    break;
  1834	            }
  1835	        }
  1836	    }
  1837	
  1838	    // round 168p: the displays of the drum pages (Drumaxx's kit / pads, BassDrum's noise sample, DrumSynth Live's
  1839	    // selected patch and its key display)
  1840	    static juce::String keyName (int key) { return juce::String (notenames::keyName (key)); }
  1841	
  1842	    juce::Rectangle<float> dslKeyCell (juce::Rectangle<float> r, int key) const
  1843	    {
  1844	        // the 120 patch keys in 4 rows of 30 (C0 … B9, FL's octave numbers)
  1845	        const auto w = r.getWidth() / 30.0f, h = r.getHeight() / 4.0f;
  1846	        return { r.getX() + w * (float) (key % 30), r.getY() + h * (float) (key / 30), w, h };
  1847	    }
  1848	
  1849	    void paintDrumDisplay (juce::Graphics& g, const plugui::LayoutItem& item, juce::Rectangle<float> r, const ChannelDef& def, float f)
  1850	    {
  1851	        using namespace theme;
  1852	        const juce::String id (item.display);
  1853	        auto text = [&] (const juce::String& t, juce::Colour c, juce::Justification j = juce::Justification::centred) {
  1854	            g.setColour (c);
  1855	            g.setFont (font (juce::jmax (8.0f, juce::jmin (12.0f * f, r.getHeight() * 0.62f))));
  1856	            g.drawFittedText (t, r.reduced (4.0f * f, 1.0f).toNearestInt(), j, 1, 0.7f);
  1857	        };
  1858	        if (id == "drumaxxkit")
  1859	        {
  1860	            const auto name = juce::String::fromUTF8 (drumaxxKitName (def.pluginState).c_str());
  1861	            text (name.isEmpty() ? tr ("(no kit name)") : name, colour::textPrimary, juce::Justification::centredLeft);
  1862	        }
  1863	        else if (id == "drumaxxpad")
  1864	        {
  1865	            const auto pad = item.steps;
  1866	            const auto keyValue = drumaxxParam (def.pluginState, 44 * pad + 4);
  1867	            const auto key = std::isfinite (keyValue) ? (int) keyValue : -1;
  1868	            skin::button (g, r.reduced (2.0f * f), pad == drumPart, false, accent, radius::md * f);
  1869	            g.setColour (pad == drumPart ? colour::onAccent : colour::textSecondary);
  1870	            g.setFont (font (juce::jmax (8.0f, 10.0f * f), true));
  1871	            g.drawFittedText (juce::String (pad + 1) + "\n" + (key >= 0 && key < 128 ? keyName (key) : juce::String()), r.toNearestInt(),
  1872	                              juce::Justification::centred, 2, 0.7f);
  1873	        }
  1874	        else if (id == "drumaxxpadname")
  1875	        {
  1876	            const auto name = juce::String::fromUTF8 (drumaxxPadName (def.pluginState, item.steps).c_str());
  1877	            text (name.isEmpty() ? tr ("Pad {0}", { juce::String (item.steps + 1) }) : name, item.steps == drumPart ? accent.brighter (0.4f) : colour::textSecondary,
  1878	                  juce::Justification::centredLeft);
  1879	        }
  1880	        else if (id == "bassdrumnoise")
  1881	        {
  1882	            if (! bassDrumStateUsable (def.pluginState) || def.samplePath.empty())
  1883	                text (tr ("Needs FL Studio's files"), colour::textSecondary);
  1884	            else
  1885	            {
  1886	                const auto name = juce::String::fromUTF8 (bassDrumNoiseName (def.pluginState).c_str());
  1887	                text (name.isEmpty() ? tr ("(no noise sample)") : name, colour::textPrimary, juce::Justification::centredLeft);
  1888	            }
  1889	        }
  1890	        else if (id == "dslpatch")
  1891	            text (keyName (drumPart) + "  (" + juce::String (drumPart) + ")", colour::textPrimary);
  1892	        else if (id == "dslsend" || id == "dslmorph")
  1893	        {
  1894	            const auto slot = drumSynthKeyToSlot (drumPart);
  1895	            const auto at = drumSynthPatchParams * slot + (id == "dslsend" ? 34 : 36);
  1896	            const auto v = slot >= 0 && at < (int) def.modelParams.size() ? (int) std::lround (def.modelParams[(std::size_t) at]) : -1;
  1897	            text (id == "dslsend" ? (v < 0 ? juce::String ("-") : juce::String (v)) : (v < 0 || v > 119 ? tr ("(none)") : keyName (v)), colour::textPrimary);
  1898	        }
  1899	        else if (id == "dslkeys")
  1900	        {
  1901	            const auto inner = r.reduced (3.0f * f);
  1902	            for (int key = 0; key < drumSynthPatches; ++key)
  1903	            {
  1904	                const auto cell = dslKeyCell (inner, key).reduced (0.5f * f);
  1905	                const auto slot = drumSynthKeyToSlot (key);
  1906	                const auto level = (std::size_t) (drumSynthPatchParams * slot) < def.modelParams.size() ? def.modelParams[(std::size_t) (drumSynthPatchParams * slot)] : 0.0f;
  1907	                static constexpr bool blackKey[12] = { false, true, false, true, false, false, true, false, true, false, true, false };
  1908	                const auto black = blackKey[key % 12];
  1909	                g.setColour (key == drumPart ? accent : level > 0.0f ? accent.withAlpha (0.35f) : black ? colour::bg1 : colour::bg4);
  1910	                g.fillRect (cell);
  1911	                if (key % 12 == 0)
  1912	                {
  1913	                    g.setColour (key == drumPart ? colour::onAccent : colour::textSecondary);
  1914	                    g.setFont (font (juce::jmax (6.5f, juce::jmin (8.0f * f, cell.getHeight() * 0.6f))));
  1915	                    g.drawText (juce::String (key / 12), cell, juce::Justification::centred, false);
  1916	                }
  1917	            }
  1918	        }
  1919	    }
  1920	
  1921	    // a click on a pad button / the key display selects the part the knobs edit; true when handled
  1922	    bool clickDrumDisplay (const plugui::LayoutItem& item, juce::Point<float> at)
  1923	    {
  1924	        if (item.type != plugui::Item::display || item.display == nullptr || ! layoutRect (item).contains (at) || ! conditionHolds (item))
  1925	            return false;
  1926	        const juce::String id (item.display);
  1927	        if (drumaxx && (id == "drumaxxpad" || id == "drumaxxpadname"))
  1928	        {
  1929	            selectPart (item.steps);
  1930	            return true;
  1931	        }
  1932	        if (drumsynth && id == "dslkeys")
  1933	        {
  1934	            const auto inner = layoutRect (item).reduced (3.0f * layoutFactor());
  1935	            for (int key = 0; key < drumSynthPatches; ++key)
  1936	                if (dslKeyCell (inner, key).contains (at))
  1937	                {
  1938	                    selectPart (key);
  1939	                    return true;
  1940	                }
  1941	        }
  1942	        return false;
  1943	    }
  1944	
  1945	    void resizeLayout()
  1946	    {
  1947	        for (auto& k : knobs)
  1948	        {
  1949	            const auto* item = itemFor (k->getName());
  1950	            if (item == nullptr) // (hidden knobs too: another page's knobs keep their place for when the page shows)
  1951	                continue;
  1952	            auto r = layoutRect (*item);
  1953	            if ((item->type == plugui::Item::vslider || item->type == plugui::Item::hslider) && (item->style & plugui::noCaption) == 0 && item->label != nullptr)
  1954	                r.removeFromBottom (13.0f * layoutFactor());
  1955	            if (item->type == plugui::Item::knob)
  1956	            {
  1957	                if ((item->style & plugui::noCaption) == 0)
  1958	                    r.removeFromBottom (14.0f * layoutFactor());
  1959	                const auto d = juce::jmin (r.getWidth(), r.getHeight());
  1960	                r = r.withSizeKeepingCentre (d, d).withY (r.getY());
  1961	            }
  1962	            k->setBounds (r.toNearestInt());
  1963	        }
  1964	    }
  1965	
  1966	    // a stepper's number moves one unit (the engine's own units for a 0…1 number box, round 169h)
  1967	    const EngineParam* unitParam (const juce::Slider& k) const
  1968	    {
  1969	        const auto* p = modelparams::findByName (instrumentKind, k.getName().toStdString());
  1970	        return p != nullptr && p->hasUnits() ? p : nullptr;
  1971	    }
  1972	    void stepBy (juce::Slider& k, int delta)
  1973	    {
  1974	        if (const auto* p = unitParam (k))
  1975	            k.setValue (p->rawOfUnit (p->unitOf ((float) k.getValue()) + delta), juce::sendNotificationSync);
  4250	                     TR_NOOP ("Mod X"));
  4251	        modY = knob ([name] { return ui::percentSpec (name() + " - " + tr ("Mod Y (filter resonance)"), 0.0); },
  4252	                     [] (const ChannelDef& d) { return (double) (d.instrument == InstrumentKind::synth ? d.synth.resonance : d.sampler.resonance); },
  4253	                     [] (ChannelDef& d, double v) {
  4254	                         if (d.instrument == InstrumentKind::synth)
  4255	                             d.synth.resonance = (float) v;
  4256	                         else if (d.instrument == InstrumentKind::sampler)
  4257	                         {
  4258	                             d.sampler.resonance = (float) v;
  4259	                             d.sampler.flChannel.clear();
  4260	                         }
  4261	                     },
  4262	                     TR_NOOP ("Mod Y"));
  4263	        // Time: Gate and Shift on FL's time-knob scale (REA round 146b)
  4264	        auto timeSpec = [name] (const char* what, int maximum, int defaultValue, bool offAtMax) {
  4265	            ui::ValueSpec s;
  4266	            s.name = name() + " - " + tr (what);
  4267	            s.maximum = maximum;
  4268	            s.defaultValue = defaultValue;
  4269	            s.interval = 1.0;
  4270	            s.pixelsForRange = 300.0;
  4271	            s.toText = [maximum, offAtMax] (double v) {
  4272	                const auto i = juce::roundToInt (v);
  4273	                if (offAtMax && i >= maximum)
  4274	                    return tr ("Off");
  4275	                // steps:ticks, as FL shows them (24 ticks per step)
  4276	                const auto ticks = juce::roundToInt (channelmisc::flTimeKnobBeats (i) * 96.0);
  4277	                return juce::String (ticks / 24) + ":" + juce::String (ticks % 24).paddedLeft ('0', 2);
  4278	            };
  4279	            return s;
  4280	        };
  4281	        timeGate = knob ([timeSpec] { return timeSpec (TR_NOOP ("Gate: long notes are cut to this length (Off = no limit)"), 1446, 1446, true); },
  4282	                         [] (const ChannelDef& d) { return d.misc.gateBeats > 0.0f ? (double) channelmisc::flTimeKnobValue (d.misc.gateBeats) : 1446.0; },
  4283	                         [] (ChannelDef& d, double v) {
  4284	                             const auto i = juce::roundToInt (v);
  4285	                             d.misc.gateBeats = i >= 1446 ? 0.0f : (float) std::max (1.0 / 96.0, channelmisc::flTimeKnobBeats (i));
  4286	                         },
  4287	                         TR_NOOP ("Gate"));
  4288	        timeShift = knob ([timeSpec] { return timeSpec (TR_NOOP ("Fine time shift: delays the notes, up to one step"), 1024, 0, false); },
  4289	                          [] (const ChannelDef& d) { return (double) channelmisc::flTimeKnobValue (d.misc.shiftBeats); },
  4290	                          [] (ChannelDef& d, double v) { d.misc.shiftBeats = (float) channelmisc::flTimeKnobBeats (juce::roundToInt (v)); },
  4291	                          TR_NOOP ("Shift"));
  4292	        fullPorta.setButtonText (tr ("Full porta"));
  4293	        fullPorta.setTooltip (tr ("Full porta: portamento notes ignore the Gate and play their whole length"));
  4294	        fullPorta.onClick = [this] { edit ([on = fullPorta.getToggleState()] (ChannelDef& d) { d.misc.fullPorta = on; }); };
  4295	        // Vol / Key tracking
  4296	        trackVol.setButtonText (tr ("Vol"));
  4297	        trackKey.setButtonText (trc ("@key tracking: Key"));
  4298	        for (auto* b : { &trackVol, &trackKey })
  4299	        {
  4300	            b->setClickingTogglesState (true);
  4301	            b->setRadioGroupId (146);
  4302	            b->onClick = [this] { repaint(); for (auto& k : knobs) k->repaint(); };
  4303	            addAndMakeVisible (b);
  4304	        }
  4305	        trackVol.setTooltip (tr ("Velocity tracking: the note's velocity moves its pan"));
  4306	        trackKey.setTooltip (tr ("Keyboard tracking: the note's key moves its pan"));
  4307	        trackKey.setToggleState (true, juce::dontSendNotification);
  4308	        auto tracking = [this] (ChannelDef& d) -> NoteTracking& { return trackKey.getToggleState() ? d.misc.key : d.misc.velocity; };
  4309	        auto trackingOf = [this] (const ChannelDef& d) -> const NoteTracking& { return trackKey.getToggleState() ? d.misc.key : d.misc.velocity; };
  4310	        trackPan = knob ([name] { return ui::panSpec (name() + " - " + tr ("Tracking: pan")); },
  4311	                         [trackingOf] (const ChannelDef& d) { return (double) trackingOf (d).pan; },
  4312	                         [tracking] (ChannelDef& d, double v) { tracking (d).pan = (float) v; }, TR_NOOP ("Pan"));
  4313	        trackMid = knob ([this, name] {
  4314	                             const auto key = trackKey.getToggleState();
  4315	                             auto s = countSpec (name() + " - " + tr ("Tracking: the middle value (no offset)"), key ? 131 : 128, key ? 60 : 100,
  4316	                                                 [key] (int v) { return key ? juce::MidiMessage::getMidiNoteName (120 - v, true, true, 5) : juce::String (v); }); // FL: C5 = 60
  4317	                             return s;
  4318	                         },
  4319	                         [trackingOf] (const ChannelDef& d) { return (double) trackingOf (d).mid; },
  4320	                         [tracking] (ChannelDef& d, double v) { tracking (d).mid = juce::roundToInt (v); }, TR_NOOP ("Mid"));
  4321	        // FL's tracking Mod X / Y (round 151): the notes' Mod X / Y follow the velocity / key like their pan
  4322	        trackModX = knob ([name] { return ui::panSpec (name() + " - " + tr ("Tracking: Mod X (the note's filter cutoff / a plugin's MOD X)")); },
  4323	                          [trackingOf] (const ChannelDef& d) { return (double) trackingOf (d).modX; },
  4324	                          [tracking] (ChannelDef& d, double v) { tracking (d).modX = (float) v; }, TR_NOOP ("Mod X"));
  4325	        trackModY = knob ([name] { return ui::panSpec (name() + " - " + tr ("Tracking: Mod Y (the note's filter resonance)")); },
  4326	                          [trackingOf] (const ChannelDef& d) { return (double) trackingOf (d).modY; },
  4327	                          [tracking] (ChannelDef& d, double v) { tracking (d).modY = (float) v; }, TR_NOOP ("Mod Y"));
  4328	        // Echo delay / fat mode
  4329	        echoFeed = knob ([name] { return ui::percentSpec (name() + " - " + tr ("Delay feedback (100% = the echoes as loud as the note)"), 0.0); },
  4330	                         [] (const ChannelDef& d) { return (double) d.misc.echo.feedback / 2.0; },
  4331	                         [] (ChannelDef& d, double v) { d.misc.echo.feedback = (float) (2.0 * v); }, TR_NOOP ("Feed"));
  4332	        echoPan = knob ([name] { return ui::panSpec (name() + " - " + tr ("Delay pan: each echo moves this much")); },
  4333	                        [] (const ChannelDef& d) { return (double) d.misc.echo.pan; }, [] (ChannelDef& d, double v) { d.misc.echo.pan = (float) v; },
  4334	                        TR_NOOP ("Pan"));
  4335	        echoModX = knob ([name] { return ui::panSpec (name() + " - " + tr ("Delay Mod X: each echo moves the note's Mod X this much")); },
  4336	                         [] (const ChannelDef& d) { return (double) d.misc.echo.modX; }, [] (ChannelDef& d, double v) { d.misc.echo.modX = (float) v; },
  4337	                         TR_NOOP ("Mod X"));
  4338	        echoModY = knob ([name] { return ui::panSpec (name() + " - " + tr ("Delay Mod Y: each echo moves the note's Mod Y this much")); },
  4339	                         [] (const ChannelDef& d) { return (double) d.misc.echo.modY; }, [] (ChannelDef& d, double v) { d.misc.echo.modY = (float) v; },
  4340	                         TR_NOOP ("Mod Y"));
  4341	        echoPitch = knob ([name] {
  4342	                              ui::ValueSpec s;
  4343	                              s.name = name() + " - " + tr ("Delay pitch: each echo moves this many cents");
  4344	                              s.minimum = -1200.0;
  4345	                              s.maximum = 1200.0;
  4346	                              s.interval = 1.0;
  4347	                              s.toText = [] (double v) { return tr ("{0} cents", { juce::String (juce::roundToInt (v)) }); };
  4348	                              return s;
  4349	                          },
  4350	                          [] (const ChannelDef& d) { return (double) d.misc.echo.pitch; }, [] (ChannelDef& d, double v) { d.misc.echo.pitch = (float) v; },
  4351	                          TR_NOOP ("Pitch"));
  4352	        echoTime = knob ([name] {
  4353	                             ui::ValueSpec s;
  4354	                             s.name = name() + " - " + tr ("Delay time (steps:ticks)");
  4355	                             s.minimum = 1.0;
  4356	                             s.maximum = 192.0 * 4.0;
  4357	                             s.defaultValue = 144.0;
  4358	                             s.interval = 1.0;
  4359	                             s.pixelsForRange = 400.0;
  4360	                             s.toText = [] (double v) { const auto t = juce::roundToInt (v); return juce::String (t / 48) + ":" + juce::String ((t % 48) / 2).paddedLeft ('0', 2); };
  4361	                             return s;
  4362	                         },
  4363	                         [] (const ChannelDef& d) { return (double) d.misc.echo.timeBeats * 192.0; },
  4364	                         [] (ChannelDef& d, double v) { d.misc.echo.timeBeats = (float) (juce::roundToInt (v) / 192.0); }, TR_NOOP ("Time"));
  4365	        echoCount = knob ([name] { return countSpec (name() + " - " + tr ("Delay number of echoes"), 10, 4); },
  4366	                          [] (const ChannelDef& d) { return (double) d.misc.echo.echoes; },
  4367	                          [] (ChannelDef& d, double v) { d.misc.echo.echoes = juce::jlimit (1, 10, juce::roundToInt (v)); }, TR_NOOP ("Echoes"));
  4368	        pingPong.setButtonText (tr ("Ping pong"));
  4369	        pingPong.setTooltip (tr ("Ping pong: the echoes' pan bounces off the left and right ends"));
  4370	        pingPong.onClick = [this] { edit ([on = pingPong.getToggleState()] (ChannelDef& d) { d.misc.echo.pingPong = on; }); };
  4371	        fatMode.setButtonText (tr ("Fat mode"));
  4372	        fatMode.setTooltip (tr ("Fat mode: a second, mirrored series of echoes (opposite pan and pitch steps), all at the feedback level"));
  4373	        fatMode.onClick = [this] { edit ([on = fatMode.getToggleState()] (ChannelDef& d) { d.misc.echo.fat = on; }); };
  4374	        // Root note row: main pitch
  4375	        mainPitch.setButtonText (tr ("Enable main pitch"));
  4376	        mainPitch.setTooltip (tr ("The channel follows the main pitch (the toolbar's master pitch)"));
  4377	        mainPitch.onClick = [this] { edit ([on = mainPitch.getToggleState()] (ChannelDef& d) { d.misc.mainPitch = on; }); };
  4378	        // FL's Add to key and Root note keyboard (round 149): a sampler's root moves its pitch, other instruments are
  4379	        // transposed by (C5 − root) with Add to key
  4380	        addToKey.setButtonText (tr ("Add to key"));
  4381	        addToKey.setTooltip (tr ("Add to key: the notes are played the root note's distance from C5 away (instruments that are not samplers)"));
  4382	        addToKey.onClick = [this] { edit ([on = addToKey.getToggleState()] (ChannelDef& d) { d.misc.addToKey = on; }); };
  4383	        // FL's Root note › Fine tune (round 151 [DATA: .flp event 0x8E, cents]): every note of the channel this many
  4384	        // cents away
  4385	        fineTune = knob ([name] {
  4386	                             ui::ValueSpec s;
  4387	                             s.name = name() + " - " + tr ("Fine tuning");
  4388	                             s.minimum = -100.0;
  4389	                             s.maximum = 100.0;
  4390	                             s.interval = 1.0;
  4391	                             s.toText = [] (double v) { return tr ("{0} cents", { (v > 0.0 ? "+" : "") + juce::String (juce::roundToInt (v)) }); };
  4392	                             return s;
  4393	                         },
  4394	                         [] (const ChannelDef& d) { return (double) d.misc.fineTune; }, [] (ChannelDef& d, double v) { d.misc.fineTune = (float) v; },
  4395	                         TR_NOOP ("Fine tune"));
  4396	        rootReset.setButtonText (tr ("Reset"));
  4397	        rootReset.setTooltip (tr ("Reset the root note to C5"));
  4398	        rootReset.onClick = [this] { if (rootKeys.onPick) rootKeys.onPick (60); };
  4399	        addAndMakeVisible (rootReset);
  4400	        rootKeys.onPick = [this] (int key) {
  4401	            edit ([key] (ChannelDef& d) {
  4402	                if (d.instrument == InstrumentKind::sampler)
  4403	                {
  4404	                    // the sample plays at its own pitch on the root: the pitch moves by the root's change (within ±24)
  4405	                    const auto pitch = std::clamp (d.sampler.pitch + (float) (d.misc.rootNote - key), -24.0f, 24.0f);
  4406	                    const auto moved = (int) std::lround (pitch - d.sampler.pitch);
  4407	                    d.sampler.pitch = pitch;
  4408	                    d.misc.rootNote = std::clamp (d.misc.rootNote - moved, 0, 127);
  4409	                }
  4410	                else
  4411	                    d.misc.rootNote = key;
  4412	            });
  4413	        };
  4414	        rootKeys.onRegion = [this] (int low, int high) {
  4415	            edit ([low, high] (ChannelDef& d) {
  4416	                d.keyLow = juce::jlimit (0, 127, low);
  4417	                d.keyHigh = juce::jlimit (d.keyLow, 127, high);
  4418	            });
  4419	        };
  4420	        addAndMakeVisible (addToKey);
  4421	        addAndMakeVisible (rootKeys);
  4422	        // Arpeggiator: FL's Slide and Chord
  4423	        arpSlide.setButtonText (tr ("Slide"));
  4424	        arpSlide.setTooltip (tr ("Slide: the arpeggio glides from note to note (instruments that slide)"));
  4425	        arpSlide.onClick = [this] { edit ([on = arpSlide.getToggleState()] (ChannelDef& d) { d.arp.slide = on; }); };
  4426	        arpChord.addItem (tr ("Auto (sustain)"), 1);
  4427	        arpChord.addItem (tr ("None (octaves)"), 2);
  4428	        for (const auto& [chordName, intervals] : arpChords())
  4429	            arpChord.addItem (tr (chordName), arpChord.getNumItems() + 1);
  4430	        arpChord.setTooltip (tr ("Chord: Auto plays the held notes; a chord plays it on the lowest held note"));
  4431	        arpChord.onChange = [this] {
  4432	            const auto id = arpChord.getSelectedId();
  4433	            if (id <= 0)
  4434	                return;
  4435	            edit ([id] (ChannelDef& d) {
  4436	                if (id == 1)
  4437	                    d.arp.chord.clear();
  4438	                else if (id == 2)
  4439	                    d.arp.chord = { 0 };
  4440	                else if (id - 3 < (int) arpChords().size())
  4441	                    d.arp.chord = arpChords()[(std::size_t) (id - 3)].second;
  4442	            });
  4443	        };
  4444	        for (auto* b : { &fullPorta, &pingPong, &fatMode, &mainPitch, &arpSlide })
  4445	            addAndMakeVisible (b);
  4446	        addAndMakeVisible (arpChord);
  4447	        // Group
  4448	        cut = knob ([this, name] { return countSpec (name() + " - " + tr ("Cut: notes of this channel stop the channels cut by this group"), 
  4449	                                                     juce::jmax (99, current() != nullptr ? current()->cutGroup : 0), 0,
  4450	                                                     [this] (int g) { return juce::String (cutGroupText (g, channel)); }); },
  4451	                    [] (const ChannelDef& d) { return (double) d.cutGroup; }, [] (ChannelDef& d, double v) { d.cutGroup = juce::jmax (0, juce::roundToInt (v)); },
  4452	                    TR_NOOP ("Cut group"));
  4453	        cutBy = knob ([this, name] { return countSpec (name() + " - " + tr ("By: this channel is cut by the channels of this group"),
  4454	                                                       juce::jmax (99, current() != nullptr ? current()->cutBy : 0), 0,
  4455	                                                       [this] (int g) { return juce::String (cutGroupText (g, channel)); }); },
  4456	                      [] (const ChannelDef& d) { return (double) d.cutBy; }, [] (ChannelDef& d, double v) { d.cutBy = juce::jmax (0, juce::roundToInt (v)); },
  4457	                      TR_NOOP ("Cut by"));
  4458	        cutSelf.setButtonText (tr ("Cut self"));
  4459	        cutSelf.setClickingTogglesState (true);
  4460	        cutSelf.setTooltip (tr ("Cut self: a new note of this channel stops its own sounding note (hi-hats, 808s)"));
  4461	        cutSelf.onClick = [this] { edit ([on = cutSelf.getToggleState(), ch = channel] (ChannelDef& d) { setChannelCutsItself (d, ch, on); }); };
  4462	        // Arpeggiator
  4463	        arpDirection.addItemList ({ tr ("Off"), tr ("Up"), tr ("Down"), tr ("Up and down"), tr ("Random"), tr ("Up and down (sticky)") }, 1);
  4464	        arpDirection.setTooltip (tr ("Arpeggiator direction (Off = the notes play as written)"));
  4465	        arpDirection.onChange = [this] { edit ([d = arpDirection.getSelectedId() - 1] (ChannelDef& c) { c.arp.direction = juce::jlimit (0, 5, d); }); };
  4466	        for (const auto& [text, beats] : arpSpeeds())
  4467	            arpTime.addItem (tr (text), arpTime.getNumItems() + 1);
  4468	        arpTime.setTooltip (tr ("Arpeggiator time: the length of each step"));
  4469	        arpTime.onChange = [this] {
  4470	            const auto i = arpTime.getSelectedId() - 1;
  4471	            if (i >= 0 && i < (int) arpSpeeds().size())
  4472	                edit ([b = arpSpeeds()[(std::size_t) i].second] (ChannelDef& c) { c.arp.stepBeats = b; });
  4473	        };
  4474	        arpRange = knob ([name] { return countSpec (name() + " - " + tr ("Arpeggiator range (octaves)"), 4, 1); },
  4475	                         [] (const ChannelDef& d) { return (double) d.arp.range; },
  4476	                         [] (ChannelDef& d, double v) { d.arp.range = juce::jlimit (1, 4, juce::roundToInt (v)); }, TR_NOOP ("Range"));
  4477	        arpGate = knob ([name] {
  4478	                            auto s = ui::percentSpec (name() + " - " + tr ("Arpeggio gate (past 100% the next note waits)"), 0.5);
  4479	                            s.minimum = 0.05;
  4480	                            s.maximum = 128.0 / 48.0;
  4481	                            return s;
  4482	                        },
  4483	                        [] (const ChannelDef& d) { return (double) d.arp.gate; },
  4484	                        [] (ChannelDef& d, double v) { d.arp.gate = juce::jlimit (0.05f, 128.0f / 48.0f, (float) v); }, TR_NOOP ("Gate"));
  4485	        // FL's Repeat 0 plays the whole chord at once on each step (round 149)
  4486	        arpRepeat = knob ([name] { return countSpec (name() + " - " + tr ("Arpeggiator repeat: times the arpeggio plays in an octave before the next one (0: the chord at once)"), 16, 1,
  4487	                                                     [] (int i) { return i == 0 ? tr ("Chord") : juce::String (i); }); },
  4488	                          [] (const ChannelDef& d) { return (double) d.arp.octaveRepeat; },
  4489	                          [] (ChannelDef& d, double v) { d.arp.octaveRepeat = juce::jlimit (0, 16, juce::roundToInt (v)); }, TR_NOOP ("Repeat"));
  4490	
  4491	        for (auto* b : { &porta, &mono })
  4492	            addAndMakeVisible (b);
  4493	        addAndMakeVisible (cutSelf);
  4494	        addAndMakeVisible (arpDirection);
  4495	        addAndMakeVisible (arpTime);
  4496	        refresh();
  4497	        engine.addChangeListener (this);
  4498	        setSize (preferredWidth, preferredHeight);
  4499	    }

  4501	    ~MiscPanel() override { engine.removeChangeListener (this); }
  4502	
  4503	    void paint (juce::Graphics& g) override
  4504	    {
  4505	        g.fillAll (theme::colour::bg2);
  4506	        for (const auto& [area, title] : sections)
  4507	        {
  4508	            g.setColour (theme::colour::bg3);
  4509	            g.fillRoundedRectangle (area.toFloat(), 4.0f);
  4510	            g.setColour (theme::colour::textSecondary);
  4511	            g.setFont (theme::font (theme::type::caption, true));
  4512	            auto text = theme::caps (title);
  4513	            if (&title == &sections[(std::size_t) juce::jmax (0, rootSection)].second && rootSection >= 0 && current() != nullptr)
  4514	                text << ": " << RootKeyboard::keyName (current()->misc.rootNote); // FL's "Root note: C5"
  4515	            g.drawText (text, area.withHeight (20).reduced (8, 0), juce::Justification::centredLeft);
  4516	        }
  4517	    }
  4518	
  4519	    void resized() override
  4520	    {
  4521	        sections.clear();
  4522	        auto area = getLocalBounds().reduced (8);
  4523	        auto section = [this] (juce::Rectangle<int> r, const juce::String& title) {
  4524	            sections.push_back ({ r, title });
  4525	            return r.withTrimmedTop (20).reduced (6, 4);
  4526	        };
  4527	        constexpr int knobW = 50, knobH = 64;
  4528	        auto row = [] (juce::Rectangle<int>& r, std::initializer_list<FrameKnob*> ks) {
  4529	            auto line = r.removeFromTop (knobH);
  4530	            for (auto* k : ks)
  4531	                k->setBounds (line.removeFromLeft (knobW));
  4532	        };
  4533	        auto top = area.removeFromTop (118);
  4534	        {
  4535	            auto levels = section (top.removeFromLeft (4 * knobW + 12), tr ("Levels adjustment"));
  4536	            row (levels, { pan, volume, modX, modY });
  4537	        }
  4538	        top.removeFromLeft (6);
  4539	        {
  4540	            auto poly = section (top.removeFromLeft (2 * knobW + 30), tr ("Polyphony"));
  4541	            row (poly, { maxPoly, slide });
  4542	            auto toggles = poly.removeFromTop (22);
  4543	            porta.setBounds (toggles.removeFromLeft (64));
  4544	            mono.setBounds (toggles);
  4545	        }
  4546	        top.removeFromLeft (6);
  4547	        {
  4548	            auto time = section (top.removeFromLeft (3 * knobW + 12), tr ("Time"));
  4549	            row (time, { timeGate, timeShift, swing });
  4550	            fullPorta.setBounds (time.removeFromTop (22));
  4551	        }
  4552	        top.removeFromLeft (6);
  4553	        {
  4554	            auto track = section (top, tr ("Tracking"));
  4555	            auto tabs = track.removeFromTop (0);
  4556	            juce::ignoreUnused (tabs);
  4557	            const auto title = sections.back().first;
  4558	            trackVol.setBounds (title.getRight() - 84, title.getY() + 2, 38, 16);
  4559	            trackKey.setBounds (title.getRight() - 44, title.getY() + 2, 38, 16);
  4560	            row (track, { trackPan, trackModX, trackModY, trackMid });
  4561	        }
  4562	        area.removeFromTop (6);
  4563	        auto bottom = area.removeFromTop (130);
  4564	        {
  4565	            auto group = section (bottom.removeFromLeft (2 * knobW + 16), tr ("Group"));
  4566	            row (group, { cut, cutBy });
  4567	            cutSelf.setBounds (group.removeFromTop (24).reduced (4, 0));
  4568	        }
  4569	        bottom.removeFromLeft (6);
  4570	        {
  4571	            auto arp = section (bottom.removeFromLeft (330), tr ("Arpeggiator"));
  4572	            auto boxes = arp.removeFromLeft (118);
  4573	            arpDirection.setBounds (boxes.removeFromTop (22));
  4574	            boxes.removeFromTop (4);
  4575	            arpTime.setBounds (boxes.removeFromTop (22));
  4576	            boxes.removeFromTop (4);
  4577	            arpChord.setBounds (boxes.removeFromTop (22));
  4578	            boxes.removeFromTop (4);
  4579	            arpSlide.setBounds (boxes.removeFromTop (22));
  4580	            arp.removeFromLeft (6);
  4581	            row (arp, { arpRange, arpGate, arpRepeat });
  4582	        }
  4583	        bottom.removeFromLeft (6);
  4584	        {
  4585	            auto echo = section (bottom, tr ("Echo delay / fat mode"));
  4586	            {
  4587	                // seven knobs (round 151 added Mod X / Y): narrower when the window is
  4588	                auto line = echo.removeFromTop (knobH);
  4589	                const auto w = juce::jmin (knobW, line.getWidth() / 7);
  4590	                for (auto* k : { echoFeed, echoPan, echoModX, echoModY, echoPitch, echoTime, echoCount })
  4591	                    k->setBounds (line.removeFromLeft (w));
  4592	            }
  4593	            auto toggles = echo.removeFromTop (22);
  4594	            pingPong.setBounds (toggles.removeFromLeft (100));
  4595	            fatMode.setBounds (toggles.removeFromLeft (100));
  4596	        }
  4597	        area.removeFromTop (6);
  4598	        {
  4599	            auto rootRow = section (area.removeFromTop (74), tr ("Root note"));
  4600	            rootSection = (int) sections.size() - 1;
  5440	        case InstrumentKind::drumpad:
  5441	        case InstrumentKind::boobass:
  5442	        case InstrumentKind::drumsynth:
  5443	        case InstrumentKind::harmless:
  5444	        case InstrumentKind::harmor:
  5445	        case InstrumentKind::gms:
  5446	        case InstrumentKind::morphine:
  5447	        case InstrumentKind::ogun:
  5448	        case InstrumentKind::minisynth:
  5449	        case InstrumentKind::dx10:
  5450	        case InstrumentKind::kepler:
  5451	        case InstrumentKind::keplerExo:
  5452	        case InstrumentKind::poizone:
  5453	        case InstrumentKind::fruitkick:
  5454	        case InstrumentKind::bassdrum:
  5455	        case InstrumentKind::toxic:
  5456	        case InstrumentKind::threeosc:
  5457	        case InstrumentKind::autogun:
  5458	        case InstrumentKind::transistorbass:
  5459	        case InstrumentKind::simsynth:
  5460	        case InstrumentKind::wasp:
  5461	        case InstrumentKind::plucked:
  5462	        case InstrumentKind::beepmap:
  5463	        case InstrumentKind::sawer:
  5464	        case InstrumentKind::flkeys:
  5465	        case InstrumentKind::granulizer:
  5466	        case InstrumentKind::drumaxx:

  5468	        case InstrumentKind::fruitydance:
  5469	        case InstrumentKind::sytrus:

  5471	        case InstrumentKind::layer:

  5481	
  5482	bool isChannelSettingsOpen (int channel)
  5483	{
  5484	    return std::any_of (windows().begin(), windows().end(), [channel] (auto& w) { return w->getChannel() == channel && ! w->isModulation(); });
  5485	}
  5486	
  5487	void closeChannelSettings (int channel)
  5488	{
  5489	    auto& open = windows();
  5490	    open.erase (std::remove_if (open.begin(), open.end(), [channel] (auto& w) { return w->getChannel() == channel && ! w->isModulation(); }),
  5491	                open.end());
  5492	}
  5493	
  5494	std::unique_ptr<juce::Component> makeChannelModulationPanel (AudioEngine& engine, int channel)
  5495	{
  5496	    return std::make_unique<ModulationPanel> (engine, ChannelRef::rack (channel));
  5497	}
  5498	
  5499	std::unique_ptr<juce::Component> makeChannelPrecomputePanel (AudioEngine& engine, int channel)
  5500	{
  5501	    return std::make_unique<PrecomputePanel> (engine, ChannelRef::rack (channel));
  5502	}
  5503	
  5504	int showChannelLayoutPage (juce::Component& frame, int page)
  5505	{
  5506	    if (auto* panel = dynamic_cast<ModelPanel*> (&frame); panel != nullptr && panel->pageCount() > 0)
  5507	    {
  5508	        const auto count = panel->pageCount();
  5509	        if (page >= 0 && page < count)
  5510	            panel->showPage (page);
  5511	        return count;
  5512	    }
  5513	    for (auto* child : frame.getChildren())
  5514	        if (const auto count = showChannelLayoutPage (*child, page); count > 0)
  5515	            return count;
  5516	    return 0;
  5517	}
  5518	
  5519	int channelFrameHeaderHeight() noexcept
  5520	{
  5521	    return ChannelFrame::headerHeight;
  5522	}
  5523	
  5524	std::unique_ptr<juce::Component> makeChannelFrame (AudioEngine& engine, int channel, int tab)
  5525	{
  5526	    const auto kind = engine.getSong().channels[(std::size_t) channel].instrument;
  5527	    auto frame = std::make_unique<ChannelFrame> (engine, ChannelRef::rack (channel), makeChannelSettingsPanel (engine, channel),
  5528	                                                 hasEnvelopeTab (kind) ? std::make_unique<ModulationPanel> (engine, ChannelRef::rack (channel)) : nullptr);
  5529	    frame->showTab (tab < 0 ? frame->tabCount() - 1 : tab);
  5530	    return frame;
  5531	}
  5532	
  5533	std::unique_ptr<juce::Component> makeChannelSettingsPanel (AudioEngine& engine, int channel)
  5534	{
  5535	    if (engine.getSong().channels[(std::size_t) channel].instrument == InstrumentKind::synth)
  5536	        return std::make_unique<SynthPanel> (engine, ChannelRef::rack (channel));
  5537	    if (engine.getSong().channels[(std::size_t) channel].instrument == InstrumentKind::controller)
  5538	    {
  5539	        if (parseKeyboardController (engine.getSong().channels[(std::size_t) channel].controllerState))
  5540	            return makeKeyboardControllerEditor (engine, channel); // round 165c (it showed the Envelope Controller's read-only page)
  5541	        return makeEnvelopeControllerEditor (engine, channel);
  5542	    }
  5543	    if (engine.getSong().channels[(std::size_t) channel].instrument == InstrumentKind::midiOut)
  5544	        return makeMidiOutEditor (engine, channel);
  5545	    if (engine.getSong().channels[(std::size_t) channel].instrument == InstrumentKind::soundfont)
  5546	        return makeSoundFontEditor (engine, channel);
  5547	    if (engine.getSong().channels[(std::size_t) channel].instrument == InstrumentKind::layer)
  5548	        return makeLayerEditor (engine, channel);
  5549	    if (engine.getSong().channels[(std::size_t) channel].instrument == InstrumentKind::dashboard)
  5550	        return makeDashboardEditor (engine, channel);
  5551	    if (engine.getSong().channels[(std::size_t) channel].instrument == InstrumentKind::voltage)
  5552	        return makeVoltageControllerEditor (engine, channel);
  5553	    if (engine.getSong().channels[(std::size_t) channel].instrument == InstrumentKind::vfx)
  5554	        return makeVfxEditor (engine, channel);
  5555	    if (engine.getSong().channels[(std::size_t) channel].instrument == InstrumentKind::fruitydance)
  5556	        return makeDanceEditor (engine, channel); // round 165e
  5557	    if (engine.getSong().channels[(std::size_t) channel].instrument == InstrumentKind::videoplayer)
  5558	        return makeVideoPlayerEditor (engine, channel); // round 165g
  5559	    if (engine.getSong().channels[(std::size_t) channel].instrument == InstrumentKind::wavetraveller)
  5560	        return makeWaveTravellerEditor (engine, channel); // round 167f
  5561	    if (const auto kind = engine.getSong().channels[(std::size_t) channel].instrument;
  5562	        kind == InstrumentKind::sakura || kind == InstrumentKind::drumpad || kind == InstrumentKind::boobass
  5563	                 || kind == InstrumentKind::drumsynth || kind == InstrumentKind::harmless || kind == InstrumentKind::harmor
  5564	                 || kind == InstrumentKind::gms || kind == InstrumentKind::morphine
  5565	                 || kind == InstrumentKind::ogun || kind == InstrumentKind::minisynth
  5566	                 || kind == InstrumentKind::dx10 || kind == InstrumentKind::kepler || kind == InstrumentKind::keplerExo || kind == InstrumentKind::poizone
  5567	                 || kind == InstrumentKind::fruitkick || kind == InstrumentKind::bassdrum || kind == InstrumentKind::toxic
  5568	                 || kind == InstrumentKind::threeosc
  5569	                 || kind == InstrumentKind::autogun || kind == InstrumentKind::transistorbass
  5570	                 || kind == InstrumentKind::simsynth || kind == InstrumentKind::wasp || kind == InstrumentKind::plucked
  5571	                 || kind == InstrumentKind::beepmap || kind == InstrumentKind::sawer || kind == InstrumentKind::flkeys
  5572	                 || kind == InstrumentKind::granulizer || kind == InstrumentKind::drumaxx
  5573	                 || kind == InstrumentKind::slayer || kind == InstrumentKind::fruitydance || kind == InstrumentKind::sytrus)
  5574	        return std::make_unique<ModelPanel> (engine, ChannelRef::rack (channel));
  5575	
  5576	    return std::make_unique<SettingsPanel> (engine, ChannelRef::rack (channel));
  5577	}
  5578	
  5579	bool hasNodeEditor (const ChannelDef& channel) noexcept
  5580	{
  5581	    return channel.instrument == InstrumentKind::synth || channel.instrument == InstrumentKind::sampler || isModelKind (channel.instrument);
  5582	}
  5583	
  5584	std::unique_ptr<juce::Component> makeNodeChannelFrame (AudioEngine& engine, std::function<ChannelDef* (Song&)> locate)
  5585	{
  5586	    ChannelRef ref { std::move (locate), -1 };
  5587	    const auto* def = ref.get (engine);
  5588	    if (def == nullptr)
  5589	        return nullptr;
  5590	    auto page = makeInstrumentPage (engine, ref);
  5591	    if (page == nullptr)
  5592	        return nullptr;
  5593	    const auto kind = def->instrument;
  5594	    return std::make_unique<ChannelFrame> (engine, ref, std::move (page), hasEnvelopeTab (kind) ? std::make_unique<ModulationPanel> (engine, ref) : nullptr);
  5595	}
  5596	std::function<void (int)>& openMixerInsertHandler()

  5600	}
